# Implementation Plan: excalidraw-sketch-engine

**Feature**: A cross-platform scene engine (Excalidraw-baseline diagramming with Clean and Sketchy rendering, layers/groups/frames, and a FreeCAD-style 2D constraint solver) that extends the existing sketch layer without breaking v1 sheets, fusion or exports.
**Date**: 2026-11-14
**Status**: Repaired after the architecture and adversarial reviews (both were BLOCKED; their blockers are addressed below). Phase 0 spikes may start now (Spike 0.1.2 only after OD-3). Phase 4 repair applied (consistency blockers B1-B4, pre-mortem P1 #1-#3 and the validation gaps; see `consistency-review.md`, `pre-mortem.md`, `validation.md`); the repaired plan has NOT been re-reviewed. Not yet "ready for implementation" end to end: Phase 1 conversion (Story 1.4.2) waits on OD-1, solver production code (4.1.2+) on OD-3 and Spike 0.1.2, fonts (2.1.2b) on OD-4, new-sheet default (2.2.1c) on OD-5, importer placeholders (5.1.2) on OD-6, elbow arrows on OD-7, Point/Arc elements (Story 3.2.4 and the arc/point parts of 1.2.1, 2.1.1, 4.1.2b, 4.2.1b, 5.1.2) on OD-8. Phase 4 triad-review repair iteration 1 (2026-11-14) then applied the engineering blockers E1-E3 and gaps, the UX blockers U1-U4 and gaps, and the product gaps (frozen tool inventory, numeric tolerances, adoption metric, abort/pivot criteria); that repair is also NOT re-reviewed. **ADRs**: ADR-001 storage authority and sidecar schema (Proposed; gated on OD-1 and Stories 0.1.1/1.1.1); ADR-002 coordinate model (Accepted); ADR-003 solver build and clean-room boundary (Proposed; gated on OD-3 and Spike 0.1.2); ADR-004 renderer Clean/Sketchy (Proposed; default style depends on OD-5); ADR-005 v1/v2 coexistence and flag (Proposed; depends on OD-1); ADR-006 layer model (Accepted); ADR-007 no new dependencies / third-party ports (Accepted; each port is still blocked until its license re-check in Spike 0.1.5 passes) (all in `project_plans/excalidraw-sketch-engine/decisions/`)

**Path shorthand** (used in every task; no other abbreviation):
- `CM` = `kmp/src/commonMain/kotlin/dev/stapler/stelekit`
- `CT` = `kmp/src/commonTest/kotlin/dev/stapler/stelekit`
- `BT` = `kmp/src/businessTest/kotlin/dev/stapler/stelekit`
- `JT` = `kmp/src/jvmTest/kotlin/dev/stapler/stelekit`
- `AT` = `kmp/src/androidUnitTest/kotlin/dev/stapler/stelekit`
- `V2` = `CM/sketch/v2` PURE ENGINE (scene, commands, tools, bridge, hit, index, render, text, binding, snap, codec, export draw-list/SVG): imports nothing from `ui`, `db`, `repository`, `projectbridge`, `merge`, `platform` and owns no coroutine scope
- `RT` = `CM/sketch/v2/runtime` RUNTIME ADAPTERS (SheetSaver, SheetConverter, SolveRunner, ImageAssetStore, SketchLog, projection source, session/lease): may import `projectbridge`, `repository`, `db`, platform seams; owns scopes
- `UI2` = `CM/ui/sketch/v2`, `SOLV` = `CM/sketch/constraints`, `XCAL` = `CM/sketch/excalidraw`
- Scale convention (ADR-002 addendum, Story 1.2.4): 1 Excalidraw px = 1 scene unit = 1/16 inch on import and export (no conversion factor), so export(import(x)) is exact; hit tolerances are screen px divided by zoom.

**Standing definition of done for every task that adds a test class** (CLAUDE.md): register in `AllBusinessTests` / `AllJvmTests` / `AllAndroidUnitTests` as applicable; commonTest files also get a `kmp/src/commonTest/kotlin/BUILD.bazel` entry; a `jvmTest` file importing `androidx.compose.ui.test` carries `// jvmTest-reason: <pixels|desktop-only>`; no `Thread.sleep`, `GlobalScope`, uncancelled `Dispatchers.IO` scope or real-clock `delay`; any new seam implementation is constructed in a composition root and gets a `wiring-catalog.txt` line (or it fails `WiringCompletenessTest`; a wasmJs-only implementation has no catalog line, D-141); long-lived scopes own their `CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Default)` plus a `CoroutineExceptionHandler`, never a `rememberCoroutineScope()`; domain errors are `Either<…Error, T>`; logging through `Logger`, never `android.util.Log`, never user text.

---

## Step 0.5 - Alternatives considered (CREATIVE pass)

| Approach | Strength | Weakness | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| A. New `v2` element/scene engine in commonMain beside v1, with a lossless lift and a partial projection (chosen) | Carries text, curves, bindings, constraints; v1 pipeline, fixtures and fusion untouched; independently testable once in commonTest | Two editors and two renderers during the transition; adapter surface to maintain | Chosen |
| B. Retype `SketchPrimitive` in place (add kinds, Double coords) | One model, no adapter | Breaks Bareiss/fusion exactness, `SketchCsvWriter`/`SketchSvgWriter`, golden fixtures and sidecar schema:1 readers in one blast radius | Rejected |
| C. Embed Excalidraw in a WebView, bridge scene to Kotlin | Fidelity for free | No constraints, no commonMain access, young WebView wrappers, offline bundle per platform, violates "engine in commonMain" | Rejected (ADR-007) |

## Step 1 - System type
Local-first, multi-target (JVM/Android/wasmJs; iOS compile-only) Compose editor over a file-authoritative persistence layer (sidecar JSON + SQLDelight cache), with an interactive numerical solver and an untrusted-file importer. Complexity 4.

---

## Domain Glossary

| Term | Definition | Notes |
|------|-----------|-------|
| `SceneUnit` | Double coordinate unit; 1 unit = 1/16 inch (ADR-002) | Value class over `Double`, finite-only |
| `ElementId` | Stable string id of a scene element | Newtype; remapped on paste/import |
| `SceneElement` | Sealed hierarchy: `RectElement`, `DiamondElement`, `EllipseElement`, `LineElement`, `ArrowElement`, `FreedrawElement`, `TextElement`, `ImageElement`, `FrameElement`, plus technical geometry `PointElement` and `ArcElement` (OD-8; constraint targets) | Exhaustive `when`; `extras` JSON preserved. A circle is an `EllipseElement` with `rx == ry` (`isCircle`); only circles accept Radius/Diameter/Tangent |
| `ElementBase` | Shared fields: id, layerId, groupIds, frameId, zKey, locked, opacity, style, seed, version, `ordinal` (per-kind auto number), optional user `name`, extras | `version` bumps per edit, drives caches; `ordinal` is assigned once and never reused (Story 3.5.7) |
| `ZKey` | Fractional order string within a layer | Newtype; collisions fall back to array order on import |
| `ElementStyle` | stroke, fill, strokeWidth, strokeStyle, roughness, roundness, fillStyle | Immutable data class |
| `SketchSceneV2` | Immutable scene: `Map<ElementId, SceneElement>` + order + layers + constraints | Structural sharing; max 5,000 elements |
| `Layer` / `LayerId` | Named paint-order bucket with visible/locked/opacity/order (ADR-006) | Default layer always exists |
| `GroupId`, `FrameId` | Ids for nested groups and clipping frames | `FrameId` = the frame element's `ElementId` |
| `Binding` | Arrow endpoint to shape link: `{elementId, fixedPoint, mode}`; legacy `{focus, gap}` accepted on import | Mode `Inside`/`Orbit`/`Skip` |
| `SketchDocumentV2` | Scene + sheet settings + view; the unit that history snapshots | |
| `Command` | `apply(doc) -> Either<CommandError, Applied(doc, changedIds)>` | Evolution of v1 function-per-command |
| `Transaction` | Group of commands committed as one history step | Includes solver output |
| `HistoryV2` | Bounded (100) snapshot stack of `SketchDocumentV2` | Evicts whole entries only |
| `Tool` | Pure state machine `onEvent(InputEvent, ToolContext) -> ToolResult` | Compose-free |
| `InputEvent` | `(type, point, pressure?, pointerKind, pointerId, timestamp, modifiers)` | Normalised in the adapter |
| `PointerKind` | Touch / Mouse / Stylus / Eraser | Splits navigate vs act |
| `DrawOp` | One render instruction in scene space (path, text run, image ref, clip, style) | Goldened in commonTest |
| `StrokeStyleProvider` | `Clean` or `Sketchy` geometry strategy (ADR-004) | Seeded by `ElementBase.seed` |
| `TextMetricsProvider` | Seam that measures text; fixed-advance fake in tests | Persisted width/height and wrap points are AUTHORITATIVE for layout on every target; re-measure only on edit |
| `SpatialIndex` | AABB R-tree or grid over element bounds | Property-tested against brute force |
| `ConstraintProblem` / `SolveResult` | Flat parameter vector + constraints / `Solved`, `Conflict(ids)`, `Redundant(ids)`, `NoConvergence` | Solver knows no elements |
| `GeometricConstraint` | Coincident, PointOnObject, Horizontal, Vertical, Parallel, Perpendicular, Tangent, Equal, Symmetric, Fixed | |
| `DimensionalConstraint` | Distance(+X/Y), Angle, Radius, Diameter; `isDriving` false = reference | |
| `ConstraintStatus` | Dof count, FullyConstrained / UnderConstrained / OverConstrained, ids | Shown with glyph + text, not colour alone |
| `ProjectionResult` | `Projected(SketchPrimitive)` or `Dropped(reason)` from `V2ToV1.project` | Feeds fusion only |
| `SheetCodec` | Per-`schema` sidecar sheet reader/writer | v1 and v2 implementations |
| `SheetLease` | One-owner lock on an open sheet, held by a `SheetLeaseRegistry` in `RT/session` (UI-free; the legacy and v2 editors, preview and importer all go through it) | Stops dual editors |
| `SheetPayload` | Sealed per-sheet content in `ProjectBundle`: `V1(primitives)` or `V2Opaque(bytes, sha256)` | v2 bytes pass through the bundle pipeline untouched |
| `TechnicalData` | Zones, named dimensions, sum/equal constraints, layer-name mirror, sheet scale; stored in `project-model.json` (`sheetRef`, `dimensions[]`), NOT in the sheet file | Unchanged by v2; see Story 1.1.4 |
| `ConversionState` | Per sheet: `NotConverted`, `Declined`, `Failed(reason)`, `Converted`. A sheet changes state only through the explicit Upgrade action (`UpgradeOffer`), never by being opened | Router input (UX U2, UX-104) |
| `SketchEditorRouter` | Chooses v1 or v2 editor at open time from the flag and `schema` | |
| `SketchRenderDispatcher` | Single function choosing preview/embed renderer by `schema` | |
| `ImportReport` | Counts of imported/skipped elements with reason codes | Never contains user text |
| `ExcalidrawScene` | Parsed `.excalidraw` (tolerant, all-optional) before mapping | Untrusted |
| `ElementLibrary` | Local reusable shape sets, `.excalidrawlib` compatible | |
| `ToolStrip` | Adaptive tool UI (bottom dock compact, rail expanded) | Tool lock supported |
| `SketchDiagnostics` | Typed list of solver/import/save messages shown via live region | Replaces `error: String?` for v2 |
| `Dropped(reason)` | Typed reason an element is excluded from the v1 projection | |
| `SheetPaths` | The one rule mapping (sheet uuid, schema) to the sidecar file path: 1 -> `sheets/<uuid>.json`, 2 -> `sheets/<uuid>.v2.json` | Owned by Story 1.1.3 |
| `SidecarSlugLock` | Per-slug mutex held across read-plan-write by every sidecar writer (reconciler, saver, converter, restore) | Owned by Story 1.1.3 |
| `ConversionReady` | Build-time constant; true only while the bundle (1.1.3), technical-data (1.1.4), codec (1.1.5), fusion/preview (1.4.5) and router test classes are registered. It gates BOTH v2 modes (a brand-new v2 sheet must reach fusion, digest and previews too) | Checked by `ConversionReadyConsistencyTest` (list in Story 1.4.3); the converter refuses when false |
| `V2Mode` | The per-graph setting `sketch.engine.v2`, stored as a string beside the per-graph accessors of `ProjectBridgeSettings` and read with `getString` only (missing, blank or unknown reads as `Off`; the key never shipped, so nothing is migrated): `Off` (default) / `NewProjectsOnly` (new sketches are v2 only in a `NewSketchProject` (canonical sentence in requirements.md "Flag modes"); no Upgrade chip, nothing converted) / `On` (also the Upgrade offer and conversion). The effective mode is the stored mode capped by what the build allows (`ConversionReady`, `ConversionOffered`) | Requirements "Flag modes"; Tasks 1.4.3a, 1.4.3d; ADR-005 item 1a |
| `ConversionOffered` | Build-time release decision, default true, false in a release shipped after an A4 NO-GO; gates only conversion, the `On` mode and the Upgrade chip, never the read path or `NewProjectsOnly` | Task 1.4.3e; ADR-005 index |
| `SketchBackup` | `<slug>/backup/<uuid>.v1.json` taken before first v2 write | Outside `sheets/`, never scanned as a sheet (ADR-001, Task 0.1.1b) |
| `SceneLimits` | The ONLY home of numeric caps; ONE canonical field list of 32 fields, defined in Story 1.2.4 and referenced everywhere by name (elements, constraints, coordinates, points, JSON depth, sheet/import/import-span/image/decoded-image bytes, image side and pixels, parse budget, text and name chars, library items and library bytes, layers, soft-banner thresholds, PNG side, history, semantics nodes, diagnostics entries, notices, go-to results, conflict copies, zoom range) | Story 1.2.4 (built FIRST in Epic 1.2); a test greps that no other numeric cap exists |
| `SketchErrorState` | One enum of every user-visible error state; drives the UI action set AND exactly one `sketch.*` log line | `RT/telemetry`, Story 6.2.7 (UX-71, UX-75) |
| `ConflictCopy` | `<slug>/conflicts/<uuid>.v2.conflict-<hash8>.json`: the in-memory scene saved by `writeSheetConflictCopy` when `SheetSaver`'s `baseSha` check finds the file changed underneath it. Distinct from the existing reconciler artefact `<slug>/project-model.conflict-<hash8>.json` written by `ProjectSidecarStore.writeConflict` (project MODEL, slug root, unchanged by this plan) | Outside `sheets/`, never scanned as a sheet or hashed (Story 1.4.4, Task 0.1.1b) |

| `ConstraintEligibility` | Pure rule table: constraints apply only to technical geometry (line, rect, polyline, circle (an ellipse with equal radii), arc, point), never to text, arrow, image, freedraw or frame; also yields the disabled-reason string per (element kind, constraint) | Task 4.2.1d; used by the flyout, auto-constrain and the importer |
| `FingerDrawing` | Per-device setting: when ON a touch pointer acts like a stylus and draws; the ONE user-facing name is "Finger drawing" (never "Pen mode") | Story 3.1.2 |
| `SketchNoticeQueue` | The single priority queue for transient notices, toasts and live-region announcements | Story 3.6.7 |
| `SingleKeyShortcuts` | Setting that enables the single-letter tool shortcuts (WCAG 2.1.4): `On`/`Off`; active only while the canvas has focus; defaults desktop On, Android On only with a hardware keyboard and no touch exploration, web Off (UX-108) | Story 3.6.1 |
| `RetentionPolicy` | Rules for growth, pruning, git sync and ignore of `backup/` and `conflicts/` | Task 0.1.1c |
| `ConstraintTarget` / `TargetPart` | A constraint operand: `(elementId, part)` with part Whole, Start, End, Vertex(i), Centre, Edge(i), ArcStart or ArcEnd; the part is what the user picks when targeting an endpoint, vertex, centre or edge | Story 4.2.6; stored in the constraint fields of `SheetCodecV2` (default Whole) |
| `ElementName` | The user-visible name of an element: kind plus per-kind monotonic ordinal ("Line 3", "Circle 1") unless the user renamed it | Story 3.5.7; used by messages, semantics, the Elements list and Go to element |
| `SketchConsentCopy` | The ONE source of the old-version/whole-project consent wording used by the flag-enable confirmation, the Upgrade chip's Details sheet and consequence line, the first-write dialog (which names the project) and the post-conversion banner. The acknowledgement behind the first-write dialog is per sketch PROJECT (device, project slug; not synced) because that is the unit older builds fail on | Story 1.4.6 (UX-103, D-16); a test fails if a consent literal exists elsewhere |
| `UpgradeOffer` | The non-modal `UpgradeChip` banner row ("Upgrade blocks other devices." [Start]; Details, Not now and Keep this sketch classic under More) shown in the classic editor for a `NotConverted` sheet when the flag is on; Details, the consent sheet (where the line does not fit) and the first-write dialog carry the full whole-project sentence (D-41); the only way a v1 sheet is converted | Story 1.4.6, Task 1.4.6g (UX-104, D-16) |
| `AdoptionGate` | The named owner go/no-go (UNMEASURED / PIVOT / ADJUST / PROCEED, in that precedence) at the rendered slice that decides whether the gated UI stories and the flag release proceed | Task 6.2.4e; criteria in requirements.md |
| `OnboardingSequencer` | The rule that at most ONE onboarding item (finger-drawing hint, Upgrade chip, first-run coach mark, single-key chip, empty state) is visible at a time, in the rank order of the ux.md banner table (the one place the order is defined), each following its own row of the `OnboardingPolicy` recurrence table (once, twice-or-acted, until-acted, while-empty); a priority class of `SketchNoticeQueue` | Task 3.6.7c (UX-116, UX-122, D-19) |
| `DimensionSuggestion` | The smart-dimension aid: with one line, one circle, or a line plus a circle selected, a chip offers the natural dimension (Length, Radius, centre-to-edge Distance) with the current value prefilled; accepting it opens the label editor | Tasks 4.2.7c, 4.2.7d (UX-115, D-21) |
| `FeatureBaseline` | The counted checklist of 41 Excalidraw features (15 style-panel properties, 13 arrange and edit actions, 8 align/distribute actions, 5 library operations) pinned to a named Excalidraw release; every id maps to a command or control; enforced by `FeatureBaselineCompletenessTest` | requirements.md metric 1; Task 3.5.4c |
| `QualitySignals` | Local, never-transmitted counters (v2 sessions, crashes, solves, `NoConvergence` solves, save conflicts) plus the owner's data-loss log, read against the flag-exit thresholds of requirements.md | Task 6.2.3b (D-26) |
| `TouchTargetFloor` | The ONE interactive-target size rule: 44 dp whenever a touch or pen pointer is present (touch-primary form factor or a touch/pen event observed), 24 dp only on mouse-only devices (WCAG 2.5.8 minimum); visible glyphs may stay smaller than their hit area | UX-46, UX-92 (D-17); audited by Task 3.6.6a |
| `Baselines` | Measured reference figures (owner's excalidraw.com authoring time, empty-canvas and 500-rect reference frame p95 on JVM and Pixel_Fold) recorded before the bars that cite them | Story 0.1.7 |
| `V2StorageAvailable` | The platform capability: `store != null && store.isAvailable()`; true on desktop, Android and a browser whose `V2StorageStatus` is `Ready` (an Available `OpfsStorageVerdict` and a completed preload), false on iOS, in a browser whose status is `Unavailable`, while the status is `Checking` (a pending state, never the no-editor screen) and when the sidecar store is null (a demo graph, or no graph open); with it false every stored mode behaves as `Off` | Story 1.4.3 (Tasks 1.4.3d, 1.4.3h); D-104, D-116 |
| `OpfsStorageVerdict` | The pure per-browser decision `Available(Move)`, `Available(CopyVerify)` or `Unavailable(reason)` (reasons `NotProbed`, `NoOpfs`, `NoCreateWritable`, `NoAtomicReplace`, `NoSliceRead`) over five probe facts (OPFS root, `createWritable`, move verified, copy-verify verified, slice read); persistence is NOT a fact of it (a denied `persist()` is a warning, `PersistenceState`); decided at startup and by Spike 0.1.8 per browser | Story 1.4.7; Story 0.1.8 (D-112, D-125) |
| `V2StorageStatus` | The pure availability state the UI reads: `Checking` (verdict not probed or preload not finished, at most 10 s), `Ready(sessionOnly, privateSuspected)` (an Available verdict AND `preloadState` `Ok`) or `Unavailable(reason)` (a verdict reason, `PreloadFailed`, `PreloadTooLarge` or `CheckTimedOut`); `V2StorageAvailable` is true only for `Ready`; the types are in Story 1.4.3 so that no non-parked task depends on Story 1.4.7 | Story 1.4.3 (Task 1.4.3k), Story 1.4.7 (Tasks 1.4.7a, 1.4.7j); D-124, D-128, D-129 |
| `OpfsWriteQueue` | The wasm per-path ordered queue of every OPFS operation: an operation starts after the tails of all its paths settle and becomes their tail; `awaitDurable(path)` awaits a path's tail and returns the typed failure of any operation of it | Story 1.4.7 (Task 1.4.7f); D-119, D-120 |
| `SketchWriterLease` | The per-graph single-writer lease for sheet writes: always held on desktop and Android, a releasable `WebLock.tryAcquireHeld("stelekit-sketch-writer:<graphId>")` on wasm (taken when the first v2 sheet opens for editing, never at startup, released when the graph stops being the ready graph, defence in depth because a persistent web graph never switches, D-142); a second tab is `HeldElsewhere`, read-only, and reloads the sidecar root when it takes over; consumed by the router (Task 1.4.3n), the store (Task 1.4.3o) and the saver (Task 1.4.3r) | Story 1.4.3 (the port, Task 1.4.3m), Story 1.4.7 (`WasmSketchWriterLease`, Task 1.4.7i); D-123 |
| `SolverTier` | The per-target result of Spike 0.1.2: `Full`, `GeometricOnly` or `Hidden`; a target-independent miss selects `GeometricOnly` everywhere, a time miss only on its own target | Story 0.1.2, Story 4.1.3 (D-114) |

(70 terms.)

---

## Pattern Decisions

| Component | Pattern Chosen | Source | Alternative Rejected | Reason |
|-----------|---------------|--------|---------------------|--------|
| Scene | Immutable Domain Model with structural sharing | PoEAA Domain Model | Mutable scene graph | Snapshot undo and cache invalidation by `changedIds` |
| Edits | Command with `changedIds` + Transaction | GoF Command / PoEAA Unit of Work | Intent + re-solve on redo | Redo must replay patches verbatim (pitfalls S4, undo) |
| Tools | State machine per tool | GoF State | One big gesture `when` | Testable in commonTest with event scripts |
| Renderer | Strategy (`Clean`/`Sketchy`) over one draw list | GoF Strategy | Renderer per output format | Structural SVG/PNG/screen parity |
| Text/Fonts | Adapter (`TextMetricsProvider`) | GoF Adapter | Direct `TextMeasurer` calls | Cross-target determinism, test fake |
| Persistence | Repository over cache + file-authoritative sidecar via `SheetCodec` | PoEAA Repository / Strategy | Widen `sketch_primitives` | v2 needs own tables; ADR-001 |
| v1/v2 bridge | Anti-corruption layer (`V1ToV2`, `V2ToV1`) | DDD ACL | Shared model | ADR-002 |
| Editor choice | Router + lease | Fowler Registry-lite | Flag read inside editors | Flag never controls readability (ADR-005) |
| Solver | Ports-and-adapters (pure contract) | Hexagonal | Solver inside scene | Import-direction test; swappable algorithm |
| Importer | Parse, don't validate (`ExcalidrawScene` then mapper) | type-driven-design | Decode straight into scene | Untrusted input; typed rejects |
| Ids and numbers | Newtypes `ElementId`, `ZKey`, `SceneUnit`, `GroupId` | type-driven-design | Raw String/Double | Cross-entity confusion, non-finite values |
| States | Sealed: `SceneElement`, `SolveResult`, `ProjectionResult`, `CommandError`, `ToolResult` | type-driven-design | Strings/flags | Exhaustive handling |
| Spatial query | Index with brute-force oracle | - | Linear scan only | Snap/hit/cull at 5,000 elements; flat-scan benchmarked first (Spike 0.1.6) |

| `CM/projectbridge/SidecarFileState.kt` (`SUPPORTED_SCHEMA = 1L`, shared by model and sheets) | One constant gates both file kinds; a `schema:2` sheet makes the whole project `Corrupt(NewerSchema)` | Refactor-first | Story 1.1.1 splits it per file kind and adds `SheetCodec` dispatch + the old-reader golden BEFORE any v2 write |

| `CM/projectbridge/ProjectSidecarStore.kt` `writeLocked` (L107-123 at `54f059f351`: writes sheets then model, deletes unlisted `sheets/*.json` in the sweep at L119-121, creates only slug dir and `sheets/`) | No `backup/` dir; staging/unreferenced sheet files are not defined | Refactor-first | Task 0.1.1b changes the store (creates `backup/` and `conflicts/`, excludes them from `Present(files)` and `bundleHash`, reads the model first and only the sheets it references, limits the unreferenced-sheet delete sweep to files that classify as schema 1; a schema-2 file is deleted only by the converter's cleanup or restore, never by the generic sweep); Story 1.1.3 adds the `opaque`/`expectedSha` contract (checked at write AND at `flushPending` time) and `SidecarSlugLock`; Story 1.4.2 adds `writeNow` |

| `CM/ui/sketch/SketchViewModel.kt` (348), `SketchCanvas.kt` (212), `SketchCanvasDraw.kt` | Large, v1-specific, `ZONE_SELECT` naming | Isolate via seam | `SketchEditorRouter` + separate v2 view model; extract only `PlacementController`/`SketchStore` |
| `CM/sketch/SketchCommands.kt` (223, closed `CommandError`) | Function-per-command, closed error set | Extend as-is (v1); v2 has its own `Command`/error | v1 untouched; status formatting shared via a tiny adapter |
| `CM/sketch/SketchScene.kt` linear `candidatesIn`, `SketchHistory.kt` | No index; snapshot history | Extend as-is | v2 has `SpatialIndex`; `HistoryV2` copied with tests, generalise only on a third consumer |
| `CM/repository/SqlDelightSketchRepositories.kt` (246) + `.sq` sketch block (lines ~1413-1516) | Primitive-shaped tables | Extend as-is | Additive tables/queries only; `sketch_primitives` untouched |

| `CM/ui/sketch/LayerPanel.kt` (checkbox + detached labels) | A11y and feature gaps | Extend as-is (v1) | v2 gets `UI2/LayersPanel`; v1 panel left for the legacy editor |

## Migration Plan
- **Migration files**: `CM/db/MigrationRunner.kt` (one new hash-guarded step `sketch_sheets_schema_version`), `kmp/src/commonMain/sqldelight/.../SteleDatabase.sq`, regenerated `kmp/src/generated/sqldelight/` (committed). ONE change: `ALTER TABLE sketch_sheets ADD COLUMN schema_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1` (the existing ALTER pattern in `MigrationRunner.all` is read first in Task 1.4.1a). **No `sketch_elements` / `sketch_constraints` tables** (dropped, see Story 1.4.1 decision): the sidecar file is authoritative, the scene loads whole into memory (max 5,000 elements, 200 constraints) with an in-memory index, and no search, fusion, thumbnail or cold-start reader needs per-element rows; every autosave would otherwise rewrite up to 5,000 rows through `DatabaseWriteActor` for no reader. `schema_version` is the DB mirror read by `SketchRenderDispatcher` and the reconciler to avoid parsing JSON per embed; when it disagrees with the file, the file wins (ADR-001) and the reconciler rewrites the mirror. The mirror is carried by `insertSketchSheet`, its `RestrictedDatabaseQueries` stub and both `replaceProjectContents` (Story 1.4.1), so no `INSERT OR REPLACE` can reset it to 1; the router and the legacy `SketchStore` refusal trust the FILE's schema, not the mirror, whenever the two may disagree (Stories 1.4.1, 1.4.3).
- **Reversibility**: additive and forward-only; v1 tables and rows are never altered or deleted (a converted sheet's old `sketch_primitives` rows are left in place and ignored, because consumers dispatch on `schema_version`). Rollback = flag off (v2 sheets stay readable, ADR-005).
- **Zero-downtime**: the column is added by `MigrationRunner.applyAll()` on existing DBs; sheet conversion is per sheet and EXPLICIT (the Upgrade action of Story 1.4.6, never merely opening the sheet), using the crash-safe protocol in Story 1.4.2: backup first, v2 sheet to a DISTINCT path (`sheets/<uuid>.v2.json`), then ONE model write that flips the sheet reference, then old v1 file cleanup. Re-run is idempotent (same ids).
- **Rollback procedure**: set the flag off; to restore v1 content, run the backup-restore operation of Story 6.2.6 (copies `backup/<uuid>.v1.json` to `sheets/<uuid>.json`, removes `sheets/<uuid>.v2.json`, rewrites the model reference and `fileSha256` in one model write); a settings-screen action plus a tested function, not a manual file copy.
- **Gates**: `MigrationRunnerSchemaSyncTest` green; a test opens a real v1-era DB fixture and asserts the column exists with value 1 on existing rows; every new write query has a `RestrictedDatabaseQueries` `@DirectSqlWrite` stub; no `getAll*` read exists.

## Observability Plan
- **Logs** (`Logger`, ids only, never element text): `sketch.tool.activated(tool)`; `sketch.solve.failed(kind, constraintIds, reason)`; `sketch.import.failed(reasonCode)`, `sketch.import.summary(imported, skipped)`; `sketch.migrate.outcome(sheetId, result)`; `sketch.persist.failed(reason)`; `sketch.sheet.rejected(reasonCode)` (native decode caps); `sketch.save.conflict(sheetId)` (Story 1.4.4); `sketch.adoption(authored, imported)` and `sketch.quality(v2Sessions, crashes, solves, noConvergence, conflicts)` (counts only, local, Task 6.2.3b, not error states; they feed the flag-exit criteria of requirements.md). Every user-visible error state maps to exactly one line through `SketchErrorState` (Story 6.2.7). A test asserts no log line contains element text and that 1,000 pointer moves emit none.
- **Metrics** (into `benchmarks/budgets/`, new file `sketch-engine-budgets.json`; currently only `annotation-resource-budgets.json` exists there): `sketch.frame_ms_500_elements` (relative to empty canvas), `sketch.solve_ms_100_constraints`, `sketch.hit_test_candidates_5000`, `sketch.import_ms_5000_elements`, `sketch.draw_list_build_ms_5000`, `sketch.native_decode_ms_5000`, `sketch.encode_ms_5000` and `sketch.save_bytes_5000` (encode time and written bytes for a 5,000-element sheet; CI gate, Story 6.2.2). Rule for frame time: ONE relative rule, p95 frame time with 500 elements <= 1.5x the empty-canvas p95 measured in the same run on the same host. This runs in CI on the JVM harness only. The empty-canvas and 500-rectangle reference figures the rule is compared against are MEASURED by Task 0.1.7b (`reference.*` entries in the budgets file), so the budget is checked against a measured prototype, not an assumption. The Pixel_Fold AVD number is an owner-run advisory gate (Story 6.2.4c, OD-2), not CI: a headless software-rendered AVD is not a reliable frame-time source.
- **Alerts**: none new (local-first app); regressions are caught by the budget gate in CI. Android crashes already persisted by `AndroidCrashRecorder`/`PreviousSessionLog`.

## Risk Control
- **Feature flag**: `sketch.engine.v2` per graph, a three-value mode `V2Mode` (`Off` default, `NewProjectsOnly`, `On`; ADR-005 item 1a; requirements "Flag modes"); gates editor choice and new-sheet kind only. No mode other than `Off` can be turned on (shown disabled with the reason) until Stories 1.1.3, 1.1.4, 1.1.5 and 1.4.5 are green (`ConversionReady`, enforced by `ConversionReadyConsistencyTest`, whose list includes the 1.1.5 codec and technical-data test classes, not by a hand-set constant alone), so a v2 sheet can never reach fusion without projection wiring; `On` and conversion additionally need `ConversionOffered` (false after an A4 NO-GO), so a NO-GO release still creates v2 sheets in new sketch projects without any path that converts an existing one.
- **Rollback procedure**: flag off; v2 sheets render read-only; no data conversion required. Standard PR revert for code. Content restore uses Story 6.2.6.
- **Staged rollout**: dark in this order: (1) model + codec + bundle pipeline + fusion projection wiring + persistence (conversion unreachable until 1.1.3, 1.1.4, 1.1.5, 1.4.5 are green), (2) renderer + tools, (3) constraints, (4) interop. First v2 write on a graph shows a one-time confirmation naming the old-build consequence (OD-1; own UI Story 1.4.6). **Flag-exit and retirement criteria**: the default of `sketch.engine.v2` flips and the classic editor may be retired only against the thresholds in requirements.md "Flag and legacy-editor exit criteria" (Task 6.2.5d), read from `QualitySignals`. **Reader-only release ordering (OD-1, pre-mortem P2 #4)**: the reader side (Stories 0.1.1, 1.1.1, 1.4.1, and the read-only paths of 1.1.3/1.4.5/6.1.1) ships in a release BEFORE any release in which conversion or new-v2-sheet creation is reachable, so devices that sync from the owner's graph can already read `schema:2` (a lagging Android release or cached wasm build is the case to avoid). The first-write confirmation lists this; the flag stays hidden until that release is the minimum seen.

Owner: Tyler.

  - D-21 Smart dimension (Story 4.2.7, UX-115): suggestion chip default ON; bracket-plate bound 42 actions by the Constrain menu and 34 with the chips, counted by ONE convention (a typed value plus its Enter is one action; the Line tool is held with tool lock; INFERRED recount of ux.md section 9; the test asserts both). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-22 Excalidraw baseline pin (requirements.md, Tasks 0.1.5d and 3.5.4c): Excalidraw 0.18.x (0.18.1, April 2026 per research/build-vs-buy.md, SEARCH-level) until Task 0.1.5d records the exact tag and date; 41-item counted checklist (15 style panel, 13 arrange and edit, 8 align/distribute, 5 library); `link` and cardinality arrowheads stay out. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-23 Metric 5 bar (requirements.md): scenario "floor plan layers"; 7 operations each in at most 3 actions and exactly one undo step, 100% pass in `UxOrganisationAcceptanceTest`. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-24 Metric 7 real exports (Task 0.1.5d): N = 5 owner-supplied excalidraw.com exports by the end of W1; fewer by W7 is reported as "UNMEASURED against real exports", not as met. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-25 A4 go/no-go and A5 decision rule (requirements.md, Story 6.2.8): A4 GO when every device that syncs the owner's graph can run a reader-capable build before conversion is reachable; NO-GO ships `ConversionOffered = false` (D-59), so conversion stays unreachable and v2 sheets exist only in a `NewSketchProject` (D-68); A5 rule as written in Story 6.2.8. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-26 Flag exit and legacy retirement (requirements.md, Tasks 6.2.3b and 6.2.5d): thresholds on data-loss reports, crash rate and `NoConvergence` rate before the flag default flips (to `On`, or to `NewProjectsOnly` on a row capped there, D-67) and before the classic editor can be retired. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-27 0.1.7c pre-check bar (Story 0.1.7): PASS at least 3 of 5, MARGINAL 2, FAIL 0-1 holds the Epic 2/3 UI stories (the script-checked "Pre-check hold" set, by edges from Task 0.1.7c) until the owner records a choice. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-28 Web accessibility equivalents (Task 3.6.6d, UX-113): 320 CSS px and 400% reflow, WCAG 1.4.12 text spacing, `prefers-reduced-motion` through `MotionPolicy`. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-29 Success feedback (UX-114): export names the file and location, restore names the kept copy, library add and insert announce counts, all through the notice queue. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-30 Solver time bars (requirements NFR, A2, metric 3, Spike 0.1.2, ADR-003): 100 constraints solve under 50 ms on JVM (hard) and under 150 ms on wasmJs (hard); 50 ms on wasmJs is an ADVISORY target recorded, not gating; residual acceptance 1e-6 scene units against a tighter 1e-9 solver stop tolerance. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-31 Import decode (Story 5.1.1, Tasks 5.1.1e and 5.1.1f, E1): the importer cuts the file into JSON spans with a byte-level scanner and never decodes more than `maxImportSpanBytes` = 2 MB in one call (a `SceneLimits` field; ADR-002's limits list names it), depth is enforced in the scanner before decode, only a `files` entry's `dataURL` is a raw byte range (the 2 MB cap applies to the entry's other members), file, drop and clipboard share one `ImportSource` byte-or-text input, and the differential against a whole-file decode is stated per equivalence class; base64 image payloads decoded in 64 KB slices; chosen over a wasm-only smaller `maxImportBytes` so web does not refuse image-bearing files. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-32 Edge-pan safe default (Story 3.1.2, Task 3.1.2g, UX-121): the strip is OFF on every target Task 0.1.4d did not mark Supported (and on Android below API 29, iOS and Safari until device-verified), with Hand tool, two-finger pan and pan arrows always available; where Supported it covers only the protected span (200 dp expected). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-33 Pan arrows (Task 3.6.3d, UX-120): a four-arrow pan pad, quarter-canvas step per tap, collapsed to ONE 44 dp "Pan" button that opens an opaque pad (a plus-shaped grid of five cells, or a row of four where the grid would cover over the table's share of a small canvas; no auto-collapse timer, D-44), default Auto (shown only while Finger drawing is ON and a touch or pen pointer is present; NOT on a mouse- or keyboard-only desktop, and independent of the edge strip), inset clear of an active strip, plus the bottom-sheet handle-as-button. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-34 Chrome budget (Story 3.1.4, UX-119): layout on both axes (landscape phone = rail plus side sheet), the minimum canvas, at most three transient rows (ONE banner slot with a fixed rank order, the hint/status row, the selection bar), the collapse order, and tools flat only when `flatExtent` fits (otherwise grouped as split buttons with a main and a separate flyout target) at every width class; EVERY figure is defined in the ux.md chrome table and computed by `check-plan.py` (single source; no figure is typed elsewhere), tested at every window of the table at font scales 1.0 and 2.0 with every banner candidate pending. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-35 First-write dialog (Task 1.4.6a, UX-64): ONE checkbox "I understand" gating Convert and open; [Open classic], Esc and back write and record nothing, and a NEW sketch in an unacknowledged project shows the new-sheet variant (no backup sentence, buttons [Create classic sketch] and [Create and open], Esc and back cancel and create nothing, so no path creates a classic sheet silently); "Keep this sketch classic" lives only in the Upgrade chip menu. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-36 Consent comprehension (Story 6.2.8 task 2, UX-123): pass bar ceil(0.8 n) correct answers to "what will you see, and about which sketches" (correct = the whole project); a miss is a copy defect and one re-run, a second miss an owner decision. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-37 Regression baseline (Task 0.1.7d, requirements metric 6): the list and count of existing sketch tests and golden fixtures is recorded from `git ls-tree` at a RECORDED commit SHA (written on the first line of the file; an ancestor of `origin/main`, so it survives a squash merge, and CI fetches full history), so the baseline cannot include work done after it, and Task 6.2.5a re-derives it from that same SHA and diffs. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-38 A6 go/no-go (requirements.md "A6 go/no-go", Task 0.1.2d): PROCEED at 1 or more of 3 parts marked in-app, ADJUST (at least 1 of 3 in-app AND at least 1 of the 14 Constrain flyout entries crossed out, or the spike's geometric-only fallback) narrows the exposed constraint kinds, DEFER at 0 of 3 parks (never deletes) the Epic 4.2 UI stories; Job 2 is 11.3% of total cost (3.75M of 33.20M CU, Epics 4.1 and 4.2), but A6 gates only the six constraint-UI items and Task 6.2.4f (1.18M CU, 3.5% of the total): no edge from A6 reaches Epic 4.1, so the solver is built in every outcome. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-39 Move without dragging (Story 3.6.3, UX-40): the crosshair mode gains a "Move element" action (select, nudge with the pad or arrow keys, Apply, one undo step) so a touch-only user never needs to drag an element; arrow-key nudge of a selection stays. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-40 Degrees-of-freedom text (Story 4.2.3, UX-31): the DOF status line is shown only on sheets that have at least one constraint or are technical sheets, never on a plain diagram sheet. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-41 Banner content fit and the Upgrade chip (Stories 3.1.4, 1.4.6; UX-119, UX-126, UX-104): a banner is one row (summary, one primary button, one More button) with a computed text budget; the Upgrade chip therefore carries the consequence line "Upgrade blocks other devices." and a Details sheet, is a consent row that is replaced by a sheet (never by a short title) whenever the wrapped line exceeds the row's 2 lines (computed: from font scale 1.11 at 320 dp wide and from 1.52 at 360 dp wide, D-64), and the full whole-project sentence is shown in the first-write dialog that [Start] always opens before the first conversion in a project (the chip row no longer carries the sentence itself); read-only and the other state banners are dismissible for the session, Blocking banners are not. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-42 Action chip row (Story 3.1.4, UX-126): every touch-required control (modifier chips, [Done], the picker's and solver's [Cancel], the smart-dimension chip, the Crosshair chip) lives in a canvas overlay at the touch floor that no row collapse removes; the hint/status row is text only. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-43 Top-bar Finger chip and fixed rail (Story 3.1.4, Task 3.1.4d owns the chip, Task 3.1.2d the setting; UX-80, UX-102): the Finger drawing state chip is pinned in the top bar (not in the scrolling strip), the rail has one fixed width (split-button targets lie along the strip axis), and the strip is laid out at the table's sizes in every pointer class so the touch-floor switch never reflows. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-44 Small windows and small selections (Stories 3.1.4, 3.2.2, Tasks 3.6.3d, 3.1.4c; UX-120, UX-127, UX-128): the pan pad has no auto-collapse timer and falls back to a row of four arrows where a grid would cover over the table's share of the canvas; toasts stack in a lane of at most the table's share of the canvas; windows below the guarantee (a web page at 400% zoom) use a degraded layout with no minimum claimed; handles are reduced by size tiers so hit areas never overlap, with a Transform menu as the always-available path. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-45 Selectability, Alt and the web chord (Stories 3.2.2, 3.6.1, 3.6.8; UX-12, UX-109, UX-111): the canvas never selects locked or hidden-layer elements, the Elements list may select a locked one (never a hidden one); Alt is an optional accelerator on Windows and macOS only, native or web, never on Linux (D-53), and no function is Alt-only; the Constrain chord is Ctrl+K on the web (Ctrl+Shift+K opens the Firefox console) and Ctrl+Shift+K elsewhere; one counting convention for "steps" (a typed string with its confirming key is one step), so UX-01 stays at 4. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-46 Product rules (requirements.md metrics 8, A1 and A6): metric 8 needs at least 5 new diagrams in the read window (below that the result is "insufficient data", not a pass or a fail, and the window extends up to 26 weeks); Task 0.1.7c also records the owner's current diagram frequency; an A6 DEFER removes "Job 2 first" from the A1 PIVOT options (an owner who still chooses it supersedes A6 in the ADR); Story 4.2.4 is NOT gated by A1 or A6. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-47 Import bytes (Stories 5.1.1, 3.5.6; E1): `.excalidrawlib` import runs the same scanner in library mode, one span per library item (`maxImportSpanBytes`), with a stored-library byte cap `maxLibraryBytes` (31st `SceneLimits` field); clipboard text is size-checked again after UTF-8 encoding and must start with `{` or a BOM to be scanned at all; a `files` entry whose `dataURL` contains an escape other than `\/` becomes an image placeholder with reason `DataUrlEscaped`. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-48 Product exit and gate rules (requirements.md metric 8, "Flag and legacy-editor exit criteria", "Combined outcomes: A1 x A4 x A6", A5; Tasks 6.2.3b, 6.2.4e, 6.2.5d, Story 6.2.8): metric 8 counts only AUTHORED v2 sheets (created in-app, at least 3 drawn elements; imports and conversions are reported separately) and the bar is ceil(0.5 n) of n at least 5; the crash and `NoConvergence` thresholds are stated as integer counts (crash flip: n at least 50 sessions and at most floor(n/100) crashes; stop-ship: 2 or more in the last 20 sessions; `NoConvergence` flip: n at least 200 solves and at most floor(n/50); stop-ship: 11 or more in the last 100); the window runs from the ONE window start of metric 8 and is capped at 26 weeks, the `NoConvergence` row is not applicable while the constraint UI is parked, and an unmet minimum n is "insufficient data" with the mode staying `Off` by default (`NewProjectsOnly` only if the owner records it); a second A1 ADJUST is an owner decision (PROCEED with named defects, or PIVOT); the A1 x A4 x A6 table of requirements.md (which also carries the pre-check FAIL rows, D-66) is the one statement of the combinations; under `NewProjectsOnly` a new diagram in an existing project is classic and is not counted by metric 8 (the denominator still includes it, requirements.md metric 8); A5 participants are the owner plus 2 to 4 people the owner knows who make diagrams, names recorded before W1 (fewer than 2 others: "owner-only, UNMEASURED"). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-49 Banner and toast text (Stories 3.1.4, 3.6.4, 3.6.7, 1.4.6; UX-126, UX-104): a banner row is a severity icon, a FIXED summary and short title chosen by row id (word-wrapped at the computed line width of 320 dp, 15 characters at font scale 1.0 and 7 at 2.0, checked by `check-plan.py`), one primary button and More; dynamic names and counts live only in Details; the pen-detected flip is the 14th row (`PenDetected`); primary labels fit the button and are distinct ("Use new", "Back on", "Draw" and "Turn on" replace four "Turn on" buttons; "Fix it", "Go there" and "Info" become "Pick one", "Open there" and "Learn more"; "Start" stays because "Upgrade" is 7 characters against a 5-character word budget at font scale 2.0), each with an accessible name that is the label plus its object; the Upgrade chip's consequence line is "Upgrade blocks other devices." (the word "digest" is retired for it); a toast is one row with a text budget, one visible action and a More button. Alternative: widen the primary button (costs summary room). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-50 Top bar at large font scale and the Selection menu (Stories 3.1.4; UX-80, UX-119, UX-127): from font scale 1.5 the Finger chip reads "On" or "Off" (still text) and the save status is a glyph-only 44 dp button with the full state in semantics, tooltip and the Blocking banner; the compact-height rail has no selection bar or hint row, so a 48 dp top-bar Selection menu holds Duplicate, Group, Align, Order, Transform and Lock (Overflow in the degraded window).   - D-51 Overlay layout (Story 3.1.4, Task 3.6.3d; UX-119, UX-120): the view cluster is one row of seven targets where the horizontal budget allows and two rows otherwise, the chip row moves above it when it cannot sit beside it (together at most 70% of the collapsed canvas), the mic button leads the chip row, the edge-strip inset is the system gesture inset (assumed 32 dp) plus 44 dp, the pan pad is a plus of five cells (no corner cells), and tool lock is also a chip while on. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-52 Keyboard regions and focus reveal (Stories 3.6.1, 3.6.2, 3.6.3, 3.6.6; UX-117, UX-125): one tab order (top bar, notice region, tool strip, canvas, chip row, crosshair pad, selection bar, view cluster, side pane, Notices list, status line) that F6 and the skip link follow; banner and toast actions are reached through the notice region and the Notices list (no new shortcut chord); focus on a canvas element under an overlay pans the view the least amount that clears it. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-53 Shortcut scope and Alt (Stories 3.6.1, 3.2.2; UX-85, UX-109): the "Single-key shortcuts" setting covers every printable single-character key without a modifier (letters, digits, `?`, `[`, `]`, `Q`), not only letters; Alt is an accelerator on Windows and macOS only, native or web, and never on Linux (the web build detects Linux). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-54 A1 gate measurement (requirements "A1 go/no-go gate", Task 6.2.4e; D-15, D-48): the gate is a PAIRED measurement on ONE diagram (the owner draws the same diagram in the app and in excalidraw.com on the same device, app first so practice favours excalidraw.com), the bar is a ratio of at most 2.0, the gate is n = 1 by design, and the result precedence is PIVOT, then ADJUST (ratio over 2.0 or any named must-fix defect), then PROCEED; the 2.0 ratio, the blocker class and the NoConvergence bars (about 2% to flip, 11 in 100 to stop ship, requirements flag-exit table) are INFERRED and tunable. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-55 Chrome-table test fixture (Task 3.6.4c; engineering E2): `scripts/sketch-chrome/gen-chrome-fixture.py` generates `CT/sketch/v2/ChromeTableFixture.kt` FROM the ux.md chrome table (a Kotlin raw-string constant plus a parser), a CI step (`--check`) fails when the committed fixture differs, so an edit of a figure OR of a banner or toast string in ux.md is covered the same way, and every test that quotes the table reads it, so no figure or string is typed into a test and no Bazel filegroup or wasm file access is needed; the generator takes the ux.md path as a parameter and escapes `$` (selftest); the fixture and parser live in their own no-`@Test` Bazel targets (Tasks 3.6.4c-e). Alternative: a Gradle resource copy plus a Bazel filegroup (not chosen: wasmJs and commonTest cannot read files). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-56 Banner and toast copy rules (Stories 3.1.4, 3.6.4, 1.4.6; UX-126, UX-104, UX-123): rows are checked by greedy word wrap at the computed line width (15 characters per line at 320 dp and font scale 1.0, 7 at 2.0), five summaries were reworded to fit, the Upgrade chip is a consent row with ONE consequence line "Upgrade blocks other devices." and a sheet instead of a short title whenever the wrapped line exceeds 2 lines (computed, D-64), later sketches of an acknowledged project convert on [Start] with no modal because the consequence stays on the chip, every primary label has an accessible name that is label plus object, labels are not reused ("Use new", "Back on", "Draw", "Turn on"), the live region announces the full summary, Details is an item of More (a "Learn more" primary opens it), and chrome text is held at font scale 2.0 above 2.0 while panels and sheets follow the system scale. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-57 Read-only [Use new] and one feature name (Story 1.4.3, 1.4.6; UX-69, UX-76): [Use new] opens the banner variant `ReadOnlyUseNew` of the enable confirmation (copy about THIS already-v2 sketch; stores `NewProjectsOnly` on Turn on; disabled with "Not available in this build yet" while `ConversionReady` is false), stores `NewProjectsOnly` on Turn on, never swaps the open editor and leaves the sketch read-only until reopened (the `EngineChange` toast says so); the user-facing name is "the new editor" and "Sketch engine v2" is never shown. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-58 Toast catalogue (Stories 3.1.4, 3.5.2, 5.2.3, 3.6.7; UX-114, UX-126): the toast table lists a fixed summary, short title, placeholder length and action for constraint added and removed, constraint limit, arrow unbound, locked layer, locked items skipped, library add, insert and delete, file exported, copied to a graph, restored, classic editor, low memory and the engine-change notice; names, counts and file names live in Details. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-59 Flag modes (requirements.md "Flag modes"; ADR-005 item 1a; Tasks 1.4.3a, 1.4.3d, 1.4.3e, 1.4.6h; product gap P1): the flag is the ordered mode `Off` / `NewProjectsOnly` / `On` (stored as a string, unknown reads as Off); `ConversionReady` gates both v2 modes (a new v2 sheet must reach fusion too) and the new build constant `ConversionOffered` gates only `On`, conversion and the Upgrade chip; an A4 NO-GO ships `ConversionOffered = false`, so v2 sheets exist only in `NewSketchProject`s (the canonical sentence: brand-new projects and complete all-v2 projects; an existing empty project is classic) and the Settings control shows "All sketches (Upgrade)" disabled with "Not available in this build yet"; the read-only banner's [Use new] stores `NewProjectsOnly`; the ADR-001 v1-compatible projection option is ESTIMATED at 3 M and 2 S tasks, 0.74M CU, and is not in the plan's counts. Alternative: two booleans (rejected: undefined fourth combination). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-60 Geometric-only fallback acceptance (requirements metric 3, Story 4.1.3, ADR-003 items 6 and 7; P4): the fallback's named scenario is the "fallback plate" (plate 6 x 4 in plus a square pocket, analytic DOF 4, conflicting Distance rejected), metric 3 keeps every numeric bar, the 1e-6 residual bar is an ADR-003 spike exit criterion, and the "Fully constrained" bracket plate waits for Fixed, Radius and Parallel to pass their property tests; if Spike 0.1.3e rules Robolectric out, metric 4 reports Android as "owner-checked" (draw-list equality plus the on-device check). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-61 Parked outcomes and the release path (requirements.md "Parked outcomes", plan "Parked-dependency reductions", Tasks 6.2.5c, 6.2.5e, 6.2.8a; P2): a parked node is recorded `Skipped(<outcome>)` and never deleted; every node with an edge into a parked node has a reduced form (script-checked); the reader-only release is its own task (6.2.5e) with no edge from the gate or any parked node; PIVOT "import/export first" ships the new-project flag release and the pivot costs include the release path (rollout tests, final verification, the two release-notes tasks, the think-aloud protocol). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-62 Pre-check hold (requirements.md "Sunk cost before the A1 gate", plan "Pre-check hold", Task 0.1.7c; P3): on a FAIL the held set is every Epic 2 and 3 node outside the dependency closure of the kept pure engines (Stories 4.1.1-4.1.3, 5.1.1-5.1.4, 5.2.1, 5.2.2, 6.1.2), minus the non-UI diagnostics and fixture tooling; each root of it has an edge from node 0.1.7c (as has every W1 node: the pre-check is wave 0, inside the OWNER0 session, D-107) and the script recomputes the set. The cost of "stop at the pure engines" is the cost of the whole closure: only the KEPT closure is built (33 nodes: 28 outside W1, 6185k raw tokens, 9.15M CU, and 5 W1 spikes it needs, 795k raw tokens, 1.18M CU; 10.33M CU in all, 31.1% of the total) (iteration 13 replaced a hard-coded 15-node list, 5.3M CU of the engines alone, that left out the scene, codec, store and text-metric prerequisites; iteration 14 moved the pre-check into the OWNER0 session, so the W1 spikes the closure needs are counted and the other W1 nodes are not spent); the NOTKEPT line of the plan-hold block names 0.1.2d (A6) and 0.1.3d (float drift): they are lettered tasks of the kept spike stories 0.1.2 and 0.1.3 that no kept node has an edge to, so they are outside the closure (the script's story-level expansion would add them, which is why the line exists), A6 is not read under a stop and both are `Skipped(pre-check)`; the script fails if a NOTKEPT node is named by an edge of a kept node and compares the node names and every figure of this sentence with its own computation. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-63 Metric windows and the A6 ADJUST trigger (requirements metric 8, "A6 go/no-go"): the window start is defined ONCE (the first release in which the owner can create new-editor sketches in a graph they use, the flag release in any mode; `window-start:` is the first line of `quality-signals.md`) and both metric 8 and the flag-exit table read it; A6 ADJUST is at least 1 of 3 in-app AND (at least 1 of the 14 Constrain flyout entries crossed out OR the geometric-only fallback). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-64 Consent sheet (ux.md 14.2, Task 1.4.6i; U1, U2): the sheet replaces the Upgrade chip row whenever the wrapped summary exceeds the row's 2 lines (computed per window width: from font scale 1.11 at 320 dp wide and from 1.52 at 360 dp wide); it opens at most once per sketch project per day on this device (D-102) and never again in the same session after a dismissal, never mid-gesture or over text entry, every dismissal means Not now, focus moves in and returns, the banner slot is left empty and the sketch menu and the Notices list keep the upgrade reachable; the consent button stays "Start" because "Upgrade" needs a wider button that the computed block prices. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-65 First-run walkthrough (ux.md section 22, Task 3.6.8e; U3): three steps (tools, zoom, shortcuts) with Back, Next, Done, Esc, "Step n of 3", focus to the popover and back (the popover is a labelled non-modal region in the tab order after its anchor: Tab moves through it and on without wrapping, F6 leaves to the next region and the tour continues, Esc ends it from the popover or from anywhere), one polite announcement per step (step 1 is announced through the focused popover's accessible name), touch, mouse and keyboard wording chosen when a step is shown (and for step 1 by the flat or grouped strip; the Dock never collapses) with no screen positions, no key that needs canvas focus and no instruction to act on the pointed-at control, popover placement and highlight-ring contrast rules, re-runnable from the overflow entry "Show tips". Owner: Tyler.
  - D-66 Pre-check FAIL outcomes and precedence (requirements.md "Combined outcomes", "Sunk cost before the A1 gate"; Task 0.1.7c, `plan-hold` block; product gap P1): "stop entirely" (added in iteration 12, D-84) builds nothing further; "stop at the pure engines" records every node outside the kept closure `Skipped(pre-check)` (so also Story 4.1.4, Story 6.1.1, the reader-only release 6.2.5e, the A1 gate 6.2.4e and the flag release 6.2.5c; the HELD line covers Epics 2 and 3 only and `check-plan.py` computes the wider stop set) and ships nothing user-visible; "continue, adoption is not the goal" only releases the hold, the A1 gate still runs as written including PIVOT on "would not continue" and its fourth outcome "unmeasured under the cap" (D-108), and the gate never re-imposes the hold; the reader-only release ships in every outcome except v1 extended only and a pre-check stop. Alternative: let "continue" pre-answer the gate's "would you continue" question (rejected: the gate is the later, better-informed answer). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-67 Default-flip criteria for NewProjectsOnly-ceiling rows (requirements.md "Flag and legacy-editor exit criteria"; Task 6.2.5d; product gap P2): the default flips Off to `NewProjectsOnly` on A4 NO-GO, PIVOT "import/export first" and PIVOT "Job 2 first" under A4 NO-GO against the same signal table, minimum n, window start and 26-week cap as the `On` flip, with the A1 criterion read as PROCEED or the option's PIVOT ADR, `ParityV1SuiteTest` limited to behaviours a new sheet uses, the reader-only release out, and WITHOUT "every device reader-capable (A4)" (false by definition there, and not needed because `NewProjectsOnly` creates v2 only in a `NewSketchProject`); the owner decides by recording it in the ADR-005 index, nothing flips automatically, and a flipped default still shows the per-device enable confirmation first. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-68 `NewSketchProject` means brand-new (requirements.md "Flag modes"; ADR-005 item 1a; Tasks 1.4.3d; product gap P3): under `NewProjectsOnly` a new sheet is v2 only in a project created by that very create action or in an existing project that has at least one sheet and only v2 sheets; an existing project with no sheets is classic (the user-facing reasons are the one `ClassicEditor` table of ux.md 14.1), so no project an older build opens today becomes unreadable; `SheetSplitter` creates a v2 sheet from a v2 sheet in every mode that lets the source be edited. Alternative: let an existing empty project count (rejected: it breaks the "no existing project becomes unreadable" guarantee of A4 NO-GO). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-69 Read-only banner [Use new] has its own confirmation copy (ux.md 14.1 and section 1; Task 1.4.6c; U1): `SketchConsentCopy.ReadOnlyUseNew` says the sketch already uses the new editor, that reopening unlocks editing THIS sketch, that new sketches in new projects also use it, that classic sketches stay classic and that older versions of SteleKit already cannot open this project; its confirming button is [Turn on and reopen], which stores the mode and reopens the sketch at once (two steps, not three: D-90); it never carries a "stay classic" promise about the sketch itself. Owner: Tyler.

  - D-104 **OWNER DECISION (2026-11-15): Web gets the new editor through OPFS atomic storage, Option W (PROMINENT; this entry replaces the earlier default that capped Web at Off; requirements.md "Web first release: what works", Story 1.4.3 platform-capability bullet, Stories 0.1.8 and 1.4.7, Task 1.4.3h; engineering E3, UX-133)**: verified in the code, `supportsTempRenameWrites` is false on wasmJs (`wasmJsMain/.../PlatformFileSystem.kt:622`) and iOS (`iosMain/.../PlatformFileSystem.kt:15`), so before this change `AtomicFileWriter.mode` is UNSUPPORTED and `FileProjectSidecarStore.read` and every write return `Unavailable` there, and wasmJs has no `readFileBytes` override (the default throws). The owner chose to make Web a v2 target: wasmJs gets an OPFS atomic replace and the byte and prefix reads (Story 1.4.7, D-112); `V2StorageAvailable` is true on wasmJs in a browser whose OPFS verdict is Available (so the `NewSketchProject` predicate runs there), false in a browser without it (that browser behaves as `Off` with the browser reason), true on JVM and Android, false on iOS, and false when the store is null (D-116); `.excalidraw` import is enabled on Web again (it creates v2 sheets) through a canvas-local drop handler (BUG-004 is open); a v2 sheet that reaches iOS or an OPFS-less browser opens as no editor with that reason and recovery actions (D-117); Task 1.4.3g keeps JVM and Android and the wasm `readFilePrefix` override and the cache-served wasm read claims live in Task 1.4.7b; iOS stays compile-only and storage-unavailable. Cost (owner-visible, re-costed in iteration 17 from the actual task list): Option W costs 7 M, 9 S and 2 XS tasks (Tasks 0.1.8a, 0.1.8b, 0.1.8c, 1.4.7a, 1.4.7b, 1.4.7c, 1.4.7d, 1.4.7e, 1.4.7f, 1.4.7g, 1.4.7h, 1.4.7i, 1.4.7j, 1.4.7k, 1.4.7l, 1.4.7m, 1.4.7n, 1.4.7o), 1445k raw tokens, 2.14M CU (3.74M CU with verification), 6.4% of the total of 33.20M CU; the shared storage ports of Story 1.4.3 that stay built when it is dropped (Tasks 1.4.3k, 1.4.3l, 1.4.3m, 1.4.3n, 1.4.3o, 1.4.3p, 1.4.3q, 1.4.3r, 1.4.3s, 1.4.3t) cost 590k raw tokens, 0.87M CU; Option W is 2.14M CU, 6.4% of the total; the total web footprint is 3.01M CU (2035k raw tokens, 9.1% of the total, stated once in requirements.md); iteration 16 priced it at 1035k raw tokens and 1.53M CU (2.68M CU with verification, 4.9% of the then total of 31.52M CU) and iteration 15 priced it at 445k raw tokens and 0.66M CU because it had four tasks for the verdict, the primitives, the file system and one test and none for the durability contract, the write ordering, the temp and rename side effects, the stale-temp sweep, the two-tab lease and its consumers, the preload fact and its bound, the saver states, the composition roots or the web UX (D-119 to D-145); the decision is unchanged and the owner may reconsider it with these numbers, by the rule of D-133. Owner: Tyler (decided).
  - D-112 Option W design and its per-browser pivot (requirements.md "Web first release: what works"; Stories 0.1.8 and 1.4.7; Tasks 0.1.8a, 0.1.8b, 1.4.7a to 1.4.7m; engineering): `AtomicFileWriter` runs on wasm in `ATOMIC_REPLACE` mode over `createWritable()` (committed on `close()`) plus `FileSystemFileHandle.move()`, or, where `move()` fails the spike, write-temp-then-copy with a byte-for-byte read-back of the temp and of the destination before the temp is deleted (a mismatch re-copies from the verified temp once and keeps the temp when it still differs); the wasm `PlatformFileSystem` serves bytes, prefixes, listing and existence from its preloaded cache and persists through ONE ordered queue (D-119 to D-122); every browser-API fact is INFERRED / UNVERIFIED until Spike 0.1.8 has a row for the browser; per-browser pivot: a browser failing any of the four gating criteria is `Unavailable` and keeps the classic-only state (stored mode behaves as `Off`, mode control disabled with the reason, no v2 sheet created or opened), the other browsers are unaffected, and if neither Chrome nor Firefox passes Option W is abandoned as a whole, Story 1.4.7 is recorded `Skipped(opfs-spike)` and the owner re-records D-104 (D-127); Safari is the expected risk and no worker-based `createSyncAccessHandle` path is planned (owner-confirmable); an ephemeral ("open temporarily") web graph never reaches OPFS by design, so its sketches live for the session only and the first-write dialog says so (UX-139). Owner: Tyler.

  - D-114 Spike 0.1.2 decides the solver tier per target (requirements.md "Abort and pivot criteria"; Task 0.1.2c; ADR-003 item 7; product P4): `SolverTier` is `Full`, `GeometricOnly` or `Hidden` per target; target-independent misses (residual, classification, branch flip) select `GeometricOnly` everywhere, a time miss only on its own target, so a wasmJs miss never takes the full solver from desktop and Android; the geometric-only set is re-measured on the missing target and that target is `Hidden` if it misses too; metric 3 is judged per target on the scenario its tier can pass.   - D-119 What "saved" means in a browser, and the acknowledgement contract (Tasks 1.4.7e, 1.4.7f, 1.4.7k; engineering E1; UX-140): a browser has no `fsync`, so `Persisted` means the OPFS `close()` or `move()` of the final path resolved; the synchronous `AtomicFileWriter.write` returns `Right` at `Cached`, a new `FileSystem.awaitDurable(path)` (default `Right`, a wasm override over the queue) lets `SheetSaver` report `Saved` only at `Persisted`, an asynchronous failure (quota, missing `move()`, read-back mismatch) arrives as a typed error and the existing `SaveFailed` banner, the scene is never rolled back, and `visibilitychange` flushes at once while `beforeunload` guards unpersisted paths. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-120 One ordered OPFS write queue per path set (Task 1.4.7f; engineering E2): every OPFS write, delete, move and mirror write is enqueued behind the tails of all the paths it names, `opfsWriteInFlight[path]` becomes the tail so the host write-through also waits for earlier operations, and an operation clears its entry only while it is the tail. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-121 Temp, replace and rename side effects on wasm (Task 1.4.7g; engineering E3): a temp is written through `writeTempBytes` (bytes cache plus OPFS only: not dirty, no host write-through), a replace or rename marks the TARGET dirty once, drops the temp and the stale entry of the other cache and schedules one host write-through of the final bytes, and a rename of a source never records a `DELETE`. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-122 Stale temp files on wasm (Task 1.4.7h; engineering E4): `StaleTempSweeper` falls back to the UUIDv7 timestamp of `<name>.<uuidv7>.tmp` when the modification time is null (it always is for an OPFS-only file) and the wasm `preload` runs the recursive `sweepTree` once under a short-lived sweep lock, never under the writer lease (D-130); the claim that a stale temp is removed "on the next write" was false and is gone. Owner: Tyler.

  - D-146 Seam classification, who edits `WiringAudit.SEAMS`, and no red window (Tasks 1.4.3p, 1.4.3q, 1.4.3s, 1.4.3t, 1.4.7e, 1.4.7i, 1.4.7m, 2.1.2b, 2.1.2c, 6.2.1a, 6.2.1c; engineering E1, E2; supersedes the placement of D-132 and D-141 where they differ): read in the audit code (`WiringAudit.findImplementations` finds implementations only of the interfaces on `SEAMS`; `evaluate` fails an implementation that no root constructs; `WiringCatalog.check` fails a line whose seam is not on `SEAMS` and an entry-point test that never calls `WiredAppFixture.` or `DesktopWiredAppFixture.`; `WiringCompletenessTest.realCatalog_should_PassAgainstRealTestSources` runs the real catalog). businessTest cannot call either fixture (they exist only in `androidUnitTest` and `jvmTest`), so no entry-point test lives in businessTest. Of the 14 names of Story 6.2.1, five are interfaces that go on `SEAMS` (`TextMetricsProvider`, `SketchWriterLease`, `BrowserPersistence`, `SketchDurability`, `PageLifecycle`; the rows of the `plan-wiring` table) and nine are classes or pure strategies that their owner builds, are never audited and never catalogued (the `plan-seam-classes` block), covered by the two reachability tests of Task 6.2.1a. **The rule that removes every red window** (`check-plan.py` checks it per row of `plan-wiring`): an implementation must be constructed no later than the later of (the task that adds its interface to `SEAMS`, the task that declares it): either the SEAMS task constructs it (Task 1.4.3p for the two commonMain defaults, Task 1.4.7m for the web durability and lifecycle classes) or its declaring task constructs it (Task 2.1.2b for `ComposeTextMetricsProvider`, Tasks 1.4.7e and 1.4.7i for the wasm classes, at both wasm `StelekitAppPlatformIntegrations(` sites, which exist as required fields since Task 1.4.3t); a catalog line is added only by a task after the SEAMS task and its entry-point test lives in `androidUnitTest` or `jvmTest`. The SEAMS list assertion of `WiringCompletenessTest` counts 7 names today, 8 after Task 2.1.2b, 10 after Task 1.4.3p and 12 after Task 1.4.7m (script-computed from the headings of the three tasks, which have a path between them, so no two tasks edit the list in one wave). The new fields of `StelekitAppPlatformIntegrations` are REQUIRED (the file's own rule, "no default on purpose"): Task 1.4.3p adds them with temporary defaults, Task 1.4.3s passes them from `SketchStorageDefaults` at the Android and desktop sites and the two fixtures, and Task 1.4.3t does the same at both wasm sites (`browser/Main.kt` lines 365 and 516) and deletes the defaults; `SketchStorageHostSitesTest` fails when a production site omits a field or constructs the default itself, which is what the two entry-point tests of Task 1.4.3q alone could not show (the Android fixture builds its own integrations). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-147 The classic file side on the web build is gated by a separate per-browser setting, default `Off` (Tasks 1.4.7o and 1.4.7n; requirements.md "Web baseline for classic sketches" consequence 2 and metric 6; product P1; owner-confirmable): the store's availability is the file system's atomic-write capability, so without a gate Option W would turn on, for the first time on wasm, the classic sketch's sidecar write-through, `SidecarReconciler` pull, `importNew`, `replaceProjectContents`, the exports and `preview.svg` for every classic project in an Available browser, in mode `Off` and without the flag `sketch.engine.v2`, contradicting "classic sketches are unchanged" and putting a path that never ran on wasm between a web database and a desktop-synced bundle. The classic file side on the web therefore runs for a classic sheet only when BOTH the OPFS verdict is `Available` AND the setting `sketch.web.classicFileSide` (per browser, independent of `V2Mode`) is `On`; the default is `Off`, so a classic-only project behaves exactly as before (database authoritative, `DeviceLocal`, `AtomicWriteUnsupported`, no `preview.svg`); a v2 sheet is reconciled regardless of the setting, per sheet and never per project (D-153: the classic sheets of a project that also holds a v2 sheet stay database-only under `Off`), because v2 sheets live in the sidecar and cannot exist without the opt-in of the new editor. There is no UI for the setting in this release; the owner turns it on for the browser pass in the console (`localStorage.setItem('sketch.web.classicFileSide', 'On')`, then a reload, Task 1.4.7d), and choosing `On` as a release default is a release decision that waits for `WasmClassicReconcileTest` and that pass and is the owner's. The test covers what exists only because wasm gains a file side: an existing web database plus a desktop-synced bundle of the same slug, divergent edits, and the database overwrite of a pull, with the setting `Off` (nothing touched) and `On` (the reconcile outcome rows of the desktop `SidecarReconcilerTest`); that the same code runs unchanged on wasm is INFERRED until it is green. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-148 The post-spike re-read of the pre-check (Task 0.1.8c; requirements.md "Sunk cost before the A1 gate", block `post-spike-recount`; product P2; owner-confirmable): the pre-check PASS can rest on web items counted provisionally, and Spike 0.1.8 can void them (three Firefox items, Firefox fails: `ABANDON-DEMAND`); D-113 and D-133 made the recount information only, so a spike-voided PASS reached the W9 spend and was caught only at the A1 gate as UNMEASURED. Task 0.1.8c is an edge-block gate like Task 0.1.7c with zero agent work: the line `0.1.8c: 0.1.8 @ZW` makes it zero-width (wave W1, so no wave is added) and every W2 node has an edge from it, so no W2 node starts before the owner has recorded `continue` or `stop`; the gate asks for a decision exactly when the recount is FAIL and the recorded result was not (every case of the block is recomputed by `check-plan.py`); `continue` keeps the recorded result on the owner's stated reason, `stop` uses the existing pre-check stop rows (the kept closure and the stop set are the same, the W1 spend is already incurred); it is excluded from every closure the way the gate 0.1.7c is, and it is skipped with the spike when Option W is dropped at OWNER0. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-149 Boot refusal versus the `OtherTab` banner (UX-152, UX-142, UX-149; Tasks 1.4.6k, 1.4.7i, 1.4.7d; engineering U1): verified in the code, a second tab on a persistent web graph fails `WebLock.tryAcquireLeader("stelekit-sqlite-driver-<graphId>")` in `DriverFactory.createDriverAsync` (`DriverFactory.js.kt:38`) and `browser/Main.kt:278` calls `showBootError`, which writes a plain `<p>` into `#loading`; the editor never exists, so the `OtherTab` banner cannot appear. Outcomes by platform and graph kind: Desktop and Android show neither (the lease is always held); web, persistent graph: boot refusal; web, graph opened temporarily or the in-memory demo fallback: neither (no driver lock, separate in-memory databases, no OPFS sidecar, so the writer lease is never taken); the `OtherTab` banner stays only as defence in depth for a driver that does not take the lock and for the two-instance unit tests. The refusal screen is specified in ux.md 14.9 (`boot-refusal` block) and built by Task 1.4.6k, with an automatic retry that waits on the same lock through `navigator.locks.request(name, { signal })` and reloads when it is granted (INFERRED: Task 1.4.7d records which of the two a real second tab shows and whether the pending request is granted when the first tab closes). Owner: Tyler.
  - D-150 A bundle parked under one lease epoch is never replayed under a later one (Tasks 1.4.3m, 1.4.3o, 1.4.7i; engineering E3): `flushPending` kept a write parked while the lease was not `Held`, but a bundle parked while `Held` and replayed after the lease was lost and re-acquired would overwrite the newer sheets and model of the other tab, because re-acquiring reloads the sidecar root. The `SketchWriterLease` port carries a monotonic `epoch` that grows each time the lease becomes `Held` again (constant on `AlwaysHeldSketchWriterLease`); the store stamps every parked bundle with the epoch at park time and `flushPending` replays only a bundle whose epoch equals the current one; a bundle parked under an older epoch is turned into conflict copies instead (`writeSheetConflictCopy` per sheet it carries, and `<slug>/conflicts/project-model.conflict-<hash8>.json` for its model, both lazy files of the preload table), a `sketch.save.stale_bundle_to_conflict` log line carries ids only, and the saver shows the existing `SaveConflict` banner. Tests: `flushPendingAfterLeaseReacquire_should_NotReplayStaleBundle`, `staleParkedBundle_should_BecomeConflictCopy`, `staleParkedModel_should_BecomeConflictCopy`, `reacquire_should_ReloadTheSidecarRootAndGrowTheEpoch`. Owner: Tyler.
  - D-151 The private-window cue at large text scale (UX-153; ux.md 14.8 `durability-wording` and `hint-cue` blocks; engineering U2): `HintStatus` is absent in the windows the computed block of ux.md lists (D-155 corrected the set), exactly where a touch user has no tooltip, and the 31-character cue wraps to two lines in a one-line row at scale 2.0. The cue gets shorter forms (D-155: four, the compact one being "Private: may clear.", 19 characters, at most the 20 characters one line holds at scale 2.0), a rule for sharing the one hint line with the "Tool lock on" prefix and the hint text, and an alternative home where the row collapses: a persistent chip in the action chip row, which already has its height reserved in the overlay figure of the chrome table, so no layout figure changes; `check-plan.py` fits the cue at both scales and lists the windows where `HintStatus` collapses from the table. Owner: Tyler.
