# Phase 1: Foundation (a dependency DAG)

### Epic 1.1: Sidecar codec and canonical serialization
**Goal**: Make the sidecar schema-aware per file kind and deterministic before any v2 data exists.

#### Story 1.1.1: Per-kind schema limits and `SheetCodec` dispatch (Refactor-first)
**As a** maintainer, **I want** schema limits per file kind and a codec per schema, **so that** v2 sheets are readable by new builds and still rejected by old ones.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Model limit stays 1; sheet limit becomes 2; schema 3 still fails closed.
  - *Given* the new classifier, *When* `classify(kind=Sheet, schema=2)` and `classify(kind=Sheet, schema=3)` run, *Then* the first is `Valid` and the second is `Corrupt(NewerSchema)`; `classify(kind=Model, schema=2)` is `Corrupt(NewerSchema)`.
- A referenced v2 sheet is readable by the new reader through the store.
  - *Given* a model whose `sheetRef` references `sheets/s2.v2.json` holding a `schema:2` sheet with a matching `fileSha256`, *When* the reordered `readLocked` (Task 0.1.1b) runs with the per-kind limits, *Then* the result is `Present` including that file, and the same bytes referenced from a model that is read by the simulated old limit (previous max 1) are `Corrupt(NewerSchema)`.
- v1 bundles round-trip byte-identically.
  - *Given* every existing golden v1 bundle fixture, *When* decoded then re-encoded by the v1 `SheetCodec`, *Then* the bytes equal the input (existing golden tests unchanged and green).
**Files**: `CM/projectbridge/SidecarFileState.kt`, `CM/projectbridge/ProjectSidecarStore.kt`, `CM/projectbridge/ProjectModelJson.kt`, `CM/projectbridge/SheetCodec.kt` (new), `BT/projectbridge/SheetCodecDispatchTest.kt`
##### Task 1.1.1a: Introduce `SidecarFileKind` and per-kind max; keep `SUPPORTED_SCHEMA` as deprecated alias for model (S)
##### Task 1.1.1b: Extract v1 sheet encode/decode behind `SheetCodec` with no behaviour change (M)
##### Task 1.1.1c: Extend Story 0.1.1 gate to the new classifier (old-build behaviour simulated by the previous max) (XS)

#### Story 1.1.2: Canonical JSON for doubles and stable ordering
**As a** maintainer, **I want** one deterministic number/key format, **so that** hashes and git diffs match on every target.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Property: decode(encode(x)) equals x for finite doubles; NaN/Infinity rejected.
  - *Given* any finite `Double` from `Arb.double()`, *When* encoded by `CanonicalJson` and decoded, *Then* the result equals the input; and *Given* `Double.NaN`, *Then* encoding returns `Either.Left(NonFinite)`.
- Golden bytes equal across JVM and wasmJs.
  - *Given* the fixture scene from Story 0.1.3, *When* serialised on JVM and wasmJs, *Then* SHA-256 of the output is identical.
- Header-first key order (E1; ADR-001 item 5, D-105).
  - *Given* an object with the keys `elements`, `layers`, `extras`, `sheetUuid` and `schema`, *When* encoded with `headerFirstKeys = [schema, sheetUuid]`, *Then* the output begins `{"schema":` and `"sheetUuid":` comes second, the remaining keys follow in sorted order, every nested object is fully sorted, and the bytes are identical on JVM and wasmJs (`CanonicalJsonTest.headerKeys_should_LeadAndTheRestSorted`).
**Files**: `V2/codec/CanonicalJson.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/CanonicalJsonTest.kt`
##### Task 1.1.2a: Implement formatter + sorted-key emitter over `kotlinx-serialization-json` `JsonElement`, with the header-first exception (`schema` then `sheetUuid` first, every other key sorted; ADR-001 item 5, D-105) (S)
##### Task 1.1.2b: Property + golden tests, wasm run (S)

#### Story 1.1.3: Bundle pipeline dispatches per sheet schema (Refactor-first; BLOCKER A)
**As a** maintainer, **I want** `ProjectBundle`, `SidecarCodec`, `SidecarReconciler` and both `replaceProjectContents` implementations to dispatch per sheet schema and pass v2 sheets through untouched, **so that** no project-level write (dimension edit, post-sync coordinator, DB restore, pull) can drop or rewrite a v2 sheet.
**Design** (verified facts: `SidecarCodec.encode` builds each sheet file only from `bundle.primitives`; `decodeUnsafe` reads only `primitives`; `loadLocal` builds the bundle from DB `getPrimitives`; `reconcile()` hashes `encode(local)`; `replaceProjectContents` is the only pull path):
- `ProjectBundle` gains `sheetPayloads: Map<SheetUuid, SheetPayload>`; `SheetPayload` is sealed `V1(primitives)` / `V2Opaque(bytes, sha256)`. `SketchSheet` gains `schemaVersion` (1 default). `primitives` stays and holds v1 sheets' primitives only.
- **Single owner of the v2 sheet path/file/schema fields (this story; Stories 0.1.1, 1.4.2, 6.2.6 and every fixture use exactly this, nothing re-defines it)**: `SheetPaths.pathFor(uuid, schema)` in `CM/projectbridge/SheetPaths.kt` is the one rule: schema 1 -> `sheets/<uuid>.json` (today's `sheetPath(uuid)`), schema 2 -> `sheets/<uuid>.v2.json`. The model's `sheetRef` gains `schema` (omitted = 1) and, for schema 2, an explicit `file` equal to `SheetPaths.pathFor(uuid, 2)`; decode REJECTS (`Corrupt`) a `file` that differs from the rule (no arbitrary paths from git-synced input), and `fileSha256` stays the sha of the referenced file's bytes. `SketchSheet.schemaVersion` is the only DB-side field: the file path is derived, so there is no `file` column. `SidecarCodec.sheetPath` call sites (`ProjectModelJson.kt:48,57,174`) all go through `SheetPaths`. The model's top-level `schema` stays 1. Fixtures: v1 sheet `sheets/s1.json`, v2 sheet `sheets/s2.v2.json`.
- **Opaque bytes are never written by the reconciler (data-loss rule, review C2)**: `reconcile`'s `WriteLocal`/push NEVER writes a `V2Opaque` sheet's bytes; the store call carries `opaque: Map<path, expectedSha>` for every v2 sheet: `write` does not replace or delete those paths, and verifies under its mutex that each on-disk file's sha equals `expectedSha` before writing the model (which names that sha); on mismatch it writes nothing and returns `SidecarWriteOutcome.StaleOpaque`, and the reconciler re-reads and re-plans. v2 bytes are therefore only ever written by `SheetSaver`, `SheetConverter` and the restore operation.
- **Parked and replayed writes are re-validated at flush time (pre-mortem P1 #2)**: today `write()` parks the whole `files` map last-wins in `pending[slug]` when git sync is busy and `flushPending` later calls `writeLocked(slug, files)` unconditionally (`ProjectSidecarStore.kt:98-103, 157-166`). The `opaque`/`expectedSha` check therefore runs INSIDE `flushPending`, under the store mutex and the slug lock, not only at park time: a parked entry stores its `opaque` map, and at flush every opaque path's on-disk sha is re-verified; on any mismatch (or when the model on disk now references a different sheet file or schema than the parked bundle assumed) the parked entry is dropped as `StaleOpaque` and the reconciler re-reads and re-plans (parked entries are intents, never replayed byte maps). A parked bundle can therefore never rewrite a converted model back to v1 or delete a v2 file.
- **One per-slug lock for every sidecar writer**: `SidecarSlugLock` (`CM/projectbridge/SidecarSlugLock.kt`, one `Mutex` per slug, held by the caller across read-plan-write, not just inside a single store call). The reconciler (load, decide, push), `SheetSaver`, `SheetConverter` (across S1-S4) and `SketchBackupRestore` all take it. Lock order: slug lock first, then the store's own mutex; nothing acquires a slug lock while holding another.
- **v2 sheet metadata round trip (review C6)**: `encode(local)` rebuilds `sheetRef` (kind, scale, `layers`, zones, `file`, `schema`) from the DB `SketchSheet`, so `SheetSaver` (Story 1.4.4) updates the DB sheet row (`layers`, `schemaVersion`) in the same locked operation in which it writes the file and the model's layer mirror; the sha is not stored in the DB, `encode` takes it from the loaded v2 bytes.
- `SidecarCodec.encode` writes a `V2Opaque` sheet byte-for-byte and takes its `fileSha256` from the bytes; `decodeUnsafe` classifies via the per-kind `SheetCodec` and returns `V2Opaque` for schema 2 WITHOUT interpreting it (interpretation belongs to `SheetCodecV2` in the editor).
- `SidecarReconciler.loadLocal` takes v2 sheets' bytes from the local sidecar file, never from DB primitives (the DB holds no v2 content); `pull`/`importNew` record `schema_version` and sha for v2 sheets in the DB via `replaceProjectContents`; `WriteLocal` never writes a v2 sheet's bytes (see the opaque rule above); it writes only the model and v1 sheets.
- Both `replaceProjectContents` implementations store a v2 sheet as a sheet row (`schema_version = 2`, sha) with no primitive rows and never delete or rewrite another sheet's data.
- Fusion/digest read v2 sheets via in-memory `V2ToV1.project` only (Story 1.4.5), never from `sketch_primitives`.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- A v2 sheet survives every project-level write unchanged.
  - *Given* a project with v1 sheet `s1` and v2 sheet `s2` (fixture bytes B), *When* each of these runs: a dimension edit save, the post-sync coordinator, `SidecarReconciler.reconcile` with an unchanged DB, a DB restore into an empty DB from the disk bundle, and a pull of a remote bundle where only `s1` changed, *Then* `sheets/s2.v2.json` bytes still equal B, the model's `fileSha256` for `s2` equals sha256(B), and the reconcile decision for the unchanged case is `Unchanged` with zero file writes (golden test).
- DB restore of a v2 sheet.
  - *Given* an empty DB and a disk bundle containing `s2`, *When* the reconciler restores, *Then* `sketch_sheets` has a row for `s2` with `schema_version = 2`, no `sketch_primitives` rows exist for `s2`, and the disk file is not rewritten.
- v1 bundles are still byte-identical.
  - *Given* every existing v1 golden bundle, *When* decoded and re-encoded through the dispatching codec, *Then* the bytes equal the input and existing reconciler/sidecar tests pass unchanged.
- A corrupt or truncated v2 sheet is never repaired by the reconciler.
  - *Given* `s2` bytes whose sha differs from the model, *When* reconcile runs, *Then* the decision is `KeepCorrupt` and no file is written.
- A stale reconcile push can never overwrite a newer v2 save or revert the S4 flip (review C2).
  - *Given* `reconcile` has done `loadLocal` for slug `kitchen` (v2 sheet `s2`, bytes B0), *When* `SheetSaver` writes B1 for `s2` (and the model with `sha(B1)`) before the reconciler's push, *Then* with the slug lock the saver blocks until the reconcile finishes and B1 is the final file, and with the lock deliberately bypassed (store-level test) the push returns `StaleOpaque` and writes nothing; and *Given* the same interleaving with `SheetConverter`'s S4 model flip in place of the saver, *Then* the model still references `sheets/s2.v2.json` afterwards (`SidecarWriterRaceTest`).
- A parked bundle replayed after conversion cannot undo it (pre-mortem P1 #2; `SidecarWriterRaceTest` park-convert-flush case).
  - *Given* git sync is busy and a project-level write (a dimension edit) built from the v1-shaped bundle of `kitchen` is PARKED in `pending["kitchen"]`, *When* `SheetConverter` then runs S1-S4 through `writeNow` for `s1` (v1 -> v2) and git sync goes idle and `flushPending` runs, *Then* the parked entry is dropped as `StaleOpaque`, `sheets/s1.v2.json` bytes are unchanged, the model still references it with its sha, no `Corrupt(Truncated)` appears, and a re-planned reconcile writes the dimension edit through the current model; the same case with the parked entry built AFTER S4 but replayed after a second `SheetSaver` write is also dropped.
- v2 metadata round trip.
  - *Given* a v2 sheet `s2`, *When* `SheetSaver` changes its layers to `["Base","Walls"]` (file, model mirror and DB row in one locked operation) and the reconciler then runs, *Then* the decision is `Unchanged` and nothing is written (golden over `encode(local)` vs the on-disk model bytes); and a model whose `sheetRef.file` differs from `SheetPaths.pathFor` is `Corrupt`.
**Files**: `CM/model/SketchModels.kt` (`ProjectBundle`, `SketchSheet`), `CM/projectbridge/ProjectModelJson.kt` (`SidecarCodec`), `CM/projectbridge/SidecarReconciler.kt`, `CM/projectbridge/ProjectSidecarStore.kt`, `CM/projectbridge/SheetCodec.kt`, `CM/repository/SqlDelightSketchRepositories.kt` (`replaceProjectContents`), `CM/repository/InMemorySketchRepositories.kt` (`replaceProjectContents`), `CM/repository/SketchProjectRepository.kt` (interface docs), `CM/projectbridge/SheetPaths.kt` (new), `CM/projectbridge/SidecarSlugLock.kt` (new), `BT/projectbridge/BundleV2PassthroughTest.kt`, `BT/projectbridge/ReconcilerV2GoldenTest.kt`, `BT/projectbridge/SidecarWriterRaceTest.kt`, `BT/projectbridge/SidecarStoreOpaqueContractTest.kt` (new, Task 1.1.3b), `BT/repository/ReplaceProjectContentsV2Test.kt`
##### Task 1.1.3a: `SheetPayload`, `ProjectBundle`/`SketchSheet` fields, codec dispatch; v1 goldens stay byte-identical (M)
##### Task 1.1.3b: Store contract plus lock: `SheetPaths` (the one path rule), the `opaque`/`expectedSha` contract (`StaleOpaque`) enforced in `write`, `writeNow` AND `flushPending` (parked entries are intents, re-validated at flush time), and `SidecarSlugLock`, with `SidecarStoreOpaqueContractTest` (store-level, lock deliberately bypassed) (M)
##### Task 1.1.3c: Reconciler: `SidecarReconciler.loadLocal/pull/importNew/reconcile` for `V2Opaque` (v2 bytes read from the local sidecar file, never written by `WriteLocal`, `KeepCorrupt` on sha mismatch), re-plan on `StaleOpaque`, taking the slug lock around read-plan-write (M)
##### Task 1.1.3d: Both `replaceProjectContents` implementations, DB-restore and corrupt-v2 tests, shared contract test over SqlDelight and InMemory (M)
##### Task 1.1.3e: Goldens over the whole story: unchanged-v2 no-write, v2 metadata round trip and `SidecarWriterRaceTest` including the park-convert-flush case (S); split from the former 1.1.3b so no task bundles store, reconciler and goldens

#### Story 1.1.4: Technical data has a home in v2 (BLOCKER C; accessors, layer mirror, refusal predicate)
**As a** user with dimensions and zones, **I want** them to stay in the model file untouched by v2, **so that** fusion, CSV and digests are unchanged.
**Decision** (verified: zones, layer names and scale live in the model file's `sheetRef` and dimensions in the model's `dimensions[]` - `ProjectModelJson.kt:100-112,180-196` - not in the sheet file; `SketchDimension` has NO primitive reference, its fields are key, kind, value, precision, sourceKind, sourceRef (a label ref or `png:...`), tolerance, locked; a zone is a named typed-length row resolved by the fusion solver, independent of geometry): technical data stays in `project-model.json`, carried verbatim and unmodified by v2. A v2 sheet file holds only the scene. v2 layers are authoritative for v2 sheets; the model's `sheetRef.layers` (names) is re-derived from them in the same model write as a mirror for v1 consumers. Because a dimension has no primitive reference, conversion cannot break one; the preservation criterion is OUTPUT equality of fusion, CSV and digest before and after (Story 1.4.2 golden), not id resolution. `sourceRef` labels: deleting or editing a labelled v2 element does nothing to the dimension row (it is a project-level value, same as v1); fusion treats a missing label exactly as it does in v1. Sum/equal constraints and zone checks are the existing v1 fusion-stage checks over projected primitives; the new solver does NOT subsume them (it handles geometric/dimensional constraints inside a sheet).
**Conversion-refusal predicate (exact; owned here as `ConversionPolicy.refusal(project, sheet): HasTechnicalData?`)**: conversion of v1 sheet X is refused with `ConversionState.Failed(HasTechnicalData)` iff (a) X's own `sheetRef.zones` is non-empty, OR (b) the project's `dimensions[]` is non-empty (dimensions are project-scoped, there is no sheet uuid on them, so any dimension in the project refuses conversion of every sheet of that project). Reason: until Story 4.2.5 the v2 editor has no surface to edit zones or dimensions, and the legacy editor remains the only coherent editor for such a project. The refusal never touches data (no backup, no write). Router reason text for it lives in `SketchReasonText` (`HasTechnicalData`: "This sketch has zones or dimensions. Open it in the legacy editor."; that sentence is for refusing to convert an EXISTING sheet and is never used for a NEW sheet, whose `ClassicEditor` Details use the separate fragment `TechnicalDataNewSheet`, "this project has dimensions or zones, and the new editor can't hold those yet", because the user is already in the classic editor). After Story 4.2.5 `ConversionPolicy.refusal` returns null. **NEW v2 sheets before 4.2.5**: a sheet created as v2 (flag on) has no zones or dimensions and the v2 editor has no zone/dimension surface until 4.2.5 (stated gap, not hidden); to avoid trapping a technical project, a NEW sheet is created as v2 only if the project passes the same predicate (no dimensions; the new sheet has no zones), otherwise it is created as v1. An existing v2 sheet stays editable if the project later gains dimensions via a legacy sheet (the refusal applies at conversion/creation only). Parity gate Story 6.2.4 blocks legacy-editor deletion until 4.2.5 is green; 4.2.5 is not in `ConversionReady` because the refusal makes the gap safe.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Technical data bytes are untouched by a v2 sheet save.
  - *Given* a model fixture with 2 zones (`sheetRef.zones`), 3 named dimensions, a sum constraint and scale on sheet `s1`, *When* `TechnicalData` read-modify-write helpers run with no change requested, *Then* the model's `zones`, `dimensions[]` and scale bytes are byte-identical.
- Layer mirror.
  - *Given* a v2 sheet with layers `Base` and `Walls`, *When* saved, *Then* the model's `sheetRef.layers == ["Base","Walls"]` in the same model write.
- Refusal predicate (pure unit test over `ConversionPolicy`).
  - *Given* (zones empty, dimensions empty) -> no refusal; (sheet zones non-empty) -> `HasTechnicalData`; (sheet zones empty, project dimensions non-empty) -> `HasTechnicalData` for every sheet of that project; (after 4.2.5 policy flip) -> never.
(The end-to-end refusal and verbatim-carry goldens need the converter, router and fusion wiring; they are Tasks 1.4.2c and 1.4.3c, which run after those stories exist.)
**Files**: `CM/projectbridge/TechnicalData.kt` (new, thin read/write helpers over the model), `RT/convert/ConversionPolicy.kt`, `BT/projectbridge/TechnicalDataTest.kt`, `BT/sketch/v2/ConversionPolicyTest.kt`, `kmp/src/businessTest/resources/sketch/technical/*`
##### Task 1.1.4a: `TechnicalData` accessors + layer-mirror write + `ConversionPolicy.refusal` predicate (M)

#### Story 1.1.5: `SheetCodecV2` (pure) with decode limits (needs 1.1.2, 1.2.1, 1.2.3, 1.2.4)
**As a** maintainer, **I want** the v2 sheet reader/writer to exist, be bounded, and be tested before anything consumes it, **so that** fusion projection (1.4.5) and conversion (1.4.2) build on one tested codec. Native sheets arrive via git sync from other devices, so decode is untrusted input.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Round trip and canonical bytes.
  - *Given* a `SketchDocumentV2` with layers, constraints, `extras` and 4 element kinds (including `PointElement` and `ArcElement` when OD-8 is accepted), *When* encoded by `SheetCodecV2` and decoded, *Then* the document equals the input and re-encoding yields identical bytes (canonical JSON, Story 1.1.2); the first bytes of the output are `{"schema":2,"sheetUuid":"` (header-first, ADR-001 item 5), so `"schema"` starts within the first 64 bytes of a REAL emitted sheet of 100 elements, and a fully sorted emission of the same sheet would not (`SheetCodecV2Test.encode_should_PutSchemaWithinTheFirst64Bytes_OfARealSheet`).
- Element-local diffs (pre-mortem P2 #6).
  - *Given* a sheet of 100 elements where one element's `x` changes, *When* re-encoded, *Then* exactly one line of the output differs (the encoder emits one element per line with stable key order, so git diffs and merges are element-local) and the bytes still parse as canonical JSON.
- Decode is bounded and total.
  - *Given* truncated, deeply nested (> `SceneLimits.maxJsonDepth`), oversized (> `maxSheetBytes`, extras bytes included), over-count (5,001 elements, 201 constraints, 2,001 points) and NaN-bearing sheet bytes, *When* decoded, *Then* the result is `Either.Left(SheetError.*)` for each, nothing throws, and no limit is exceeded in memory; a valid sheet is passed through `reconcile` (Story 1.2.3) before being returned.
- The two commonTest classes reach the businessTest registration (Task 1.1.5b). *Given* `ConversionReadyConsistencyTest` (Story 1.4.3) names `SheetCodecV2Test` and `SheetCodecV2FuzzTest`, *Then* each is in the `srcs` of `business_common_tests` and registered in `AllBusinessTests`, or `ConversionReady` is false; `check-plan.py` fails when a commonTest class of that list is named by no registration task.
**Files**: `V2/codec/SheetCodecV2.kt` (pure), `CT/sketch/v2/SheetCodecV2Test.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/SheetCodecV2FuzzTest.kt`, `kmp/src/commonTest/kotlin/BUILD.bazel` and `BT/AllBusinessTests.kt` (Task 1.1.5b registers both tests)
##### Task 1.1.5a: `SheetCodecV2` (canonical JSON, `extras`, constraints/layers fields); decode applies `SceneLimits` (size, depth, elements, constraints, points), then `reconcile`; returns `Either<SheetError,...>`; fuzz test over truncated, deeply nested, oversized and NaN-bearing sheets that never throws or exceeds the limits (M)
##### Task 1.1.5b: Bazel target and registration of `SheetCodecV2Test` and `SheetCodecV2FuzzTest` (the two commonTest classes named by the `ConversionReadyConsistencyTest` list): add `dev/stapler/stelekit/sketch/v2/SheetCodecV2Test.kt` and `dev/stapler/stelekit/sketch/v2/SheetCodecV2FuzzTest.kt` to the `srcs` of `business_common_tests` in `kmp/src/commonTest/kotlin/BUILD.bazel` (same target as `BannerCandidateTest`, Task 3.6.4f, which edits the same file) and register both classes in `AllBusinessTests` (XS); after Task 1.1.5a and before Task 1.4.3c; 2 files
##### Task 1.1.5c: `SheetCodec.classifyHeader(prefix): HeaderVerdict` (V2, V1 or Undecided) with the constants `SCHEMA_WINDOW` = 64 and `HEADER_PROBE_BYTES` = 256, and `HeaderProbeGoldenTest` (BT) over the REAL bytes of a v1 sheet (`SidecarCodec`) and of a 100-element v2 sheet (`SheetCodecV2`), registered in `AllBusinessTests` (S); after Task 1.1.5a and before Task 1.4.3i (D-105, D-116)

### Epic 1.2: Scene model v2 and the v1 bridge
**Goal**: The immutable v2 model, exact lift, honest projection, and invariants.
**Execution order inside the epic** (the story numbers are kept; the order is not the number order): Task 1.2.4a (`SceneLimits`, defined FIRST because 1.2.1 capacity, `SceneUnit` bounds, 1.2.2 clamps and 1.2.3 limits all read it) ► Story 1.2.1 ► Stories 1.2.2 and 1.2.3 in parallel. Story 1.2.4's scale addendum rides with Task 1.2.4a.

#### Story 1.2.1: Element model and scene container
**As a** developer, **I want** the sealed element hierarchy and `SketchSceneV2`, **so that** all later features share one model.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Every element kind constructs with finite values only and scene capacity is enforced.
  - *Given* a `SketchSceneV2` holding 5,000 elements, *When* `add(RectElement(...))` is attempted, *Then* the result is `Either.Left(CommandError.CapacityExceeded(5000))`.
- Non-finite values are unrepresentable.
  - *Given* `SceneUnit(Double.NaN)`, *Then* construction fails with `Either.Left(NonFinite)` (parse-don't-validate factory).
- Constraint geometry has scene elements (consistency B1, OD-8).
  - *Given* `EllipseElement(rx=10, ry=10)` and `EllipseElement(rx=10, ry=6)`, *When* `isCircle` is read, *Then* the first is true and the second is false; *Given* a `PointElement(x, y)` and an `ArcElement(center, radius, startAngle, endAngle)`, *Then* both construct only with finite values and a positive radius, each carries `ElementBase`, and the `when` over `SceneElement` stays exhaustive. Only circles, arcs, points, lines, rects and polylines are constraint targets (Task 4.2.1b); an ellipse with `rx != ry` is not.
**Files**: `V2/scene/SceneElement.kt`, `V2/scene/ElementBase.kt`, `V2/scene/SketchSceneV2.kt`, `V2/scene/Ids.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/SceneModelTest.kt`
##### Task 1.2.1a: Newtypes (`SceneUnit`, `ElementId`, `ZKey`, `GroupId`, `LayerId`) + `ElementStyle` (S)
##### Task 1.2.1b: Sealed `SceneElement` kinds with `extras: JsonObject`, including `PointElement`, `ArcElement` and the `EllipseElement.isCircle` rule (OD-8) (M)
##### Task 1.2.1c: `SketchSceneV2` (map + order + layers), structure chosen from Spike 0.1.6, capacity 5,000 (S)
##### Task 1.2.1d: `SketchV2EngineImportDirectionTest` (BT): packages under `V2` (scene, commands, tools, bridge, hit, index, render, text, binding, snap, codec, export, library, clipboard) import nothing from `ui`, `db`, `repository`, `projectbridge`, `merge`, `platform`, and declare no `CoroutineScope`/`launch`; everything that does lives in `RT` (XS)

#### Story 1.2.2: Lossless lift, partial projection
**As a** developer, **I want** `V1ToV2.lift` and `V2ToV1.project`, **so that** fusion and v1 exports keep working.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Lift then project is identity for integer sixteenths.
  - *Given* `SketchPrimitive.Line(x1=16, y1=32, x2=48, y2=32, layer="Walls")`, *When* lifted to `LineElement` and projected, *Then* the result is `Projected` and equals the original including `layer` and `z`.
- Non-representable elements are dropped with a reason.
  - *Given* a `RectElement` rotated 0.3 rad, an `EllipseElement`, a `PointElement` and an `ArcElement`, *When* projected, *Then* they return `Dropped(Rotated)` / `Dropped(UnsupportedKind)` (the last three) and the count is reported.
**Files**: `V2/bridge/V1ToV2.kt`, `V2/bridge/V2ToV1.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/BridgePropertyTest.kt`
##### Task 1.2.2a: lift + projection with typed `ProjectionResult` (S)
##### Task 1.2.2b: kotest-property identity test over `Arb.long(-2^28..2^28)` and clamp/limit cases (`TooManyLayers`, 10,000 cap) (S)

#### Story 1.2.3: Scene invariants (`reconcile`)
**As a** developer, **I want** one invariant pass after every command and import, **so that** dangling bindings/groups/frames cannot exist.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Dangling references are removed with reason codes.
  - *Given* `ArrowElement a1` with `startBinding.elementId = "gone"` and `RectElement r1` listing `boundElements=[a1, a9]` where `a9` does not exist, *When* `reconcile` runs, *Then* `a1.startBinding == null`, `r1.boundElements == [a1]`, and two reason codes are returned.
- Cycles rejected.
  - *Given* `FrameElement f1` with `frameId = f1`, *When* reconciled, *Then* `frameId` is cleared and `CyclicFrame` is reported.
**Files**: `V2/scene/Reconcile.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/ReconcileTest.kt`
##### Task 1.2.3a: reconcile for bindings, boundElements reciprocity, groupIds, frameId, containerId (M)
##### Task 1.2.3b: property test: random scene mutations (a test-local generator over `SketchSceneV2`; the command-sequence variant lives in `HistoryV2PropertyTest`, Story 1.3.2, W5, because commands do not exist in W4) leave the invariant true (M)

#### Story 1.2.4: Scale, bounds and `SceneLimits` (ADR-002 addendum; BUILT FIRST in Epic 1.2, number kept)
**As a** maintainer, **I want** one stated px/unit scale and one set of coordinate/size bounds for every boundary, **so that** import, export, hit-testing and the 200-constraint/5,000-element caps cannot drift.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Scale is 1:1 and recorded in ADR-002.
  - *Given* an Excalidraw rect with `x=100, width=100`, *When* imported, *Then* the `RectElement` has `x=100, width=100` units (6.25 in), and exported again has `x=100, width=100`; the doc states this is intentional (Excalidraw px are not physical).
- Hit and snap tolerances are defined in screen px / zoom (Story 1.3.3 uses `px / zoom`, never raw scene units).
- Native bounds are one shared constant set, INCLUDING every cap named elsewhere in the plan and UX (consistency C6). There is exactly ONE canonical field list; every other story, test and document cites fields by these names and never restates a value.
  - **Canonical `SceneLimits` (32 fields; defaults; owner-confirmable D-5, D-7, D-31, D-47)**: `maxElements=5000`, `maxConstraints=200`, `maxCoord=2^28`, `maxPointsPerElement=2000`, `maxJsonDepth=64`, `maxSheetBytes=16 MB`, `maxTextChars=10_000`, `maxNameChars=80`, `maxImportBytes=20 MB`, `maxImportSpanBytes=2 MB` (the largest JSON span the importer decodes in ONE call; Story 5.1.1, Task 5.1.1e), `maxImageBytes=60 MB` (compressed file bytes), `maxImageSidePx=8192`, `maxImagePixels=16_000_000` (replaces the former `maxImageMegapixels`, same 16 MP), `maxDecodedImageBytesPerSheet=64 MB`, `maxParseMillis=5000`, `maxLibraryItems=200`, `maxLibraryBytes=16 MB` (the stored library, summed over its items; each item is one import span of at most `maxImportSpanBytes`, E1), `maxLayers=64`, `maxLayerIdChars=64`, `softElementThreshold=4000`, `softConstraintThreshold=160`, `maxPngSidePx=16_000`, `slowSolveIndicatorMs=100`, `maxHistoryEntries=100`, `maxSemanticsNodes=200`, `maxDiagnosticsEntries=20`, `maxVisibleNotices=3`, `maxGoToResults=50`, `maxConflictCopiesPerSheet=10`, `minZoomPercent=1`, `maxZoomPercent=3000`, `maxPreloadBytes=128 MB` (the sum of the file sizes under the sidecar root that the wasm preload may read; over it the status is `Unavailable(PreloadTooLarge)`, Task 1.4.7j, D-131).
  - *Given* that list, *When* `SheetCodecV2.decode`, the importer (Story 5.1.1 uses `maxImportBytes`, `maxImportSpanBytes`, `maxImageSidePx`, `maxImagePixels`, `maxDecodedImageBytesPerSheet`, `maxParseMillis`), the clipboard, the text editor, the image tool (Story 3.4.3 uses `maxImageBytes`, `maxImagePixels`), the library, the layer commands, the capacity banner, the PNG exporter, the solve runner, history, semantics, diagnostics, the notice queue, Go to element, `RetentionPolicy` and the zoom clamp check bounds, *Then* all read `SceneLimits` (a test greps the `V2`, `RT`, `XCAL`, `SOLV`, `UI2` and `CM/projectbridge` sources for numeric literals used as caps, i.e. a literal on the right of a comparison, a `take(`, `coerceAtMost`, `limit`/`max`/`cap`-named constant or a size/count check, and fails on any not in `SceneLimits`; non-cap numerics (hit tolerance px, snap radius, paste offset 10, nudge units, Ramer-Douglas-Peucker epsilon, animation durations) are listed with a reason in `scene-limits-allowlist.txt` that may only shrink; there is no separate `ImportLimits` file).
**Files**: `V2/scene/SceneLimits.kt`, `ADR-002` (addendum), `CT/sketch/v2/SceneLimitsTest.kt`, `scene-limits-allowlist.txt` (non-cap numerics, may only shrink)
##### Task 1.2.4a: `SceneLimits` with the 32-field canonical list + ADR-002 scale addendum + the grep test and allow-list (S); the FIRST task of Epic 1.2, nothing in 1.2.1-1.2.3 starts before it

### Epic 1.3: Commands, history, hit-testing
**Goal**: Pure editing engine with transactions and a spatial index.

#### Story 1.3.1: `Command`, `Transaction`, `HistoryV2`
**As a** user, **I want** each gesture to be one undo step, **so that** undo is predictable (including solver-moved geometry).
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Undo-all restores the initial scene exactly; redo-all restores the final scene.
  - *Given* a scene S0 and 50 random commands, *When* all are undone then redone, *Then* the scene equals S0 after undo and equals S50 after redo (structural equality).
- Bounded history evicts whole entries.
  - *Given* 120 committed transactions, *When* history is queried, *Then* 100 entries remain and the oldest 20 are gone as whole transactions.
**Files**: `V2/commands/Command.kt`, `V2/commands/HistoryV2.kt`, `V2/commands/CommandError.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/HistoryV2PropertyTest.kt`
##### Task 1.3.1a: Interfaces + `Transaction` that records patches (M)
##### Task 1.3.1b: `HistoryV2` + property tests (S)

#### Story 1.3.2: Core command set
**As a** user, **I want** add/move/resize/rotate/delete/style/z-order/group/ungroup/frame/lock commands, **so that** every tool composes from them.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Delete of a bound target unbinds arrows in the same step.
  - *Given* `ArrowElement a1` bound to `RectElement r1` end, *When* `DeleteElements([r1])` commits, *Then* `a1.endBinding == null` at its last position and one `undo()` restores both `r1` and the binding.
- Duplicate remaps ids.
  - *Given* `r1` and `a1` bound to it, *When* both are duplicated together, *Then* the copies' `a1'.endBinding.elementId == r1'.id` and no id collides.
**Files**: `V2/commands/ElementCommands.kt`, `V2/commands/GroupFrameCommands.kt`, `V2/commands/LayerCommands.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/ElementCommandsTest.kt`
##### Task 1.3.2a: add/move/resize/rotate/delete/style (M)
##### Task 1.3.2b: z-order (ZKey generation), lock, group/ungroup, frame membership (M)
##### Task 1.3.2c: layer commands; duplicate/paste id remap (S)

#### Story 1.3.3: Spatial index and hit-testing
**As a** user, **I want** accurate, fast hits on any element, **so that** selection and snapping feel right at 5,000 elements.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Index query equals brute force.
  - *Given* a random scene of 5,000 elements and a random move/rotate/delete sequence, *When* any viewport rect is queried, *Then* the indexed id set equals the brute-force id set.
- Hit rules.
  - *Given* a hollow `RectElement` (no fill) at zoom 1.0, *When* a point inside (10 px from edge) is tested, *Then* it misses; at the stroke edge within 6 px/zoom it hits; at zoom 0.1 the tolerance is 60 scene units (6 px / 0.1; scene units are 1/16 in, see Story 1.2.4); hidden/locked-layer elements are skipped; the topmost by z wins.
**Files**: `V2/index/SpatialIndex.kt`, `V2/hit/HitTester.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/SpatialIndexPropertyTest.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/HitTesterTest.kt`
##### Task 1.3.3a: Index (grid or R-tree per Spike 0.1.6) incremental from `changedIds` (M)
##### Task 1.3.3b: Narrow-phase per kind (rotated rect, ellipse, bezier/polyline distance, text bounds, frame interior rule, point = tolerance disc, arc = distance to the arc segment, OD-8) (M)

### Epic 1.4: Persistence, flag, coexistence
**Goal**: v2 sheets persist safely; v1 untouched; one owner per sheet.

#### Story 1.4.1: `schema_version` column and migration (no element/constraint cache tables)
**As a** maintainer, **I want** the sheet schema mirrored in the DB on existing databases, **so that** the router, dispatcher and reconciler know a sheet's schema without parsing JSON.
**Decision (cache tables dropped)**: `sketch_elements` and `sketch_constraints` are NOT created. The file is authoritative (ADR-001); the scene loads whole (max 5,000 elements, 200 constraints) into memory with an in-memory `SpatialIndex`; nothing in the plan reads per-element rows (no search, fusion, thumbnail or cold-start path: fusion reads the in-memory projection, thumbnails build from the loaded scene). Keeping them would add write amplification (up to 5,000 rows through `DatabaseWriteActor` per autosave) and a second source of truth. Reintroduce only with a named reader and diff-based writes keyed on `(id, version)`.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- The column exists on a pre-existing database.
  - *Given* a database fixture created by the current release, *When* `MigrationRunner.applyAll()` runs, *Then* `sketch_sheets.schema_version` exists and is 1 for existing rows, no `sketch_elements`/`sketch_constraints` table exists, and re-running changes nothing.
- The column is the DB mirror, file wins.
  - *Given* a DB row with `schema_version=1` and a disk sheet `schema:2`, *When* the reconciler runs, *Then* the row is updated to 2 and the disk file is untouched.
- No sheet-row write path can reset the mirror (pre-mortem P1 #1; VERIFIED hazard: `insertSketchSheet` is `INSERT OR REPLACE` without the column, `SteleDatabase.sq:1503-1505`, so any call resets the DEFAULT to 1).
  - *Given* a sheet row with `schema_version = 2`, *When* EACH of these runs: `insertSketchSheet` (via the `RestrictedDatabaseQueries` stub and via `SqlDelightSketchRepositories.put(SketchSheet)`), both `replaceProjectContents` implementations (SqlDelight and InMemory), a layer/zones/position edit through the sketch repository, a project-level dimension edit save and the reconciler's mirror update, *Then* the row still reads `schema_version = 2` afterwards (one parameterised `SketchSheetWritePathsPreserveSchemaTest` over every write path, enumerated by grep of `sketch_sheets` writers in Task 1.4.1b; a new writer added later fails the test unless listed), and `SketchStubGatingTest`'s direct `insertSketchSheet` calls are updated to pass the column.
- The file wins over the mirror for refusal decisions.
  - *Given* a DB row with `schema_version = 1` (mirror reset or stale) and a referenced `sheets/<uuid>.v2.json` of `schema: 2`, *When* the router and the legacy `SketchStore` decide whether the sheet is editable, *Then* both read the FILE's schema (via `SheetCodec` classification) and refuse the legacy editor; the mirror is used only as a cheap hint for embeds and is then repaired by the reconciler.
**Files**: `kmp/src/commonMain/sqldelight/.../SteleDatabase.sq` (`insertSketchSheet` gains `schema_version`), `CM/db/MigrationRunner.kt`, `CM/db/RestrictedDatabaseQueries.kt` (`insertSketchSheet` stub + the schema-version update stub), `CM/repository/SqlDelightSketchRepositories.kt` (`put(SketchSheet)`, `replaceProjectContents`), `CM/repository/InMemorySketchRepositories.kt` (`replaceProjectContents`), `kmp/src/generated/sqldelight/**` (regenerated), `BT/db/SketchV2MigrationTest.kt`, `BT/db/SketchSheetWritePathsPreserveSchemaTest.kt` (new), `BT/db/SketchStubGatingTest.kt` (existing, updated)
##### Task 1.4.1a: `.sq` + `MigrationRunner` hash-guarded step; read the existing ALTER pattern first; satisfy `MigrationRunnerSchemaSyncTest` (S)
##### Task 1.4.1b: `schema_version` carried by `insertSketchSheet`, its `@DirectSqlWrite` stub, `put(SketchSheet)` and both `replaceProjectContents`; the schema-version update stub; write through `DatabaseWriteActor`; Either errors; grep-enumerate every `sketch_sheets` writer into the preservation test (S)
##### Task 1.4.1c: Regenerate (`./gradlew :kmp:generateCommonMainSteleDatabase` + rsync), commit; real-DB-fixture test; extend `UpgradeResilienceTest` (S)
##### Task 1.4.1d: `SketchSheetWritePathsPreserveSchemaTest` over every sheet-row write path, plus the file-schema-wins refusal check shared with Story 1.4.3 (S)

#### Story 1.4.2: On-demand v1-to-v2 conversion with backup (runs only on the explicit Upgrade action) (needs 1.1.3, 1.1.4, 1.1.5, 1.4.1, 1.4.5, 0.1.1b, OD-1)
**As a** user, **I want** my v1 sheets to open in v2 without loss, **so that** nothing breaks.
**Mechanism (single, chosen; BLOCKER B)**: the v1 file stays the valid, referenced sheet until ONE model write flips the reference. Verified constraints it respects: `sheetPath(uuid)` is `sheets/<uuid>.json`; `decodeUnsafe` returns `Corrupt(Truncated)` when `sha256(sheet bytes) != model.fileSha256`; `writeLocked` writes sheets first and the model last; a corrupt disk bundle is `KeepCorrupt` and never auto-repaired. Therefore the v2 sheet is written to a DISTINCT path, never over the v1 file. The path, `sheetRef.file`/`schema`/`fileSha256` fields and the `SheetPaths` rule are defined once, in Story 1.1.3; this story only uses them (`SheetPaths.pathFor(uuid, 2)` = `sheets/<uuid>.v2.json`). The converter holds the per-slug `SidecarSlugLock` (Story 1.1.3) across S1-S4 and writes through a store call `writeNow` that returns `Deferred` when git sync is busy WITHOUT parking anything in `pending[slug]` (today's `write` parks the whole bundle map last-wins and `flushPending` replays it later, which could overwrite a newer snapshot or the S4 flip). On `Deferred` at any step the converter aborts (the partial state is one of the crash-safe states below), returns `ConversionOutcome.Deferred`, and is retried on the next trigger (next open of the sheet, or the next git-sync-idle notification); it never loops. Steps, each a crash boundary:
1. S1 write `backup/<uuid>.v1.json` (Task 0.1.1b `writeBackup`, read back and sha-verified).
2. S2 write `sheets/<uuid>.v2.json` (temp + rename; canonical JSON).
3. S3 read S2 back, verify sha and decode (no model change yet).
4. S4 ONE atomic model write: `sheetRef.file = sheets/<uuid>.v2.json`, `schema = 2`, `fileSha256 = sha256(v2 bytes)`, layer-name mirror (Story 1.1.4).
5. S5 delete the now-unreferenced `sheets/<uuid>.json` (`writeLocked` unreferenced cleanup); update DB `schema_version = 2`.
Old builds: after S2 they see a `schema:2` file in `sheets/` and fail closed (`Corrupt(NewerSchema)`), which is the intended safe direction and is recoverable by any new build; this window exists only between S2 and S5 and is the same blast radius ADR-001 already accepts (OD-1). The model's `schema` stays 1. A staged `sheets/<uuid>.v2.json` is NOT touched by an intervening `writeLocked` (its sweep deletes only schema-1 files, Task 0.1.1b; pre-mortem P1 #1), and a parked project-level write cannot replay over it (Story 1.1.3, `flushPending` re-validation); the retry redoes S2 with temp+rename, which is idempotent, and an abandoned staged file is removed by the converter's cleanup (`deleteV2Sheet`) or restore.
**Gate (review R6)**: the converter itself refuses with `ConversionOutcome.NotReady` (nothing written, no backup) when `ConversionReady` is false, so the guarantee does not rest on the router alone. `ConversionPolicy.refusal` (Story 1.1.4) is evaluated first and refuses with `HasTechnicalData` before any write.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Conversion is atomic and idempotent.
  - *Given* a v1 sheet `s1` with 12 primitives and flag on, *When* the Upgrade action runs twice (merely opening `s1` runs nothing), *Then* `backup/s1.v1.json` exists once with the original bytes, the model references `sheets/s1.v2.json` (`schema:2`, 12 elements carrying the same ids), `sheets/s1.json` is gone, and the second open changes no file.
- Crash injection at every step boundary loses nothing and never yields `Corrupt`; `Deferred` at every step is equally safe.
  - *Given* a `ConversionCrashPoint` seam in `SheetConverter` that throws after each of S1, S2, S3, S4 (before S5) and in the middle of each file write (a truncated temp file), and a store fake that returns `Deferred` (git sync busy) at each of S1, S2, S3-read-back and S4 (the matrix is crash x {S1..S5, mid-write} plus Deferred x {S1, S2, S4}; `pending` stays empty in every Deferred case, and a later successful retry completes the conversion identically), *When* the app reopens after each injected crash, *Then*: after S1, S2 and S3 the project reads `Present` with the v1 sheet referenced and unchanged, an unreferenced `sheets/s1.v2.json` (if present) is ignored, and re-conversion completes; after S4 the project reads `Present` with the v2 sheet referenced and the leftover v1 file is removed by the next write; a truncated temp file is never renamed into place; in no case is the result `Corrupt(Truncated)` and the final converted state is identical to an uninterrupted conversion.
- `backup/` is never scanned as a sheet and survives later writes.
  - *Given* a converted project, *When* any later `writeLocked` runs, *Then* `backup/s1.v1.json` is byte-identical and absent from `files` and `bundleHash`.
- Golden: every shipped v1 fixture converts and exports unchanged.
  - *Given* each golden v1 fixture without technical data, *When* converted and exported through the in-memory `V2ToV1.project` path with the v1 SVG/CSV writers, *Then* output bytes equal the pre-conversion exports.
- Technical data is carried verbatim, proved by OUTPUT equality (Task 1.4.2c; dimensions have no primitive reference, so "ids resolve" is not the criterion).
  - *Given* the Story 1.1.4 fixture (2 zones, 3 named dimensions, a sum constraint) converted with the refusal lifted through a test-only `ConversionPolicy` override, *When* fusion, CSV and digest run on the converted project, *Then* the model's `zones`, `dimensions[]` and scale bytes are unchanged and the fusion/CSV/digest outputs equal the pre-conversion outputs byte for byte; and with the refusal active the converter returns `Failed(HasTechnicalData)`, writes nothing and creates no backup.
- The converter refuses while `ConversionReady` is false.
  - *Given* `ConversionReady = false`, *When* conversion is requested for a v1 sheet without technical data, *Then* the outcome is `NotReady`, no file changes and no backup exists.
- OD-1 hook: the converter returns `RequiresConfirmation` for the first v2 write in a sketch PROJECT (no (device, project slug) acknowledgement yet, D-16) and writes nothing until the router (Story 1.4.3) confirms through the dialog of Story 1.4.6; no UI code lives in this story.
**Files**: `RT/convert/SheetConverter.kt`, `RT/convert/ConversionPolicy.kt` (predicate from Story 1.1.4), `CM/projectbridge/ProjectSidecarStore.kt` (`writeNow`), `BT/sketch/v2/SheetConversionGoldenTest.kt`, `BT/sketch/v2/SheetConversionCrashInjectionTest.kt`, `BT/sketch/v2/TechnicalDataConversionGoldenTest.kt`, `kmp/src/businessTest/resources/sketch/technical/*` (shared with Story 1.1.4). `SheetCodecV2` is Story 1.1.5; the `sheetRef.file/schema/fileSha256` fields are Story 1.1.3.
##### Task 1.4.2a: `SheetConverter` with the S1-S5 protocol, `SidecarSlugLock` across S1-S4, `writeNow` (Deferred without queueing), `ConversionCrashPoint` seam, `NotReady` refusal (M)
##### Task 1.4.2b: Reconcile three-way hashes against the `sheetRef.file` reference on schema:2; `NewerSchema` read-only path; `RequiresConfirmation` result for OD-1 (M)
##### Task 1.4.2c: Technical-data golden (verbatim carry through fusion, CSV, digest output equality; refusal writes nothing) over the Story 1.1.4 fixtures (M)

#### Story 1.4.3: Flag, router, lease
**As a** user, **I want** the new editor behind a flag with a safe fallback, **so that** I can roll back.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Router matrix.
  - *Given* (mode, schema, conversionState) of (Off,1,any) and (NewProjectsOnly,1,any)->legacy editable with NO chip (conversion is unreachable in both), (On,1,NotConverted)->legacy editable WITH an `UpgradeOffer` unless `ConversionPolicy.refusal` is non-null, in which case NO chip and a disabled sketch-menu entry with the reason (D-98) (merely opening never converts; conversion runs only on the explicit Upgrade action, which shows the first-write dialog until this sketch project's acknowledgement exists; Story 1.4.6, Task 1.4.6g, UX-104), (On,1,Upgrade requested)->v2 after confirmation and conversion, (On,1,Declined)->legacy editable with NO chip ("Keep this sketch classic" in the chip menu of UX 14.2; `Declined` is remembered PER SHEET in `Settings`, is NOT re-offered at open, and is offered again only from the sheet menu "Upgrade to the new editor"; consistency B4, ux.md 14.2, UX-64 and UX-104 are aligned to this), (On,1,Failed(reason))->legacy editable with the reason and "Open in legacy editor" (UX 14.4; includes `HasTechnicalData` before Story 4.2.5), (Off,2)->v2 read-only with reason "Turn on the new editor to edit." (UX-67; pan, zoom, select and export still work; only where `V2StorageAvailable` is true, see the last row of this list), (NewProjectsOnly or On,2)->v2 editable, (any,NewerSchema)->"Update SteleKit to view" (UX-68; nothing is ever written from this state), (any mode, schema 2) while `V2StorageAvailable` is false (iOS, a browser whose OPFS verdict is Unavailable, or no sidecar store)->no editor, reason `V2StorageUnavailable` with its recovery actions (the sheet file cannot be read there; this row is evaluated BEFORE every other schema-2 row, so `(Off,2)` on such a platform is this row and never the read-only banner; UX-133, UX-134, UX-136, D-104, D-117).
- **NewSketchProject (canonical sentence):** A `NewSketchProject` is a project created by the very create action that adds the sketch, or an existing project that holds at least one sheet, every sheet of which classifies as v2 from its FILE (the database schema mirror is only a hint) and whose sheet list is complete (the sheet query reports no further page); an existing project with no sheets, with any v1 or unclassifiable sheet, or with more sheets than one query page does not qualify, because an older build opens it today.
- Mode matrix and the kind of a NEW sheet (P1; Task 1.4.3d; ADR-005 item 1a). *Given* the effective mode and the project kind (`NewSketchProject`, the canonical sentence above, so no project an older build opens today becomes unreadable under `NewProjectsOnly`; the user-facing reasons are the `ClassicEditor` reasons of ux.md 14.1. `SheetSplitter` (Stories 2.2.4 and 5.1.5) never consults this rule: it is reachable only from an editable v2 sheet, whose project already needs a v2-capable reader, so the sheet it creates is v2 in every mode that lets the source be edited), *When* the user creates a sketch, *Then* the sheet is v2 exactly when (mode = NewProjectsOnly and the project is a `NewSketchProject`) or (mode = On and `HasTechnicalData` does not refuse), after the first-write dialog's new-sheet variant where the project has no acknowledgement; in every other cell it is a classic v1 sheet and the `ClassicEditor` toast names the reason ("Created with the classic editor because <reason>"). *Given* mode `NewProjectsOnly`, *Then* no Upgrade chip is emitted for any v1 sheet, `SheetConverter` is never reachable from the UI and a v1 sheet is never written as v2. The predicate reads the project's sheets through `SketchSheetRepository.getSheets`, which is BOUNDED (default 50, `hasMore`), and classifies each sheet through `SheetCodec` on its FILE (the `schema_version` mirror is only a hint): a project whose page reports `hasMore`, or any sheet whose file does not classify as v2 (probed by header, see the next bullet), does not qualify. `FlagModeMatrixTest` runs every cell of modes x {`ConversionReady`, `ConversionOffered`, `V2StorageAvailable` (cells: desktop true, android true, wasmJs with an Available OPFS verdict true, wasmJs with an Unavailable verdict false, iOS false, store null false; the iOS cell is a fake-store cell that proves the logic on the other targets, iOS itself is compile-only and runs no test)} x project kind {created by this action, existing-empty, all-v2, mixed, v1-only, all-v2 with more than 50 sheets (`hasMore`), mirror-says-2-but-file-says-1 and the reverse, unclassifiable file} x schema and asserts the cap: stored `On` with `ConversionOffered = false` behaves as `NewProjectsOnly`, any stored mode with `ConversionReady = false` or `V2StorageAvailable = false` behaves as `Off`, and a capped option is reported disabled with the reason "Not available in this build yet" (or, where the platform cannot store files, `V2StorageUnavailable`).
- Cost bound of the `NewSketchProject` predicate (engineering E1-E4; file reads where `V2StorageAvailable` is true: desktop, Android and an OPFS-capable browser; D-91, D-95, D-104, D-105, D-106). The predicate runs only where `V2StorageAvailable` is true (desktop, Android and a browser whose OPFS verdict is Available; on iOS, in a browser whose verdict is Unavailable and with no store every mode is capped at `Off` and the predicate is never called, see the platform-capability bullet below). On wasm every read it makes is a SYNCHRONOUS read of the preloaded cache (`readModel` one entry, `readFilePrefix` a slice of at most 256 bytes of one entry, Task 1.4.7b), so there is no OPFS await inside the predicate; the async OPFS reads, and their yield to the event loop between files, are in `preload` (one `file.text()` or `arrayBuffer()` await per file, Story 1.4.7). *Given* a project with at most 50 sheets (the `getSheets` page, `SketchSheetRepository.DEFAULT_SHEET_LIMIT`; `HEADER_PROBE_BYTES` = 256), *When* the predicate runs for a create action, *Then* it reads the model through the new model-only read `ProjectSidecarStore.readModel(slug)` (Task 1.4.3h: ONE file, `project-model.json`, opened once, no `sheets/` listing and no sheet file; the existing `read` cannot be used because `FileProjectSidecarStore.readLocked` lists and reads EVERY sheet file in full, up to 500) to resolve each sheet's `sheetRef` to a path (`sheets/<uuid>.v2.json` or `sheets/<uuid>.json`, `SheetPaths`) and probes the file the `sheetRef` NAMES, never the DB mirror's guess: for each sheet it calls `FileSystem.readFilePrefix(path, HEADER_PROBE_BYTES)` (Task 1.4.3g; no ranged read exists today) and `SheetCodec.classifyHeader(prefix)` (Task 1.1.5c) returns V2, V1 or Undecided. **The file key is `schema`, not `schema_version`** (`SidecarFileState.classifyJson` reads `obj["schema"]`; `schema_version` is only the DB mirror column). `classifyHeader` reads the key by position, not by search: it accepts the bytes only when `"schema"` is the FIRST key and starts within the first `SCHEMA_WINDOW` = 64 bytes, and says V2 only for the value 2 and V1 only for the value 1 (each followed by a delimiter), anything else Undecided, so a `schema` value other than 2 never says V2. A v1 file written by `SidecarCodec.sheetJson` is pretty-printed (`ProjectModelJson.kt:46,87`), so it begins `{\n  "schema": 1,` (key at byte offset 4); a v2 file begins `{"schema":2,"sheetUuid":"` (offset 1) because the v2 canonical emitter writes the header keys FIRST and sorts only the rest (ADR-001 item 5, Task 1.1.2a, D-105): a fully sorted emission puts `schema` after `constraints`, `elements`, `extras` and `layers`, beyond the 256-byte prefix of any real sheet. `check-plan.py` computes the offsets of both layouts and of a sorted emission and fails if the sorted one would fit the window (the exception must stay load-bearing); `HeaderProbeGoldenTest` (Task 1.1.5c) classifies the REAL bytes of a v1 sheet (`SidecarCodec`) and of a v2 sheet of 100 elements (`SheetCodec`), asserts V1 and V2, and asserts `"schema"` starts below `SCHEMA_WINDOW` in both. Only a V2 header decides: since any non-v2 sheet disqualifies the project, the probe STOPS at the first sheet whose verdict is not V2 (V1, Undecided, a short or invalid header, an absent file, a torn read) with NO full parse and the project does not qualify (the full `SheetCodec` classification stays the arbiter for the router, not for this predicate; `headerVerdict_should_NeverSayV2ForAFileWhoseFullClassificationIsV1` asserts it on every fixture). Bounds: one whole-file open of the model plus at most 50 sheet-file opens of at most 256 bytes each, 50 x 256 = 12,800 bytes of prefixes, reached only by an all-v2 project; a v1-only project stops after 1 open and at most 256 bytes, a mixed one after the opens up to its first non-v2 sheet, and the `hasMore` and empty cases read no sheet file. A project with 51 to 500 sheets never qualifies (the page reports `hasMore` at 50 although `SidecarReconciler.MAX_SHEETS` is 500), and a sheet file pulled by git but not yet reconciled has no DB row, so it is invisible to the predicate (that project counts as having no such sheet and is judged on the rows it has). The predicate runs once per create action (not per recomposition), shows the UX-94 progress indicator when it takes more than 200 ms, and runs on `PlatformDispatcher.IO`. Budget (INFERRED, `[tunable]`): 50 ms cold on Android at 50 sheets, 5 ms for the `hasMore` and empty cases; on wasm the same 50 sheets are cache slices read by `readFilePrefix`, and the 10 ms budget is measured on the 50 prefix reads in Task 1.4.7c's browser test (D-126; the predicate adds only its model read, which `NewSketchProjectPredicateTest` counts), and the sidecar preload of 500 sheet files is timed in the browser pass (Task 1.4.7d), not budgeted here; desktop (local disk) is not budgeted. `NewSketchProjectPredicateTest` (commonTest, Task 1.4.3i, so it also runs on wasmJs over the same counting fake) asserts the open-count and byte bounds (one model open, no `listFiles`), the stop-at-first-non-v2 count and the zero-read cases with a counting fake file system over the generated project kinds, plus the mismatch fixtures: mirror 2 with a `.json` v1 file (no), mirror 1 with a `.v2.json` v2 file (yes), `sheetRef` naming a `.v2.json` that is absent while a v1 `.json` exists (no), and both files present (the one the `sheetRef` names decides); a real Android file read cannot be timed in CI, so the cold milliseconds are recorded in the on-device list of `owner-verification.md` against the budget.
- Reason strings (read-only reason, "Update SteleKit to view") live in ONE place (`SketchReasonText`) used by the router and `SketchRenderDispatcher`.
- One lease per sheet, in a UI-free layer.
  - *Given* sheet `s1` open in the legacy editor holding a lease from `SheetLeaseRegistry` (`RT/session`, one instance per graph, constructed in the composition root and injected into both view models, the preview path and the importer), *When* the v2 editor requests `s1`, *Then* the lease is refused with `SheetBusy` and the legacy editor is unaffected; a lease is released on dispose and on scope cancel.
- Flag flip mid-session does not swap the open editor.
- The mode is per graph and follows the graph switch (`GraphManager` is single-open and `switchGraph` changes the ready graph). *Given* graph A stored `On` and graph B stored nothing, *When* `switchGraph` makes B the ready graph, *Then* `V2ModeFlag` emits `Off` for B (never A's `On`), the router for B's sheets uses `Off`, switching back re-emits `On`, and a `set` after the switch writes only B's key (`FlagModeMatrixTest.modeFlag_should_RebindOnGraphSwitch_AndNeverShowTheOtherGraphsMode`).
- The flag cannot be enabled before the prerequisites, and the constant cannot lie (review R6).
  - *Given* Stories 1.1.3, 1.1.4, 1.1.5 and 1.4.5 are not yet registered as complete (a build-time capability constant `ConversionReady`), *Then* every mode other than `Off` is disabled with the reason and the router treats the mode as `Off`; *Given* `ConversionOffered = false`, *Then* only `On` is disabled (reason "Not available in this build yet"), `NewProjectsOnly` stays available, and `SheetConverter` returns `NotReady` exactly as for `ConversionReady = false`.
  - *Given* `ConversionReady = true`, *When* `ConversionReadyConsistencyTest` runs, *Then* it fails unless ALL of these are present in the registration lists (it reads the `AllBusinessTests` source, which registers the businessTest AND commonTest classes of this list, and checks each class loads by name on the test classpath, where `business_common_tests` puts the commonTest classes; it does not read any `BUILD.bazel`, because under Bazel the businessTest sandbox receives only the `*.kt` files of `test_audit_sources` and no filegroup exports a BUILD file): Story 1.1.3 `BundleV2PassthroughTest` (BT), `ReconcilerV2GoldenTest` (BT), `SidecarWriterRaceTest` (BT), `SidecarStoreOpaqueContractTest` (BT), `ReplaceProjectContentsV2Test` (BT); Story 1.1.4 `TechnicalDataTest` (BT), `ConversionPolicyTest` (BT); Story 1.1.5 `SheetCodecV2Test` (CT), `SheetCodecV2FuzzTest` (CT); Story 1.4.5 `MixedProjectFusionGoldenTest` (BT), `SketchPreviewV2Test` (BT); Story 1.4.1 `SketchSheetWritePathsPreserveSchemaTest` (BT); Story 1.1.1 `SheetCodecDispatchTest` (BT); where (BT) is the businessTest source set and (CT) the commonTest source set, the class-to-source-set mapping lives in this list itself and `check-plan.py` fails on a class without a tag, on a tag that disagrees with the class's `BT/` or `CT/` path in this plan, and on a (CT) class that no registration task names; and `ConversionReady` is `false` in the same build when any is missing. The converter independently refuses when it is false (Story 1.4.2).
- **The platform can store v2 sheets, or the mode is capped at `Off` (E3; D-104 owner decision, D-112, D-116; UX-133).** v2 sheets are authoritative in FILES (ADR-001). Before Story 1.4.7 wasmJs and iOS cannot write them: `supportsTempRenameWrites` is false there (`wasmJsMain/.../PlatformFileSystem.kt:622`, `iosMain/.../PlatformFileSystem.kt:15`), so `AtomicFileWriter.mode` is UNSUPPORTED and `FileProjectSidecarStore.read` and every write return `Unavailable` (`ProjectSidecarStore.kt:71-76`), and neither platform overrides `readFileBytes` (its default throws). Story 1.4.7 gives wasmJs the capability per browser; iOS gets none.
  - *Given* the `sidecarStore` provider returns null (`GraphContentViewModelSetup.kt:221-227`: a demo graph, or no graph active) or `ProjectSidecarStore.isAvailable()` is false (Task 1.4.3h), *Then* `V2StorageAvailable` is false: every stored mode behaves as `Off` (the third cap beside `ConversionReady` and `ConversionOffered`, in the same capping function), no v2 sheet is created, the `NewSketchProject` predicate is never called, Epic 5 import (which creates v2 sheets) is disabled with the same reason, the mode control is disabled with `V2StorageUnavailable` (one `SketchReasonText` entry per case, visible text and accessible description: iOS "The new editor can't save sketches on iOS yet.", a browser that cannot save them one text per `Unavailable` reason and per browser family (the table "Web storage reasons" of ux.md 14.1 is the one source), no store "The new editor isn't available in the demo graph or while no graph is open."), a v2 sheet that reaches such a platform opens as no editor with that reason and its recovery actions (UX-134), and classic sketches are unaffected (verified baseline, D-140: they persist in the database on every platform; only their file bundle, exports and preview file are missing where the store is `Unavailable`). *Given* desktop or Android with a store, or wasmJs in a browser whose `V2StorageStatus` is `Ready` (an Available `OpfsStorageVerdict` and a finished preload, Task 1.4.7j), `V2StorageAvailable` is true; while that status is `Checking` it is false but the router returns `Pending`, never the no-editor screen (UX-141). `FlagModeMatrixTest` has one cell per capability case (desktop true, android true, wasmJs with an Available verdict true, wasmJs with an Unavailable verdict false, iOS false, store null false) over every mode and asserts the effective mode, the disabled reason and zero file reads by the predicate on the false cells; the cells use a fake store whose `isAvailable()` is set (or a null store), so the test runs in commonTest on every target; the iOS cell is a fake-store cell like the others and proves the logic only, because iOS itself is compile-only and runs no test. OWNER DECISION, prominent: this makes Web a v2 target (requirements.md "Web first release: what works").
- **The storage ports live in Story 1.4.3, so Story 1.4.7 can be `Skipped(opfs-spike)` without breaking the build (E1, E2, E3, E4; D-127, D-129, D-130, D-132).** The rule: a task outside `OPFSSPIKE` never compiles against the output of a parked task (`check-plan.py` computes, for every path a non-parked task lists, which task introduces it first and fails when that task is parked). Task 1.4.3k: `CM/platform/V2StorageStatus.kt` holds `V2StorageStatus` (`Checking`, `Ready(sessionOnly, privateSuspected)`, `Unavailable(reason)`), the `StorageReason` enum (the seven reasons of the web reason table of ux.md 14.1), `PersistenceState` (`Granted`, `Denied`, `NotRequested`, `Unsupported`), the `BrowserPersistence` port (`suspend fun request(): PersistenceState`, default `UnsupportedBrowserPersistence`, which answers `Unsupported`) and `PrivateWindowHint.from(estimatedQuotaBytes)` (D-136). Task 1.4.3l adds `DomainError.FileSystemError.StorageQuotaExceeded(path)` and `DomainError.FileSystemError.WriterLeaseNotHeld(path)`; `kmp/src` has exactly two exhaustive `when`s over `DomainError` (every source set, script-grepped; `toSyncErrorMessage` is over `GitError` only and the others carry an `else`): `DomainError.toUiMessage()` in `DomainError.kt` and the `val msg: String = when (err)` of `DomainErrorTest.kt:101`, a compile error when a branch is missing, so the task adds both new branches to BOTH and `DomainErrorTest` asserts both messages. Task 1.4.3m gives `FileSystem` the defaults `storageStatus` (`Ready(false, false)`), `awaitDurable(path)` (`Right`), `writeTempBytes(path, data)` (`writeFileBytes`), `deleteTempBytes(path)` (`deleteFile`) and `readFileBytesSuspend(path)` (`readFileBytes`; the on-demand read of the files the wasm preload does not hold, D-143), adds the `SketchWriterLease` port (`state` of `Held`, `HeldElsewhere` or `Acquiring`; default `AlwaysHeldSketchWriterLease`) and the interim wasm override `storageStatus = Unavailable(NoOpfs)`, so the web build is classic-only until Task 1.4.7j replaces it; the port also carries `epoch`, which grows each time the lease becomes `Held` again (D-150). Task 1.4.3n makes `SheetLeaseRegistry` consult the `SketchWriterLease` before it grants a v2 sheet's edit lease: `HeldElsewhere` is the existing `LeaseConflict` outcome with the reason id `OtherTab` (read-only, no primary action, UX-142). Task 1.4.3p is its own early node (D-146): it adds two `StelekitAppPlatformIntegrations` fields (`sketchWriterLease`, `browserPersistence`) in `StelekitAppDependencies.kt` (a composition root of every platform; `GraphContentDeps` already carries `platformIntegrations`, so the store provider reads the lease from `deps.platformIntegrations` and `App.kt` is not edited) with TEMPORARY defaults from `SketchStorageDefaults`, which constructs `AlwaysHeldSketchWriterLease` and `UnsupportedBrowserPersistence`, and adds `SketchWriterLease` and `BrowserPersistence` to `WiringAudit.SEAMS` (the audit finds implementations only of the interfaces on that list, so an interface left off it is never checked) and updates the SEAMS list assertion `WiringCompletenessTest.seamList_should_EqualGlossaryList_When_Parsed` (10 names after it, 8 after Task 2.1.2b and 12 after Task 1.4.7m; the wasm implementations are constructed by Tasks 1.4.7e and 1.4.7i, which run after Task 1.4.3t, so no wave has an unconstructed implementation of a listed interface). Task 1.4.3s makes the Android and desktop host sites pass the values explicitly and Task 1.4.3t does the same at both wasm `Main.kt` sites, deletes the defaults (the fields are then REQUIRED, the file's own convention of no defaults on purpose, so a forgotten site does not compile) and adds `SketchStorageHostSitesTest`, the scan that proves no production host overrides the values. Task 1.4.3q adds the two entry-point tests (`WiredAppFixture` exists only in `androidUnitTest` and `DesktopWiredAppFixture` only in `jvmTest`, so the tests live there, not in businessTest) and the four `wiring-catalog.txt` lines (the `plan-wiring` table of the Dependency Visualization has one row per seam implementation of Stories 1.4.3, 1.4.7 and 2.1.2). Task 1.4.3o makes `ProjectSidecarStore` take the `SketchWriterLease` as a required constructor parameter and refuse every write under the sidecar root while it is not `Held` (`write` and `writeSourceSnapshot` return `SidecarWriteOutcome.Failed(WriterLeaseNotHeld)`, `writeConflict`, the only method that returns `Either`, returns `Left(WriterLeaseNotHeld)`, and `flushPending`, which returns an `Int` count, re-checks the lease at each replay and keeps the write parked, a bundle parked under one lease epoch is never replayed under a later one but becomes a conflict copy (D-150; the `plan-lease-epoch` block lists the outcomes, `check-plan.py` recomputes them), and `writeNow` (Task 1.4.2a), `writeBackup` (`Failed`) and `writeSheetConflictCopy` (`Left`, both Task 0.1.1b, which run before the lease parameter exists in Task 1.4.3o and are tested here) follow the same rule; nothing is written; the `plan-store-scope` block is the table, D-142), changing its two construction sites. Task 1.4.3r makes `SheetSaver` turn that refusal into the read-only `LeaseConflict` state and stop its debounce, never `SaveFailed`. `V2StorageAvailable` reads `FileSystem.storageStatus` (Task 1.4.3h) and the router reads `V2StorageStatus` (Task 1.4.3d), so both consume Tasks 1.4.3k and 1.4.3m, never Story 1.4.7. Tests: `V2StorageStatusTest` (`privateWindowHint_should_BeSuspectedBelowTheQuotaThreshold`, `unsupportedPersistence_should_AnswerUnsupported`), the `DomainErrorTest` additions (`storageQuotaExceeded_should_HaveItsUiMessage`, `writerLeaseNotHeld_should_HaveItsUiMessage`), `FileSystemStoragePortsTest` (`defaults_should_BeReadyRightAndPlainWrites`, `alwaysHeldLease_should_StayHeld`), `WriterLeaseRouterTest` (`heldElsewhere_should_RouteToLeaseConflictOtherTab`, `held_should_RouteToEditable`), `WriterLeaseRefusalTest` (`writeWithoutLease_should_ReturnFailedAndWriteNothing`, `writeSourceSnapshotWithoutLease_should_ReturnFailedAndWriteNothing`, `writeConflictWithoutLease_should_ReturnLeft`, `writeNowWithoutLease_should_ReturnFailed`, `writeBackupWithoutLease_should_ReturnFailedAndCreateNoBackupDirectory`, `writeSheetConflictCopyWithoutLease_should_ReturnLeftAndWriteNoCopy`, `flushPendingAfterLeaseLoss_should_KeepWritesParked`, `flushPendingAfterLeaseReacquire_should_NotReplayStaleBundle`, `staleParkedBundle_should_BecomeConflictCopy`, `staleParkedModel_should_BecomeConflictCopy`, `modelWriteFromStaleTab_should_BeRefused`), `WriterLeaseSaverTest` (`refusal_should_BecomeReadOnlyNotSaveFailed`) `SketchStorageHostSitesTest` (Task 1.4.3t: `everyHostSite_should_NameBothStorageFields`, `noProductionHost_should_ConstructTheDefaultsItself`) and the two entry-point tests of Task 1.4.3q (`SketchStorageAndroidEntryPointTest`, `SketchStorageDesktopEntryPointTest`, effect labels `sketch-writer-lease-held-android`, `browser-persistence-unsupported-android`, `sketch-writer-lease-held-desktop`, `browser-persistence-unsupported-desktop`).
- Refusal reaches the user through the router (golden, Task 1.4.3c).
  - *Given* the Story 1.1.4 fixture project with the refusal active and flag on, *When* the v2 editor requests a v1 sheet of it, *Then* nothing is written, no backup is created, and the router returns legacy-editable with `HasTechnicalData` text from `SketchReasonText`.
- The legacy editor refuses v2 sheets at the store level too (review C5, defence in depth).
  - *Given* a sheet whose FILE is schema 2 (the DB row may say 2 or, after a mirror reset, 1), *When* the legacy `SketchViewModel` loads it through `SketchStore` (`ui/sketch/SketchStore.kt`, the load path that reads `getPrimitives`), *Then* the load returns `Either.Left(SheetIsV2)` (a typed error in the existing `DomainError` family) and the legacy view model shows the read-only reason, independently of what `SketchEditorRouter` decided; both `SketchStore` and the router classify the file through `SheetCodec` and treat the mirror only as a hint (pre-mortem P1 #1).
- UX criteria owned by this story, cited literally (each has a test row in validation.md): UX-67 flag off: a v2 sketch opens read-only with the reason (above); UX-68 a newer-than-supported schema never writes (above); UX-133 a platform without file storage caps the mode at Off with a stated reason and opens a new-editor sheet as no editor (the platform-capability bullet above); UX-136 the routing precedence: the storage-unavailable row first.
- Banner producers (engineering E2; Story 3.6.7). *Given* the router outcomes read-only (flag off, schema 2) and lease conflict, *Then* the router emits the `ReadOnly` (primary [Use new]; Details, Restore original (only where the build has Story 6.2.6 and the sheet has a backup), Export SVG, Close and Dismiss under More; Details carries "This sketch uses the new editor. Turn on the new editor to edit.") and `LeaseConflict` (primary [Open there]; Details and Close under More; for the reason id `OtherTab` the variant `LeaseConflict:OtherTab` of ux.md 14.8, summary "Edited in another tab.", NO primary action, Details and Close under More, UX-149) `BannerCandidate`s through `DiagnosticsPort` and never touches `BannerSlot`; the ux.md routing table, the read-only banner and the banner table list the same actions. [Use new] (U3; ADR-005 items 2 and 3; UX-69): *Given* the read-only banner, *When* [Use new] is activated with `ConversionReady` true, *Then* the BANNER VARIANT `SketchConsentCopy.ReadOnlyUseNew` of the flag-enable confirmation of Story 1.4.6 opens (the one way a mode is turned on; because this sketch is already a new-editor sketch the copy says turning it on reopens THIS sketch so it can be edited and that older versions of SteleKit already cannot open this project, and never the `NewProjectsOnly` sentences "Other existing sketches and projects stay classic" and "can't open a project that contains a new-editor sketch", which would be false here), on [Turn on and reopen] `NewProjectsOnly` is stored (the smallest mode that makes the sheet editable and starts no conversion) and the sketch is reopened at once by the same explicit action as the `EngineChange` toast's [Reopen] (save pending edits, close, open in the editor the router now chooses; no editor is ever swapped without an explicit activation), so the path is two steps; the `EngineChange` toast stays for a mode changed in Settings while a sketch is open; *When* Cancel, *Then* nothing is stored; *Given* `ConversionReady` false, *Then* [Use new] is disabled with "Not available in this build yet"; `ReadOnlyUseNewTest` asserts all four, and that the open editor instance is never replaced.
- Model-only read and availability (E2, E3; Task 1.4.3h; D-106). *Given* `ProjectSidecarStore.readModel(slug)` over a counting file system and a project whose model references 500 sheets, *When* it runs, *Then* exactly one `readFileBytes` of `project-model.json` happens, `listFiles` is never called and no sheet path is touched, and the result is `Present` holding only the model entry, `Absent` for a missing model and `Corrupt` for an unclassifiable one (`SidecarModelOnlyReadTest`); *Given* a file system whose `supportsTempRenameWrites` is false, *Then* `isAvailable()` is false and `readModel` returns `Unavailable`.
**Files**: `CM/projectbridge/ProjectBridgeSettings.kt` (the per-graph string accessors `v2Mode(graphId)` and `setV2Mode(graphId, mode)`; `HomeSketchFlag.kt` is not edited), `CM/projectbridge/V2Mode.kt` (new; mode, caps, `ConversionOffered`, `V2ModeFlag`), `CT/sketch/v2/FlagModeMatrixTest.kt` (new; Task 1.4.3j), `CM/projectbridge/NewSketchProject.kt` and `CT/sketch/v2/NewSketchProjectPredicateTest.kt` (new; Task 1.4.3i), `RT/session/SheetLeaseRegistry.kt` (new, UI-free; NOT in `ui.sketch.SketchStore`, which is `internal` to `ui.sketch`), `CM/ui/sketch/SketchEditorRouter.kt`, `CM/ui/sketch/SketchReasonText.kt` (carries `HasTechnicalData`), `CM/ui/sketch/SketchStore.kt` (v2 refusal), `CT/sketch/v2/EditorRouterTest.kt`, `BT/sketch/v2/SheetLeaseRegistryTest.kt`, `BT/sketch/v2/ConversionReadyConsistencyTest.kt`, `BT/sketch/v2/LegacyStoreRefusesV2Test.kt`, `BT/sketch/v2/RouterRefusalGoldenTest.kt`, `BT/projectbridge/SidecarModelOnlyReadTest.kt` (new; Task 1.4.3h), `CM/platform/V2StorageStatus.kt` and `CT/platform/V2StorageStatusTest.kt` (new; Task 1.4.3k), `CM/error/DomainError.kt` and `CT/error/DomainErrorTest.kt` (Task 1.4.3l), `CM/platform/FileSystem.kt`, `CM/platform/SketchWriterLease.kt` (new) and `BT/platform/FileSystemStoragePortsTest.kt` (new; Task 1.4.3m), `RT/session/SheetLeaseRegistry.kt` and `BT/sketch/v2/WriterLeaseRouterTest.kt` (new; Task 1.4.3n), `CM/projectbridge/ProjectSidecarStore.kt`, `CM/ui/GraphContentViewModelSetup.kt`, `BT/projectbridge/SidecarTestSupport.kt` and `BT/sketch/v2/WriterLeaseRefusalTest.kt` (new; Task 1.4.3o), `RT/persist/SheetSaver.kt` and `BT/sketch/v2/WriterLeaseSaverTest.kt` (new; Task 1.4.3r), `CM/ui/StelekitAppDependencies.kt`, `BT/wiring/WiringAudit.kt` and `BT/wiring/WiringCompletenessTest.kt` (Task 1.4.3p), `kmp/src/businessTest/resources/wiring-catalog.txt`, `AT/sketch/v2/SketchStorageAndroidEntryPointTest.kt` and `JT/sketch/v2/SketchStorageDesktopEntryPointTest.kt` (new; Task 1.4.3q)
##### Task 1.4.3a: `V2Mode` setting (Off / NewProjectsOnly / On; stored as a per-graph string next to the `ProjectBridgeSettings` per-graph accessors, read with `getString` only, unknown reads as Off, tested with a typed fake `Settings` that throws when `getBoolean` is called on that key) with an observable `V2ModeFlag` that is NOT a copy of `HomeSketchFlag` (that class caches one global `MutableStateFlow` seeded from one global `getBoolean`, which would show graph A's mode after `GraphManager.switchGraph` to graph B): `V2ModeFlag` is keyed by graph id (`ProjectBridgeSettings.v2Mode(graphId)`), its `StateFlow<V2Mode>` is derived from `GraphManager.readyGraph` (readiness comes from the ready pair, never from the registry's `activeGraphId`) and re-reads the new graph's stored string whenever it changes, `set(mode)` writes under the graph id that is ready at the call (with no graph ready it writes nothing and returns a typed `NoGraph`), and the router and Settings read it per call; the global `homeSketch` master flag stays a global Boolean and `HomeSketchFlag.kt` is not edited, with the graph-switch test of the Story 1.4.3 criteria; the `ConversionReady`, `ConversionOffered` and `V2StorageAvailable` gates and the capping function (S)
##### Task 1.4.3b: `SheetLeaseRegistry` + `SketchEditorRouter` with `ConversionState` + tests (S); the legacy `SketchViewModel` acquires its lease through the registry (a small, tested adapter, no other change to v1 behaviour); the first-write confirmation UI is NOT here, it is Story 1.4.6 (Task 1.4.6a)
