##### Task 1.4.3d: Mode caps and router matrix: the capping function over `ConversionReady`, `ConversionOffered` and `V2StorageAvailable` (a null store is false), the (mode, schema, conversionState, project kind) matrix of `SketchEditorRouter` with its precedence (storage-unavailable first, UX-136) the `Pending` outcome while `V2StorageStatus` (Task 1.4.3k) is `Checking` (U2, UX-141) and the `ClassicEditor` reason id of each classic outcome (S)
##### Task 1.4.3g: Bounded prefix read `FileSystem.readFilePrefix(path, maxBytes): ByteArray?` for JVM desktop and Android (wasmJs gets its override in Task 1.4.7b, because this task's file list is full, as a slice of its preloaded cache; iOS gets none: `V2StorageAvailable` is false on iOS, D-104). The interface default is `readFileBytes(path)` truncated to `maxBytes` with `UnsupportedOperationException` caught and mapped to null, because the default `readFileBytes` THROWS (`FileSystem.kt:192`; only `JvmFileSystemBase`, Android and `AppPrivateFileSystem` override it, iOS and wasmJs do not), so a bare delegation would make every fake and those two platforms throw; null means "cannot read" and the predicate treats it as not-V2. JVM facts: desktop `PlatformFileSystem` extends `JvmFileSystemBase`, whose `validatePath` is `protected`, so the bounded read (`validatePath`, then `FileInputStream.readNBytes(maxBytes)`) is an `open fun readFilePrefix` on `JvmFileSystemBase` that the desktop class forwards with `super<JvmFileSystemBase>` exactly as it forwards `readFileBytes` (`jvmMain/.../PlatformFileSystem.kt:64`); the Android class implements `FileSystem` directly and overrides it with the same saf-versus-legacy split as its `readFileBytes` (`contentResolver.openInputStream(uri).readNBytes`); the task begins by reading those three classes. `FilePrefixReadTest` (registered in `AllJvmTests`): a short file returns all of it, a longer one exactly `maxBytes`, an absent one null, never more bytes read than asked on JVM, a `FileSystem` without a `readFileBytes` override returns null, one with it truncates; feeds Task 1.4.3i through its story's edge (M)
##### Task 1.4.3h: Model-only read `readModel` and availability query `isAvailable` on `ProjectSidecarStore`, plus the extension `ProjectSidecarStore?.v2StorageAvailable()` that maps a null store to false and reads `FileSystem.storageStatus` (Tasks 1.4.3k and 1.4.3m) (the provider returns null for a demo graph and when no graph is active, `GraphContentViewModelSetup.kt:221-227`), with `SidecarModelOnlyReadTest` (S); rides in Story 1.4.3 (W8, after Story 1.4.2, the last editor of `ProjectSidecarStore.kt`, so the shared-file serialisation holds) and runs before Task 1.4.3d
##### Task 1.4.3i: The `NewSketchProject` predicate: the model-only read, bounded `readFilePrefix` probes through `SheetCodec.classifyHeader`, stop at the first non-V2 sheet, with the `CountingFileSystem` fake and `NewSketchProjectPredicateTest` (S); after Tasks 1.1.5c, 1.4.3g and 1.4.3h
##### Task 1.4.3j: `SketchReasonText` entries (`V2StorageUnavailable` per case, `BuildCapped`, `HasTechnicalData`) and `FlagModeMatrixTest` over modes x capabilities x project kinds, with the commonTest registration of `FlagModeMatrixTest`, `EditorRouterTest` and `NewSketchProjectPredicateTest` (`BUILD.bazel` srcs, `AllBusinessTests`) (S); after Tasks 1.4.3d and 1.4.3i
##### Task 1.4.3k: Storage status types in `CM/platform/V2StorageStatus.kt`, with no OPFS code: `V2StorageStatus`, the `StorageReason` enum, `PersistenceState`, the `BrowserPersistence` port with `UnsupportedBrowserPersistence` and the pure `PrivateWindowHint.from(estimatedQuotaBytes)`, with `V2StorageStatusTest` registered in the commonTest `BUILD.bazel` srcs and `AllBusinessTests` (S); its own node, W1, built in every outcome of D-127
##### Task 1.4.3l: `DomainError.FileSystemError.StorageQuotaExceeded` and `WriterLeaseNotHeld` with their branches of BOTH exhaustive `when`s over `DomainError` (`DomainError.toUiMessage()` and the `val msg: String = when (err)` of `DomainErrorTest`) and the `DomainErrorTest` additions (XS); its own node, W1, built in every outcome of D-127
##### Task 1.4.3m: The `FileSystem` defaults `storageStatus`, `awaitDurable`, `writeTempBytes`, `deleteTempBytes` and `readFileBytesSuspend`, the `SketchWriterLease` port (`state` and the monotonic `epoch` that grows each time the lease becomes `Held` again, D-150) with `AlwaysHeldSketchWriterLease` (epoch constant), the interim wasm `storageStatus` override and `FileSystemStoragePortsTest` registered in `AllBusinessTests` (S); its own node, after Tasks 1.4.3g, 1.4.3k and 1.4.3l, built in every outcome of D-127
##### Task 1.4.3n: `SheetLeaseRegistry` asks the `SketchWriterLease` before it grants a v2 sheet's edit lease and the router maps `HeldElsewhere` to the `LeaseConflict` outcome with the reason id `OtherTab` and the banner variant `LeaseConflict:OtherTab` (UX-149), with `WriterLeaseRouterTest` (S); after Task 1.4.3m
##### Task 1.4.3o: `ProjectSidecarStore` takes the `SketchWriterLease` as a required constructor parameter and refuses every write under the sidecar root while it is not `Held` (`write`, `writeSourceSnapshot` and `writeBackup` return `Failed(WriterLeaseNotHeld)`, `writeConflict` and `writeSheetConflictCopy` return `Left`, `writeNow` returns `Failed`, `flushPending` re-checks per replay and keeps the write parked), (the lease check is the FIRST statement of every refusing method, before the `unavailable()` return at `ProjectSidecarStore.kt:99` and before the `isGitSyncBusy()` park at `:100`, because a parked write skips every later check) a bundle parked under one lease epoch is never replayed under a later one (it becomes a conflict copy, D-150; `flushPending` clears `pending` at `:160` and re-parks a failed replay at `:163`, which keeps the bundle's ORIGINAL epoch stamp, never the current one), its two construction sites (`GraphContentViewModelSetup.kt` provider, which takes the lease from `deps.platformIntegrations`, and `SidecarTestSupport.kt`, which has no deps and builds a test lease) and `readSourceSnapshot` uses `readFileBytesSuspend`, with `WriterLeaseRefusalTest` (seven refusing methods, the epoch stamp, two construction sites and a read path make this an M, not an S) (M); after Tasks 1.4.3h, 1.4.3l and 1.4.3p
##### Task 1.4.3p: Early wiring of the two storage ports (D-146): `StelekitAppPlatformIntegrations` gains `sketchWriterLease` and `browserPersistence` in `StelekitAppDependencies.kt` with TEMPORARY defaults taken from the new `SketchStorageDefaults` (an object in `CM/ui/StelekitAppDependencies.kt`, the one source file of this task and a composition root, so it constructs `AlwaysHeldSketchWriterLease` and `UnsupportedBrowserPersistence` inside a root; Task 1.4.3t deletes the defaults), and the task adds `SketchWriterLease` and `BrowserPersistence` to `WiringAudit.SEAMS` and to the SEAMS list assertion of `WiringCompletenessTest` (10 names after this task) (S); its own node, after Tasks 1.4.3k, 1.4.3m and 2.1.2b (the last edge only serializes the edits of `WiringAudit.kt` and of the SEAMS list assertion; no data flows from Task 2.1.2b, block `plan-serial-edges`)
##### Task 1.4.3q: The entry-point tests of the two commonMain seam implementations, `SketchStorageAndroidEntryPointTest` (`WiredAppFixture`) and `SketchStorageDesktopEntryPointTest` (`DesktopWiredAppFixture`), which prove the lease is CONSULTED, not only passed: each builds the fixture twice, once with the default lease and once with a lease that answers `HeldElsewhere` (the fixture parameter added by Task 1.4.3s), and asserts through the real `GraphContentViewModelSetup` store provider that a `write` returns `Written` and then `Failed(WriterLeaseNotHeld)` with no file created, plus that the persistence port answers `Unsupported` (block `plan-entry-observation`; the host scan of Task 1.4.3t proves no production host overrides the defaults), their four `wiring-catalog.txt` lines and their registration in `AllAndroidUnitTests` and `AllJvmTests` (S); after Tasks 1.4.3p and 1.4.3t
##### Task 1.4.3r: `SheetSaver` turns the store's `WriterLeaseNotHeld` refusal into the read-only `LeaseConflict` state and stops its debounce instead of `SaveFailed`, with `WriterLeaseSaverTest` (S); after Task 1.4.3o and after Story 1.4.4
##### Task 1.4.3s: The Android and desktop host sites pass the two storage values explicitly from `SketchStorageDefaults` (`MainActivity.kt`, the desktop `Main.kt` and `ui/App.kt`, `WiredAppFixture` and `DesktopWiredAppFixture`, whose constructors take an optional `sketchWriterLease` that defaults to `SketchStorageDefaults`; D-146) (S); its own node, after Task 1.4.3p
##### Task 1.4.3t: The wasm `browser/Main.kt` passes the two values at BOTH `StelekitAppPlatformIntegrations(` sites (lines 365 and 516 today) from `SketchStorageDefaults`, the two fields become REQUIRED (the temporary defaults of Task 1.4.3p are deleted, so a forgotten site does not compile), and `SketchStorageHostSitesTest` scans every production site that constructs `StelekitAppPlatformIntegrations` and asserts it names both fields (Android and desktop sites from `SketchStorageDefaults`; the wasm sites from `SketchStorageDefaults` until Tasks 1.4.7e and 1.4.7i swap in the wasm classes) (S); its own node, after Task 1.4.3s
##### Task 1.4.3e: Record the A4 result in the ADR-005 index, set `ConversionOffered` in its one file (`CM/projectbridge/V2Mode.kt`) and, for NO-GO, add the ADR-001 amendment A4-NO-GO (the v1-compatible projection option sized at 3 M and 2 S tasks, 0.74M CU, requirements "A4 and A5 decision rules"); owner Tyler with the agent (XS)

#### Story 1.4.4: Single-writer autosave (needs 1.1.3, 1.1.5, 3.6.4a; the diagnostics MODEL and PORT are pulled ahead into W3 so this story has no forward dependency on the late Epic 3.6)
**As a** user, **I want** saves that never persist a stale scene after undo, **so that** disk equals memory.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Rapid edit/undo/edit ends consistent.
  - *Given* a fake writer with 200 ms latency and the sequence edit, undo, edit within 100 ms, *When* the debounce (500 ms) settles, *Then* the persisted scene equals the final in-memory scene and writes are serialised with a version counter.
- OOM guard.
  - *Given* a fake serializer that throws `OutOfMemoryError`, *When* a save runs, *Then* the UI shows a `SketchDiagnostics` save-failed entry with Retry, in-memory work is kept, and the process default handler is not invoked.
- Saves write the whole sheet file (the file is the only store), skip a write when the canonical bytes equal the last-written bytes, and update the model layer mirror in the same model write.
- Saver, DB row and the other sidecar writers agree (review C2, C6).
  - *Given* a v2 layer edit, *When* `SheetSaver` runs, *Then* under the per-slug `SidecarSlugLock` it writes the v2 file, the model (layer mirror, `fileSha256`) and, through `DatabaseWriteActor`, the DB sheet row (`layers`, `schemaVersion`) as one locked operation, and a following `SidecarReconciler.reconcile` is `Unchanged` (shared golden with Story 1.1.3); a reconcile holding the slug lock delays the saver instead of being overwritten by it.
- An external replace of the open sheet never loses either version (pre-mortem P1 #3; `SheetSaverConflictTest`).
  - *Given* sheet `s2` open in the v2 editor on device A (loaded sha H0) and a `git pull` replaces `sheets/s2.v2.json` with bytes of sha H1 (edited on device B), *When* A's debounce fires and `SheetSaver` runs, *Then* under the slug lock it compares `baseSha` (H0, the sha of the file it loaded or last wrote) with the on-disk sha, finds a mismatch and does NOT overwrite: it writes A's in-memory scene to `<slug>/conflicts/s2.v2.conflict-<hash8>.json` (`hash8` = first 8 hex of the scene's sha256; never named `sheets/*.json`, never hashed or scanned), raises a `SketchDiagnostics` entry "This sketch changed on another device. Your edits were saved as a copy." with actions Reload (adopt H1, discarding the in-memory scene only after the copy exists) and Keep mine (re-bases `baseSha` to H1 and writes), logs `sketch.save.conflict`, and `sheets/s2.v2.json` still has sha H1; *Then* both versions exist on disk. A matching `baseSha` saves normally and advances `baseSha` to the new sha; a mid-session pull event for the open sheet (the post-sync coordinator notification, subscribed by the editor through `RT`) also prompts Reload without waiting for the next save.
- Leaving the screen with unsaved or failed changes asks before discarding (UX-72).
  - *Given* a save failed (or edits are pending) and the user navigates back, *Then* a dialog offers the actions Retry save, Export copy and Discard; Retry is the default and focused first, Export copy writes the current scene through the export path without touching the sidecar, Discard leaves the sheet as last saved; in-memory work is never dropped on any other path (`SketchErrorState.SaveFailed`, Story 6.2.7).
- Encode is budgeted and off the main thread (pre-mortem P2 #6).
  - *Given* a 5,000-element sheet, *When* a save runs, *Then* encoding happens on a background dispatcher inside a `Throwable` guard, `sketch.encode_ms_5000` and `sketch.save_bytes_5000` are recorded against `sketch-engine-budgets.json` (CI gate, Story 6.2.2), and when the last encode exceeded its budget the debounce backs off to a longer interval (1 s, then 2 s, reset by a fast encode); a fake slow encoder proves the back-off through virtual time.
- On web the saver reports `Saved` only after `SketchDurability.awaitPersisted` resolves and shows `Saving...` until then (Task 1.4.7k; D-119, UX-140); `SheetSaverDurabilityTest` fakes a durability port that resolves late, fails with a quota error and never resolves.
- Diagnostics raised by the saver go through the `DiagnosticsPort` of Task 3.6.4a (E2): the saver never imports `UI2`; a fake port records entries in `SheetSaverTest`.
- Banner producers (engineering E2; Story 3.6.7). *Given* a failed save and a file changed under the open editor, *Then* `SheetSaver` emits the `SaveFailed` (summary "Couldn't save. Work is kept.", [Retry], Export copy under More, not dismissible) and `SaveConflict` ("Sketch changed on disk.", [Pick one]; Details and the conflict dialog name Keep mine, Load theirs and Save as copy) `BannerCandidate`s through `DiagnosticsPort` (Story 3.6.4a) and never touches `BannerSlot`; the full text and technical details live in the Details sheet.
**Files**: `RT/persist/SheetSaver.kt`, `RT/persist/SheetSaveGuard.kt` (`baseSha`, conflict copy), `CM/projectbridge/ProjectSidecarStore.kt` (`writeSheetConflictCopy`, from Task 0.1.1b), `UI2/LeaveSketchPrompt.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/SheetSaverTest.kt`, `BT/sketch/v2/SheetSaverConflictTest.kt` (new), `BT/sketch/v2/SheetSaverReconcileIntegrationTest.kt` (new; the saver, DB row and model-mirror agreement AC above), `AT/sketch/v2/AndroidCrashGuardTest.kt` (new; `SheetSaver` and `SolveRunner` scopes swallow `OutOfMemoryError` in their `CoroutineExceptionHandler`), `AT/sketch/v2/LeaveSketchPromptTest.kt` (new)
##### Task 1.4.4a: Debounced single-writer with version check; owns scope + handler (S)
##### Task 1.4.4b: Tests incl. disposed-composition no-launch (S)
##### Task 1.4.4c: `baseSha` compare-and-swap under the slug lock plus `conflicts/` copy through `writeSheetConflictCopy`, `sketch.save.conflict` log, `SheetSaverConflictTest` (S)
##### Task 1.4.4d: Pull-event subscription (post-sync coordinator notification through `RT`) and the conflict diagnostics entry with Reload / Keep mine actions via `DiagnosticsPort` (S)
##### Task 1.4.4e: Encode off the main thread inside a `Throwable` guard, `sketch.encode_ms_5000` / `sketch.save_bytes_5000` samples, 1 s then 2 s debounce back-off with a virtual-time test, `AndroidCrashGuardTest` (S)
##### Task 1.4.4f: `LeaveSketchPrompt` (Retry save / Export copy / Discard, Retry default and focused) with `LeaveSketchPromptTest` (S)

#### Story 1.4.5: Fusion, digest and embed preview read v2 sheets through an in-memory projection (BLOCKER D; needs 1.1.3, 1.1.4, 1.1.5, 1.2.2, 1.4.1; must be green before conversion is reachable)
**As a** user with fusion/digests, **I want** dimension and digest output for a converted sheet to equal what it was before conversion, **so that** LLM digests never use zero or stale data.
**Decision**: `V2ToV1.project` output is NEVER written to `sketch_primitives`. `RT/projection/SheetProjectionSource` loads a v2 sheet's scene from the sidecar file (cached by sha), projects it in memory and hands the primitives to `ProjectFusionSource`, `SketchCsvWriter`, `SketchSvgWriter` (v1 writers) and the digest path. `ProjectFusionSource` and those consumers, and `SketchPreviewLoader` (`ui/sketch/SketchPreviewLoader.kt:37`, used by `SketchPreview`/`ScreenRouter.kt:458`, which today reads `getPrimitives` from the DB and would render EVERY converted sheet empty; review C4), dispatch per sheet on `schema_version`: v1 reads DB primitives as today, v2 reads the projection; mixed projects (some sheets converted) are supported.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Projected ids, z and layer ordering match what fusion and CSV expect.
  - *Given* a mixed project (sheet A converted, sheet B not) built from the golden fixtures, *When* fusion, CSV and digest run, *Then* output bytes equal the output from the same project with A unconverted.
- Consumers never see stale v1 rows.
  - *Given* a converted sheet whose old `sketch_primitives` rows were mutated in the DB, *When* fusion runs, *Then* the output ignores those rows.
- Embeds of converted sheets are not empty (review C4).
  - *Given* a converted sheet with 12 projected primitives, *When* `SketchPreviewLoader.load(slug)` runs, *Then* it returns `SketchPreview.Drawing` of the in-memory projection (same primitives as before conversion for a converted v1 sheet) and never `Empty` merely because the DB holds no primitives for a v2 sheet; elements that do not project are counted ("N elements not shown in this preview") until Story 6.1.1 replaces this path with the full v2 draw list.
- `Dropped` elements are counted and surfaced ("N elements not used for dimensions"); no write to `sketch_primitives` occurs (a test asserts the repository write count is 0).
**Files**: `RT/projection/SheetProjectionSource.kt`, `CM/projectbridge/ProjectFusionSource.kt`, `CM/projectbridge/SketchExportSource.kt` (or the existing CSV/SVG/digest callers; list each in the task), `CM/ui/sketch/SketchPreviewLoader.kt`, `BT/sketch/v2/MixedProjectFusionGoldenTest.kt`, `BT/sketch/v2/SketchPreviewV2Test.kt`
##### Task 1.4.5a: `SheetProjectionSource` + per-sheet dispatch in `ProjectFusionSource`, CSV/SVG/digest callers (M)
##### Task 1.4.5b: Mixed v1+v2 golden and no-write/stale-row tests (S)
##### Task 1.4.5c: `SketchPreviewLoader` dispatch on `schema_version` over `SheetProjectionSource` + non-empty-preview test (S)

#### Story 1.4.6: Migration and shell UI: first-v2-write confirmation, post-conversion banner, failure dialog (consistency N1; needs 1.4.3, 1.4.3k, 1.4.3m; copy is OD-1)
**As a** user, **I want** a clear confirmation before my first v2 write and a clear result after conversion, **so that** I know older builds will not open the project and that a backup exists.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- First-write confirmation is its own dialog wired to OD-1, and opening a sketch never converts it (UX 14.2, UX-64, UX-104; D-12). *Given* flag on and a v1 sheet `s1` (`NotConverted`), *When* the user opens `s1`, *Then* it opens in the CLASSIC editor with no dialog and no write, and the `UpgradeChip` candidate (ux.md banner table; a `SketchNoticeQueue` entry this story EMITS and the `BannerSlot` renders) is shown: summary "Upgrade blocks other devices.", primary [Start], and under More: Details, Not now and Keep this sketch classic (announced politely; Details is a sheet whose text is `SketchConsentCopy`: "This sketch can be upgraded to the new editor. Upgrading stops older SteleKit versions from opening ANY sketch in this project."; [Not now] dismisses it for the session and leaves `s1` `NotConverted`; Keep this sketch classic sets `Declined`). The chip is one banner row and cannot hold the 170-character sentence (ux.md section 1, content fit), so no conversion can start before the first-write dialog below has shown the whole-project sentence in full (D-41). The row is a consent row (no short title) and the rule is ONE sentence: **the sheet replaces the row whenever the row's summary, word-wrapped greedily at the line width of the window's width and the current font scale, takes more than the row's 2 lines**. That is computed, not guessed: from font scale 1.11 at 320 dp wide and from 1.52 at 360 dp wide (the computed block of ux.md section 1 lists the line count at scales 1.0, 1.15, 1.3, 1.5 and 2.0 for both widths, and `check-plan.py` fails if the prose differs); the sheet then shows the line, the whole-project sentence and the same actions, unclipped at font scale 2.0, with the behaviour of Task 1.4.6i (`UpgradeChipConsentSheetTest`, UX-123, UX-126); for later sketches of an acknowledged project [Start] converts with no modal, which is accepted because the consequence stays on the chip (or its sheet) at every font scale and the Details of the post-conversion banner repeat it.
  - *When* [Start] is activated and no acknowledgement exists for THIS sketch project (key: device and project slug, not synced; D-16), *Then* the converter returns `RequiresConfirmation` and the dialog (text from `SketchConsentCopy`, UX-103) names the project ("Kitchen"), states that older SteleKit versions (including another device not yet updated) won't open the WHOLE project (the dialog does not promise a message and its "won't open" claim is scoped to builds that predate the reader-only release, Task 6.2.5e, which show their own error; builds from it onward open the sketch read-only with the `ReadOnly` banner of the routing matrix and show "Update SteleKit to view" only for a schema 3 or later project; U8), names the backup location, and offers [Convert and open] (disabled until the ONE checkbox "I understand" is checked; Space toggles; the box starts unchecked every time and is never remembered by itself) and [Open classic] (always enabled; also Esc and back); *When* "Convert and open" is activated, *Then* the per-project acknowledgement is stored and conversion proceeds; *When* "Open classic" (or Esc or back) is activated, with the box checked or not, *Then* nothing is written, nothing is acknowledged and `s1` stays `NotConverted` (the chip appears again on a later open). The dialog has no second checkbox (U6): "Keep this sketch classic" exists only in the `UpgradeOffer` chip menu, where it sets `ConversionState.Declined` (per sheet, `Settings`), the chip is not shown again at open, and the sheet menu entry "Upgrade to the new editor" shows the same dialog again. 
  - *NEW-sheet variant (UX-64; D-35)*: *Given* the flag on and the user creating a NEW sketch in a project with no acknowledgement for (device, slug), *Then* nothing converts and no backup exists, so the dialog (`FirstV2WriteDialog` in its `NewSheet` mode, same `SketchConsentCopy` whole-project sentence, NO backup sentence) is titled "Create a sketch with the new editor?", has the same ONE unchecked "I understand" checkbox gating [Create and open], and offers [Create classic sketch] (always enabled; creates a classic v1 sheet and shows the UX-77 `ClassicEditor` toast "Created as classic." [Change], whose Details say "Created with the classic editor because you chose it."); *When* Esc or back is used, *Then* the creation is CANCELLED (no sheet, no write, nothing acknowledged), so no path creates a classic sheet without saying so; the acknowledgement is stored only by [Create and open]. A test row covers all four outcomes. In both variants initial focus is the warning text block (focusable, non-interactive), Tab order is the warning text block, the checkbox, the left button, the primary button, and while the box is unchecked the disabled primary button stays focusable with the visible text "Check I understand to continue." under the checkbox row and as its accessible description (`UxMigrationAcceptanceTest`).
  - **Finding the new editor at the default mode Off (U6; D-97).** *Given* mode `Off` and a build and platform that are not capped, *When* the user opens the classic sketch menu, *Then* it lists "Try the new editor", which opens Settings > Sketches > New editor with focus on the mode group, stores nothing and shows no dialog (present in modes: Off; D-110); *Given* mode `NewProjectsOnly` or `On`, or a capped build or platform, *Then* the item is absent (the Upgrade entry and the toast routes replace it); the Settings confirmation and the per-project first-write dialog stay two gates asking two different questions (the second shows the first by reference in the same session; ux.md 14.1); `FlagModeSettingsTest.tryTheNewEditor_should_BePresentOnlyInOff_OpenTheModeGroupAndStoreNothing`.
- **No offer for an action that cannot succeed (U4; D-98).** *Given* mode `On` and a v1 sheet whose `ConversionPolicy.refusal` is non-null (a dimension in the project or zones on the sheet), *When* the sketch opens, *Then* the router outcome is legacy-editable with NO `UpgradeOffer` (no chip, no consent), and the sketch menu entry "Upgrade to the new editor" is present but disabled with the reason fragment `TechnicalDataNewSheet`; *Given* the chip was shown and a dimension is added before [Start], *When* [Start] is activated, *Then* the refusal is re-read, nothing converts and no `Failed` dialog opens (the chip is withdrawn and the disabled entry explains it); `UpgradeOfferTest.refusedSheet_should_NotOfferTheChip_AndDisableTheMenuEntryWithTheReason`, `UpgradeOfferTest.refusalAppearingBeforeStart_should_WithdrawTheChip`.
- **Later sketches in the SAME project after its acknowledgement**: opening them still does not convert; the chip's [Start] converts immediately with no modal (the consequence was acknowledged for this project and the post-conversion banner repeats it every time), and [Not now] / "Keep this sketch classic" work per sheet exactly as above, so the owner can decline sketch by sketch. The acknowledgement is per sketch project per device and is not synced; a sketch in a DIFFERENT project shows the dialog again, because the consequence (older builds cannot open that project) is per project. **The whole-project sentence is seen before the first Upgrade in each project**: the chip itself carries the consequence line until the project is acknowledged, after which the user has read it in the first-write dialog and the post-conversion banner repeats it (UX-103, UX-104; consent granularity D-16).
- Consent sheet behaviour (U2; Task 1.4.6i; ux.md 14.2; UX-104, UX-123, UX-126). *Given* a v1 sheet `s1` (`NotConverted`, mode `On`) whose chip row would wrap past 2 lines (font scale 1.15 at 320 dp), *When* the chip becomes the visible onboarding item and no gesture, text entry or other modal is active (otherwise it waits), *Then* the "Upgrade this sketch?" sheet opens ONCE per sketch project per day on this device (`ProjectAckStore.consentSheetDay`, one value per project, pruned with it): focus moves to its title, which is announced ("Upgrade this sketch?, dialog"), Tab cycles Start, Not now, Keep this sketch classic and the scrolling body, and closing returns focus to the control that held it before, or to the canvas when none did (the sheet opens on its own); *When* Esc, system back, a scrim tap, a drag down or [Not now] is used, *Then* all five mean Not now (`s1` stays `NotConverted`, nothing is written or acknowledged), the sheet does not reopen in this session for any sketch of the project, the banner slot is EMPTY (no truncated row), the classic editor is fully usable at once, and the upgrade stays reachable from the sketch menu "Upgrade to the new editor" and from the Notices list entry "Upgrade available" (silent, no announcement); *When* [Start], *Then* the first-write dialog (or, for an acknowledged project, the conversion) follows as above; in a LATER SESSION (a new app process) the chip returns (until-acted) and the sheet may open once more on a later day, while within this session neither the row nor the sheet returns for any sketch of the project; *Given* the row fits (font scale 1.0 at 320 dp; up to 1.5 at 360 dp), *Then* the chip row shows and no sheet opens. RUNTIME RULE (U5): real text measurement decides at runtime (`UpgradeOfferHost` measures the summary at the live width and font scale; the sheet replaces the row exactly when it measures past 2 lines); the greedy word-wrap model of `check-plan.py` and the computed block is the conservative test oracle. `UpgradeChipConsentSheetTest` runs scales 1.0, 1.15, 1.3, 1.5 and 2.0 at 320 dp and 360 dp, asserts row-versus-sheet equals the computed matrix, asserts `measuredLines <= modelLines` for every cell and every font of the representative set of the target (the target UI font plus one wider-glyph test font; fonts the user installs are not enumerated) (so the model's "row fits" always holds when measured; method `measuredLines_should_NotExceedModelLines_ForTheRepresentativeFontSet`), and asserts the sheet unclipped at 2.0 and 3.0. Threshold crossing (U5): an open sheet stays open and reflows when the window, fold or font scale changes (never closed by itself, never swapped for the row); a visible row that stops fitting is withdrawn and the sheet follows the open rule; widths below 320 dp are outside the guaranteed set (measurement decides, the row is never shown past 2 lines, no claim is made); the sheet's primary button is [Start], as on the row (`crossingThreshold_should_KeepAnOpenSheetOpen_AndWithdrawAnOverlongRow`, same test class).
- One canonical consent copy (UX-103). *Given* `SketchConsentCopy` (`CM/ui/sketch/v2/strings/SketchConsentCopy.kt`, one object) defines `WholeProjectWarning` = "Once a sketch is upgraded, older versions of SteleKit (including another device you have not updated) cannot open ANY sketch in that project." and `BackupSentence` = "A backup of the original is kept in <backupPath>." plus a per-surface lead-in, *When* `ConsentCopyTest` renders the flag-enable confirmation, the first-write dialog and the post-conversion banner Details, *Then* each contains `WholeProjectWarning` byte for byte (the banner in its Details form; the Upgrade chip carries the consequence line and its Details sheet carries the sentence, and the first-write dialog names the project, all built from the same constants), the backup surfaces contain `BackupSentence`, and no consent literal exists anywhere else (grep plus `HardcodedStringAuditTest`), so the wording changes in exactly one place. The read-only banner's [Use new] confirmation (`ReadOnlyUseNew`, U1) is the one exception to "contains `WholeProjectWarning`": its sketch is already a new-editor sketch, so nothing becomes unreadable by turning the mode on and it carries `WholeProjectAlreadyTrue` ("Older versions of SteleKit (including another device you haven't updated) already can't open this project, because it contains a new-editor sketch.") instead; `ConsentCopyTest` asserts that variant contains "reopens this sketch" and `WholeProjectAlreadyTrue`, and contains neither "projects stay classic" nor "can't open a project that contains".
- Post-conversion banner (UX-65).
  - *Given* a sheet just converted, *Then* the `PostConversion` candidate (ux.md banner table; this story emits it) "Upgraded. Backup saved." with Details (backup path via `BackupSentence` and `WholeProjectWarning`) and Dismiss under More is shown once per sheet and not again after dismissal or reopen.
- Conversion failure dialog (UX-66, UX-14.4).
  - *Given* `Failed(reason)` (including a forced hash mismatch), *Then* the dialog says the original was not changed and offers Open in legacy editor, Try again and Close, with Copy details carrying the reason code; the original bytes are unchanged (asserted by file bytes).
- Flag flip mid-session (UX-69).
  - *Given* the v2 editor is open, *When* the mode is changed off or on, *Then* the open editor is neither swapped nor closed and the `EngineChange` toast "Applies next time you open it." is shown (dismissable, announced politely) with [Reopen], which on the user's explicit activation saves pending edits and reopens this sketch in the editor the router now chooses (`UxMigrationAcceptanceTest.engineChange_should_OfferReopen_AndReopenOnlyOnExplicitActivation`).
- Flag-enable moment (U3; UX-76; copy from `SketchConsentCopy`, owner-confirmable D-3). *Given* Settings > Sketches with `ConversionReady = true`, *When* the user picks a mode other than Off in the mode control (Task 1.4.6h; or activates [Use new] on the read-only banner, which means `NewProjectsOnly` and shows the banner variant below), *Then* a confirmation shows BEFORE the mode is stored, unless this device's acknowledgement level already covers the chosen mode (recurrence rules of the mode-control accessibility bullet below). For `On`: "Turn on the new editor for all sketches? New sketches use it and each classic sketch gets an Upgrade offer. Nothing converts until you choose Upgrade on a sketch, and a backup is kept. <WholeProjectWarning> [Turn on] [Cancel]". For `NewProjectsOnly`: "Turn on the new editor for new projects? Sketches in new sketch projects, and in projects that already hold only new-editor sketches, use it. Other existing sketches and projects stay classic and nothing is converted. <WholeProjectWarning, new-project form> [Turn on] [Cancel]"; the banner variant (`ReadOnlyUseNew`): "Edit this sketch with the new editor? This sketch already uses the new editor, so it is read-only until the new editor is on. Turning it on reopens this sketch so you can edit it. New sketches in new projects will also use it. Classic sketches stay classic and nothing is converted. Older versions of SteleKit (including another device you haven't updated) already can't open this project, because it contains a new-editor sketch. [Turn on and reopen] [Cancel]"; all three link "Learn what changes" to an in-app page listing the consequences and the restore action; *When* Cancel, *Then* nothing is stored. The acknowledgement is stored PER DEVICE as a level (none, `NewProjectsOnly`, `On`; not synced), because a synced acknowledgement would let one device's "I understand" silence the warning on a device that has not seen it; the per-project first-write dialog (above) is still shown at the first Upgrade in each sketch project on each device. *Given* `ConversionReady = false`, *Then* the whole mode control is disabled with the reason "Not available in this build yet" (text from `SketchReasonText`), and with `ConversionOffered = false` only "All sketches (Upgrade)" is, never hidden.
- The read-only banner is never an enabled dead end (D-99). *Given* `ConversionReady = false`, *When* a v2 sheet opens read-only, *Then* the `ReadOnly` banner has no primary action and its Details say "This version of SteleKit can't edit new-editor sketches yet." (`ReadOnlyUseNewTest.readOnlyBanner_should_HaveNoPrimaryWhenTheBuildCannotTurnTheModeOn`).
- First-write dialog right after an enable confirmation (ux.md 14.2; UX-103). *Given* the whole-project warning was confirmed in the Settings enable confirmation or the banner variant of [Use new] in this app session, *When* the first first-write dialog of a project opens, *Then* its first paragraphs are replaced by the one `SketchConsentCopy.ProjectReference` sentence ("As you confirmed in Settings: older versions of SteleKit can't open the whole sketch project <name> once it holds a new-editor sketch.") with the same backup sentence (convert variant), checkbox and buttons; *Given* no enable confirmation this session, *Then* the full text shows; the acknowledgement is stored identically in both (`ConsentCopyTest.firstWriteDialog_should_UseTheReferenceFormOnlyRightAfterAnEnableConfirmation`).
- Mode control accessibility and confirmation recurrence (U4; ux.md 14.1; UX-76, UX-90). *Given* Settings > Sketches, *Then* the three options are one radiogroup named "New editor" with each option's explanation as its accessible description; *When* Down or Up is pressed, *Then* focus moves between the radios and no dialog opens and nothing is stored; *Given* "All sketches (Upgrade)" is capped (`ConversionOffered` false), *Then* it stays focusable in the arrow order, is announced unavailable, and "Not available in this build yet" is visible and its accessible description; *Given* the device acknowledgement level is none, *When* `NewProjectsOnly` is chosen and confirmed, *Then* the level is `NewProjectsOnly`; *When* `On` is then chosen, *Then* the dialog shows again before storing (level below the target); *When* the user narrows `On` to `NewProjectsOnly` or to Off, *Then* no dialog shows; *Given* the level is `On`, *When* the user returns from Off to `NewProjectsOnly` or `On`, *Then* the mode is stored without a dialog; *When* the already-selected mode is chosen again, *Then* nothing is shown or written; *When* Cancel is pressed on any dialog, *Then* nothing is stored and the level is unchanged; *Given* the read-only banner, *When* [Use new] is activated at any level, *Then* its banner variant shows. `FlagModeSettingsTest` asserts each of these; the radiogroup behaviour that differs by platform (arrow keys move focus without selecting, Space or Enter chooses, a disabled option stays in the arrow order) also runs in a real browser through `FlagModeRadioWasmTest` (`:kmp:wasmJsBrowserTest`), because Robolectric cannot show the web semantics. The option lines are the ux.md 14.1 strings: Off says new-editor sketches already in the graph open read-only, New sketch projects only says a sketch added to an existing project is classic, and All sketches (Upgrade) says each classic sketch gets an Upgrade offer.
- Reason precedence (UX-131; D-111): the reasons of the ux.md 14.1 table are `Platform`, `BuildCapped`, `TechnicalData`, `ModeOff`, `NpoClassicSheets`, `NpoEmptyProject`, `NpoUnreadable`, `UserChose`, in that precedence order, and a toast names only the FIRST applicable one (`UxMigrationAcceptanceTest.classicReason_should_NameOnlyTheFirstApplicableRow_InPrecedenceOrder`).
- A NEW sheet that is created as legacy (U3): *Given* the user creates a sketch while the mode is `Off`, the build is capped, the mode is `NewProjectsOnly` and the project already exists without being all-v2 (a v1 sheet, or no sheets), or the project holds technical data that refuses v2 (`HasTechnicalData`), *Then* the sheet is created in the legacy editor and the `ClassicEditor` toast "Created as classic." with [Change] is announced politely through the `SketchNoticeQueue`; its Details give the reason from the one `SketchReasonText` table of ux.md 14.1 ("Created with the classic editor because <reason>.", for `NewProjectsOnly` ending with the route "choose All sketches (Upgrade) in Settings") and [Change] opens Settings > Sketches > New editor with focus on the mode group, with the just-created sketch left open underneath (Back returns to it; a changed mode never swaps it). Recurrence (one row of the toast recurrence table of ux.md section 16): *Given* the STORED mode is `Off` (what the user chose, not the effective mode), *Then* no toast is shown (a silent Notices entry only); *Given* any other stored mode, including one a cap lowers to an effective `Off` (the `Platform` reason after a mode stored in a browser that had OPFS, `BuildCapped`), *Then* the toast shows once per (graph, project slug, reason) per device and a different reason in the same project shows it once more; "once" is persisted by the `ClassicNoticeLedger` (D-96), an extension of `ProjectAckStore` (device-local, never synced): one per-graph `Settings` key `sketch.classicNotices.<graphId>` holding at most 200 `slug|reason` entries (the reasons are the closed `SketchReasonText` set, so one project can hold at most that many), the oldest entry evicted first when the cap is hit (an evicted entry only lets the toast show once more), entries of a project that no longer exists dropped on every write and the whole key removed through the `GraphManager.onGraphRemoved` hook (`GraphManager.kt:137`, invoked on a successful removal at `:640`; a single-slot callback already assigned by the composition roots, `MainActivity.kt:379` on Android, so the ledger cleanup is composed into that handler and never overwrites it); `ClassicNoticeLedgerTest` asserts survival across a store re-creation (restart), the 200-entry bound with eviction order, the drop for a deleted project and the removal on graph removal; *Given* a reason Settings cannot change (`Platform`, `BuildCapped`, `TechnicalData`, `UserChose`, or a mode other than `NewProjectsOnly`, or "All sketches (Upgrade)" not selectable), *Then* the toast is the `ClassicEditorFixed` row whose visible action is [Learn more] (Details stays a More item, never a second primary), not [Change]. `UxMigrationAcceptanceTest` asserts all four; a project you emptied of sketches is an existing empty project again and gets the existing-project reason; it never silently differs from the flag state shown in Settings.
- Newer-schema screen actions have an owner (U3, UX-79): *Given* the `NewerSchema` screen, *Then* "Check for updates" calls an `UpdateCheckPort` (UNVERIFIED whether an update-check seam already exists: the task begins by grepping `CM/platform` and the release workflow; fallback is opening the project's GitHub Releases URL through the existing external-link seam) and "Export last backup" copies the newest `backup/*.v1.json` through the platform file-save seam without writing to the sidecar and is hidden when the project has no such file (a backup exists only for a converted sheet; `NewerSchemaScreenTest.exportLastBackup_should_BeHiddenWhenNoBackupExists`); both are tested with fakes and neither writes to the project.
- The two no-editor states are ONE accessible screen (U1, U5; UX-134, UX-138; D-117). *Given* a sheet whose file schema is newer than this build supports, or a v2 sheet while `V2StorageAvailable` is false, *When* it is opened, *Then* `NoEditorScreen` (the shared host; `NewerSchemaScreen` and the storage-unavailable body are its two variants) shows a level-1 heading naming the state ("Update SteleKit to view this sketch" / "This sketch can't be opened here"), the reason text and the actions of ux.md 14.7: `NoEditorScreen` actions, newer schema: [Copy details], [Check for updates], [Export last backup], [Close]; storage unavailable: [Copy details], [How to open it], [Close]. For the transient reasons `PreloadFailed` and `CheckTimedOut` the same screen reads "This sketch can't be opened right now" and offers, transient: [Reload], [Copy details], [Close] (UX-147; D-135). [Copy details] copies the sketch name, project name, schema version, platform and browser and the reason id and no sketch content; [How to open it] opens the in-app page "Where new-editor sketches open" (desktop, Android and the supported browsers; it claims no export); where nothing can be exported the screen says so in one line ("Nothing can be exported from here: this device can't read the sketch."). Accessibility: focus moves to the heading on arrival (programmatic focus, so a screen reader announces the heading and the first paragraph once, WCAG 4.1.3), the container is a region named by the heading (not a dialog: no focus trap, WCAG 4.1.2), the heading is level 1 (WCAG 1.3.1), the tab order is heading, text, [Copy details], the guidance or version actions, [Close] (WCAG 2.4.3), Esc and system back activate [Close], and closing returns focus to the control that opened the sketch; `NoEditorAccessibilityTest` (Robolectric) asserts the role, name, heading level, initial focus, tab order and focus return for both variants, and `check-plan.py` compares both action lists with the ux.md 14.7 table.
- Web durability, session-only and persistence wording (U1; UX-139, UX-143; D-128). *Given* the web build and a first v2 write, *Then* the first-write dialog (both variants) gains ONE sentence from `SketchConsentCopy.WebStorage` (the wording is in ux.md 14.2; a graph opened temporarily gets the session-only sentence instead), its confirm handler requests `navigator.storage.persist()` and nothing earlier does, a `Denied` answer shows the one non-modal notice and Settings > Sketches shows "Browser storage" with its `PersistenceState` and [Ask the browser to keep it], and the mode control of a temporary graph shows the session-only line as visible text and accessible description. `UxMigrationAcceptanceTest` asserts the web variant of both dialogs, the temporary-graph variant and that no `persist()` call happens before the confirm click.
- Pending storage status (U2; UX-141; D-128). *Given* `V2StorageStatus` is `Checking`, *Then* the mode control disables its two On options with the checking reason (Off stays enabled), a v2 embed shows its loading placeholder, opening a v2 sheet shows `Pending` with [Close], and a create action waits for the status and then follows the resolved row (never a v2 sheet from `Checking`); after 10 s the status is `Unavailable(CheckTimedOut)` with its own reason. `EditorRouterTest` (the `Pending` row) and `FlagModeSettingsTest` cover it; the tailored reason of each `Unavailable` reason is `WebStorageUxTest` (Task 1.4.7l).
- The mode control's reasons have one precedence and the rest of Settings follows it (U4; UX-137; D-117). *Given* `V2StorageAvailable` is false, *Then* the radios "New sketch projects only" and "All sketches (Upgrade)" are disabled with the `V2StorageUnavailable` text of their case (Off stays enabled and selected, and its line reads "New sketches are classic." without the clause about read-only new-editor sketches, which is false there), and Restore original, the backup size, Delete backups and the new-editor settings (Finger drawing, single-key shortcuts, default style) are shown disabled with the same reason, never hidden; *Given* the platform can store and the build caps the mode, *Then* the reason is "Not available in this build yet"; *Given* both apply, *Then* the `Platform` reason wins (the order of the ux.md 14.1 reason table). `FlagModeSettingsTest` asserts the reason text per case and the disabled rows.
- UX criteria owned by this story, cited literally (each has a test row in validation.md): UX-134 the no-editor screen offers [Copy details] and the guidance action and says nothing can be exported; UX-137 one reason precedence for the mode control and the disabled rest of Settings; UX-138 the accessibility contract of the no-editor screen; UX-79 the newer-schema screen's actions both work and write nothing (above); UX-129 the discovery item is present in mode Off only (D-97, D-110); UX-130 no chip or consent for an action that cannot succeed (D-98); UX-131 `ClassicEditorFixed` where Settings cannot change the outcome and one reason by precedence (D-101); UX-132 [Export last backup] hidden when no backup exists (D-103).
- The boot-refusal screen (UX-152; D-149; Task 1.4.6k). *Given* a second browser tab on a persistent web graph whose first tab holds the driver lock `stelekit-sqlite-driver-<graphId>`, *When* the page boots, *Then* `GraphLockedElsewhereException` aborts the boot before the editor exists and the plain-DOM `#loading` overlay shows the boot-refusal screen of ux.md 14.9 (a level-1 heading, an alert message as the one announcement with no focus move, [Reload] as the first tab stop with a visible focus ring and [Open temporarily] as the second, rem sizing, reflow at 320 CSS px and at 400% zoom, 44 px targets, and an automatic reload when the lock frees or the `message-no-retry` text where the browser cannot wait on the lock; D-156), never the `OtherTab` banner; the files are `kmp/src/wasmJsMain/.../browser/BootRefusal.kt`, `.../browser/Main.kt` (`showBootError` is replaced for this exception) and `kmp/src/wasmJsMain/resources/index.html`; a temporary graph and the in-memory demo fallback take no driver lock and show neither outcome.
**Files**: `UI2/FirstV2WriteDialog.kt`, `UI2/UpgradeOfferChip.kt`, `UI2/UpgradeConsentSheet.kt` (new; Task 1.4.6i), `UI2/SketchModeSetting.kt` (new; Task 1.4.6h), `CM/ui/sketch/v2/strings/SketchConsentCopy.kt`, `UI2/ConversionBanner.kt`, `UI2/ConversionFailureDialog.kt`, `UI2/EnableV2Confirmation.kt`, `UI2/NewerSchemaScreen.kt`, `CM/ui/sketch/SketchEditorRouter.kt` (confirmation hook, Upgrade action), `RT/session/ProjectAckStore.kt` (new; per-project acknowledgement, device-local), `AT/sketch/v2/UxMigrationAcceptanceTest.kt` (new; UX-64, UX-65, UX-66, UX-69, UX-76, UX-77), `AT/sketch/v2/ConsentCopyTest.kt` (new; UX-103), `AT/sketch/v2/UpgradeOfferTest.kt` (new; UX-104, UX-123), `AT/sketch/v2/UpgradeChipConsentSheetTest.kt` (new; UX-104, UX-123, UX-126), `AT/sketch/v2/FlagModeSettingsTest.kt` (new; UX-76), `kmp/src/wasmJsTest/kotlin/dev/stapler/stelekit/sketch/v2/FlagModeRadioWasmTest.kt` (new; UX-76, UX-90), `AT/sketch/v2/ReadOnlyUseNewTest.kt` (new; UX-69, UX-76)

##### Task 1.4.6d: `NewerSchema` screen actions: "Check for updates" and "Export last backup" (S)

##### Task 1.4.6f: `SketchConsentCopy` single source (including the `ProjectReference` form of the first-write dialog's first paragraph), the three surfaces rewired to it, `ConsentCopyTest` (S)
##### Task 1.4.6g: `UpgradeOffer` chip, router outcome "legacy editable with offer", Upgrade action, Not now / Keep this sketch classic, later-sketch behaviour after the per-project acknowledgement, the consequence line "Upgrade blocks other devices." on the chip and the full sentence in its Details sheet, shown through the `OnboardingSequencer` (the classic editor hosts the chip through a thin `UpgradeOfferHost` adapter that asks the sequencer; no other v1 behaviour changes) (S)
##### Task 1.4.6h: Settings > Sketches mode control, with the classic sketch-menu item "Try the new editor" (the one discovery path, present in modes: Off; absent in `NewProjectsOnly`, `On` and a capped build or platform; it opens Settings > Sketches > New editor with focus on the mode group, asks no consent and adds no banner, toast or counter; U6, D-97) (radio group Off / New sketch projects only / All sketches (Upgrade); a capped option disabled with ONE reason by the order `Platform` then `BuildCapped` ("Not available in this build yet" only where the platform can store), never hidden; the disabled rest of Settings, UX-137; the web "Browser storage" row over `PersistenceState` with its inline refusal line, UX-147), the `NewProjectsOnly` confirmation variant and its `SketchConsentCopy` lead-in, with its accessibility contract (radiogroup named "New editor", arrow keys move focus without selecting, each option's explanatory line as its accessible description, a disabled option stays focusable with the reason as visible text and accessible description) and the per-device acknowledgement LEVEL rules (same or narrower mode and re-selecting: no dialog; wider than the level: dialog before storing; the banner's [Use new] always shows its variant), with `FlagModeSettingsTest` and the `ConsentCopyTest` extension (the radio line for New sketch projects only reads "New projects use the new editor, and so do projects that already hold only new-editor sketches. Other existing projects stay classic, and so does any sketch you add to one."; the heading says "this graph, on this device", D-88) (S)
##### Task 1.4.6i: `UpgradeConsentSheet` (the sheet that replaces the Upgrade chip row where its summary exceeds the row's 2 lines): open rule (not mid-gesture, not during text entry, not over another modal, at most once per day per sketch project on this device), dismissal (Esc, back, scrim tap, drag down and [Not now] all mean Not now), focus handling, what remains after dismissal (no banner occupant; the sketch menu entry and the Notices list), its recurrence and the font-scale matrix, with `UpgradeChipConsentSheetTest` (S)
