---

# Phase 4: Constraints

### Epic 4.1: Solver (gated by Story 0.1.2 and ADR-003 Accepted)
**Goal**: Production solver in `SOLV` meeting the NFRs, clean-room.

#### Story 4.1.1: Numeric core
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- QR with pivoting recovers rank.
  - *Given* a random 40x60 matrix constructed with rank 25 (kotest-property), *When* ranked, *Then* rank == 25; Cholesky of damped normal equations solves to residual below 1e-9; no allocation per iteration after warm-up (buffer reuse test).
**Files**: `SOLV/linalg/DenseMatrix.kt`, `SOLV/linalg/Qr.kt`, `SOLV/linalg/Cholesky.kt`, `CT/sketch/constraints/LinalgPropertyTest.kt`
##### Task 4.1.1a: Matrix + Cholesky (S)
##### Task 4.1.1b: Householder QR with pivoting + rank (M)

#### Story 4.1.2: Residuals and analytic Jacobians for the v1 constraint set
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Jacobian equals finite differences.
  - *Given* each constraint type at a random non-degenerate configuration, *When* the analytic Jacobian is compared to central differences, *Then* max error is under 1e-6.
- Degenerate geometry is safe.
  - *Given* a zero-length line with `Parallel`, *Then* residual/Jacobian contain no NaN/Infinity and the result is `Redundant`/`Degenerate`, never an exception.
**Files**: `SOLV/constraints/*.kt` (one file per family: Coincident, Orientation, Tangent, Dimensional, Symmetric), `CT/sketch/constraints/JacobianFiniteDifferenceTest.kt`
##### Task 4.1.2a: Point/line families: coincident, point-on-object, H/V, parallel/perpendicular, equal, fixed; the solver's point parameters back both `PointElement` and line endpoints (M)
##### Task 4.1.2b: Circle/arc: tangent (4 cases: line-circle, circle-circle, line-arc, endpoint), radius/diameter, symmetric; circle = `EllipseElement` with equal radii, arc = `ArcElement` (OD-8; if declined only line-circle and circle-circle tangent remain) (M)
##### Task 4.1.2c: Dimensional: distance(+X/Y), angle with disambiguation policy, reference mode (M)

#### Story 4.1.3: Solve loop, decomposition, diagnosis
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Satisfied within tolerance (property).
  - *Given* a generated satisfiable sketch (random geometry, constraints derived from it, then geometry perturbed), *When* solved, *Then* every constraint residual is under 1e-9 times sketch scale or the result is `NoConvergence` with the last good params returned.
- Redundant vs conflict, DOF.
  - *Given* the Story 0.1.2 cases, *Then* ids and DOF match; DOF from final-system rank equals the analytic count on primitive-only sketches.
- Fallback and caps.
  - *Given* an iteration budget forced to 3 for the first algorithm, *Then* the fallback runs and the result names which algorithm succeeded; wall-time cap returns `NoConvergence` rather than hanging.
- Performance.
  - *Given* 100 constraints, *Then* solve < 50 ms on JVM (budget file; the wasmJs figure is 150 ms hard, 50 ms advisory, D-30).
- The geometric-only fallback has an acceptance scenario it CAN pass (P4; requirements metric 3; D-60; per target, D-114: it runs on every target whose `SolverTier` is `GeometricOnly`, and the bracket plate on every `Full` target). *Given* only Coincident, Horizontal, Vertical, Equal and Distance, *When* `FallbackPlateScenarioTest` builds the plate (four lines, coincident corners, two Horizontal, two Vertical, width Distance 6 in, height Distance 4 in) and the pocket (four lines, coincident, Horizontal, Vertical, Equal on two adjacent sides, Distance 2 in on one), *Then* the DOF count equals the analytic 4, the maximum residual is under 1e-6 scene units, dragging a plate corner moves the whole plate with 6 x 4 in intact within 1e-6, a second Distance with another value on the bottom edge is `Conflict` naming both Distance ids, and editing the width to 7 in widens the plate by 1 in in one undo step. `BracketPlateScenarioTest` (Fixed, Radius, Parallel) is enabled only for the kinds that pass their randomised property test; until all three do, metric 3 is judged on the fallback plate.
**Files**: `CT/sketch/constraints/FallbackPlateScenarioTest.kt` (new), `CT/sketch/constraints/BracketPlateScenarioTest.kt` (new), `SOLV/Solver.kt`, `SOLV/Components.kt`, `SOLV/Diagnosis.kt`, `CT/sketch/constraints/SolverPropertyTest.kt`, `BT/sketch/constraints/SketchSolverImportDirectionTest.kt`
##### Task 4.1.3a: LM with damping + caps (M)
##### Task 4.1.3b: Components, rank diagnosis, redundant/conflict ids (M)
##### Task 4.1.3c: DogLeg/BFGS fallback only if the spike required it; else reserved note (S)
##### Task 4.1.3d: Import-direction test; benchmark sample into budgets (S)

#### Story 4.1.4: Solver runner (drag, commit)
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Drag solves on a transient scene with warm start; commit creates ONE transaction including solver-moved geometry; non-convergence keeps last good geometry.
  - *Given* a Horizontal constraint on `L1` and a drag of its end vertex to (50, 30), *When* released, *Then* `L1` end is (50, 0) within 1e-9, history has one new entry, and undo restores both the vertex and the constraint-derived position; redo replays the stored patch without re-solving (solver call counter stays flat).
- Transforms and bindings with constraints.
  - *Given* a constrained `L1` (Horizontal, Distance=100), *When* it is moved, resized or rotated as a single element, or as part of a group transform, *Then* the transform is applied, the solver re-runs, and the result either satisfies the constraints or the transform is rejected with a diagnostic and geometry unchanged (stated per transform kind in the test table); one undo step.
  - *Given* an `ArrowElement a1` bound to `RectElement r1` whose geometry the solver moved, *When* the solve commits, *Then* `a1`'s endpoint is recomputed from the binding in the SAME transaction, and redo replays the stored patch including the binding-derived geometry (solver and binding-recompute counters stay flat on redo).
- Runner owns its scope and exception handler; solving never runs in `DrawScope`.
- A slow solve shows progress and can be cancelled (UX-36).
  - *Given* a fake solver that takes longer than `SceneLimits.slowSolveIndicatorMs` (100 ms) of virtual time, *When* a constraint is committed, *Then* a "Solving..." indicator with Cancel appears near the status line once the threshold passes (and not before), the solve runs off the UI thread, and *When* Cancel is activated, *Then* the solve is cancelled, geometry is restored to the last good pose, no history entry is added and the indicator disappears; a solve under the threshold never shows the indicator (`TestScope` virtual time, no real delay).
- UX criteria owned by this story, cited literally (each has a test row in validation.md): UX-35 non-convergence leaves the last good geometry, never NaN or half-solved, with "Can't satisfy constraints here" and Undo.
**Files**: `RT/constraints/SolveRunner.kt`, `UI2/SolveProgressIndicator.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/SolveRunnerTest.kt`
##### Task 4.1.4a: Runner + transaction integration + counters (M)
##### Task 4.1.4b: Slow-solve indicator, cancel path restoring last good, virtual-time tests (S)

### Epic 4.2: Constraint integration and UI
#### Story 4.2.1: Constraint model, scene adapter, persistence
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Handles map elements to parameters; deleting geometry cascades its constraints and undo restores them.
  - *Given* `L1` with Horizontal c1 and Distance c2, *When* `L1` is deleted, *Then* c1, c2 are removed in the same step and one undo restores all three; constraint ids are stable across save/load.
- The 200-constraint cap is enforced.
  - *Given* a sheet with 200 constraints, *When* a 201st is added, *Then* the result is `Either.Left(CommandError.ConstraintCapExceeded(200))`, nothing changes, and the diagnostics entry reads "Constraint limit reached (200)". A native sheet with 201 constraints is rejected at decode by `SceneLimits` (Story 1.2.4).
- The user-facing rule for what can be constrained is ONE pure table, settled here before Stories 4.2.2 and 4.2.3 (U2, UX-81; owner-confirmable D-2). Constraints apply to technical geometry only: `LineElement` (incl. polyline), `RectElement`, circle and ellipse (`EllipseElement`), `ArcElement`, `PointElement`. Text, arrows, images, freedraw and frames are never constrainable.
  - *Given* each element kind, *When* `ConstraintEligibility.reason(kind, constraint)` is asked, *Then* an eligible pair returns `Eligible` and an ineligible one returns a plain-language reason from the string table: for text/arrow/image/freedraw/frame the ONE string `SketchReasonText.NotConstrainable`, "Text, arrows, images, freehand and frames can't be constrained. Use align and distribute instead." (frames named; the same string serves the flyout, the touch-help popup, the item semantics and the `NotConstrainable` command error, and a grep test fails on any second wording); for "Radius needs a circle or arc"; for "Parallel needs two lines"; the Constrain flyout, the auto-constrain engine, the importer's `extras` handling and the command layer ALL read this table (a test greps that none re-implements the rule), and a command that targets an ineligible element returns `CommandError.NotConstrainable(kind)`.
  - Degrees of freedom are shown in plain language (UX-83): "2 degrees of freedom" is accompanied by "(2 moves still possible)" in the panel's detail line, "Fully constrained" by "(nothing can move)", and the Details expander explains "A degree of freedom is one way the sketch can still change shape or move." The glossary term DOF never appears alone without this explanation on first display.
- UX criteria owned by this story, cited literally (each has a test row in validation.md): UX-38 deleting geometry with constraints removes them in the same step and one undo restores both, with a count toast.
**Files**: `V2/constraints/ConstraintModel.kt`, `V2/constraints/ConstraintEligibility.kt` (new), `V2/constraints/SceneToProblem.kt`, `V2/codec/SheetCodecV2.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/ConstraintAdapterTest.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/ConstraintEligibilityTest.kt` (new; UX-81, UX-83)
##### Task 4.2.1a: Model + commands (add/remove/convert-to-reference) + `ConstraintCapExceeded` (M)
##### Task 4.2.1b: Adapter mapping scene elements to solver geometry (consistency B1, OD-8): `PointElement` = point, `LineElement` = line/polyline (consecutive points), `RectElement` = 4 lines + implicit constraints, `ArcElement` = arc, `EllipseElement` with `rx == ry` = circle (an ellipse with `rx != ry` is rejected as a constraint target with the reason "Only circles can be constrained"; a Radius, Diameter or Tangent constraint added to a circle keeps `rx == ry` on every solve); arrows/text/freedraw/image/diamond/frame dimension by bbox only. Operands are `ConstraintTarget(elementId, part)`: THIS task defines the type with `TargetPart.Whole` only (the default, so every constraint of this story works unchanged); Task 4.2.6a (W7) adds the other parts, the accepted-parts table and `PartHitTester` without changing the type's shape (no forward dependency from 4.2.1 on 4.2.6). Test: each mapping, plus rejection of an unequal ellipse (M)
##### Task 4.2.1d: `ConstraintEligibility` table, reason strings, `NotConstrainable` error and the plain-language DOF wording (S); BEFORE Stories 4.2.2 and 4.2.3
##### Task 4.2.1c: Constraint fields in `SheetCodecV2` (sidecar only; there is no `sketch_constraints` table, see Story 1.4.1) (S)

#### Story 4.2.2: Auto-constraint on draw
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Snap-inferred constraints.
  - *Given* Line tool and a line ended on another line's endpoint with a snap, *Then* a Coincident is added; a near-horizontal line within 2 degrees adds Horizontal; Ctrl suppresses; all in the same transaction as the drawn element.
- Auto-constrain is an explicit toggle with a stated default per sheet kind (U2, UX-82; owner-confirmable D-2).
  - *Given* a new DIAGRAM sheet (free-form job), *Then* "Auto-constrain while drawing" is OFF by default (a diagram must not silently accumulate constraints); *Given* a sheet created as scaled/technical (sheet scale set, or created from the technical template) or a sheet converted from v1, *Then* it is ON by default; the toggle is in the view cluster and overflow with its current state in the semantics, stored per sheet, and changing it is not a document edit. Auto-constrain only ever considers `ConstraintEligibility`-eligible elements (never text or arrows).
  - *Given* touch-only use (no Ctrl key), *When* auto-constrain is ON, *Then* suppression is touch-accessible: the toast "Constraint added." carries Undo, and a held "Free draw" chip (view cluster, toggle for the next shape only) suppresses inference without a keyboard; Ctrl remains the desktop path.
- Auto-added constraints are announced and undoable (UX-39).
  - *Given* a Coincident was inferred, *Then* a one-line toast "Constraint added." (Details: "Added Coincident") with Undo appears (announced politely); *When* Undo is activated, *Then* the constraint is removed and the drawn element stays; *When* Ctrl is held on release, *Then* no constraint and no toast appear; a Horizontal inferred within 2 degrees shows "Added Horizontal".
**Files**: `V2/constraints/AutoConstrain.kt`, `UI2/ConstraintToast.kt`, `UI2/AutoConstrainToggle.kt` (new), `CT/sketch/v2/AutoConstrainTest.kt`, `AT/sketch/v2/AutoConstrainToggleTest.kt` (new; UX-82)
##### Task 4.2.2a: Inference rules + suppression (S)
##### Task 4.2.2b: Toast host with Undo and polite announcement through `SketchNoticeQueue` (XS)
##### Task 4.2.2c: Auto-constrain toggle (default OFF on diagram sheets, ON on scaled/technical and converted sheets) and the touch-accessible one-shot suppression chip (S)

#### Story 4.2.3: Constraint panel, glyphs, status
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Status line and list.
  - *Given* a sketch with 2 DOF left, *Then* the status shows "2 degrees of freedom" in a live region (in the hint/status row; D-40); *Given* a diagram sheet with no constraints and 54 free elements, *Then* no degrees-of-freedom text is shown at all (the status line is only for sheets with at least one constraint or technical sheets); *Given* an over-constrained add, *Then* the new constraint is rejected, geometry unchanged, message "Parallel conflicts with Horizontal on Line 3", with actions Remove / Make reference / Show conflicts, plus Replace existing when the conflicting constraint is a dimension of the same kind on the same target (Story 4.2.7); state is shown by glyph and text, not colour alone; fully constrained elements get a distinct dash style plus colour.
- The Constraints tab is discoverable without a selection (UX-84). *Given* a sheet with no constraints and nothing selected, *Then* the side-pane/sheet tab "Constraints" is still present (not hidden), and its empty state reads "No constraints yet. Select a line, circle or point, then choose Constrain." with the Constrain trigger disabled and its reason ("Select an element to constrain") exposed by touch help (Story 3.6.7); ux.md section 1 no longer hides the tab. *Given* a selected text, arrow or image, *Then* the Constrain items are disabled with the `ConstraintEligibility` reason.
- The Constrain flyout is available for ONE or more selected elements (consistency B3, UX-37) and, at compact width, from the dock and the overflow menu (UX-118, Story 3.1.3).
  - *Given* a single `LineElement` L1 selected, *When* the Constrain flyout opens, *Then* it is shown and Horizontal, Vertical, Fixed and Distance (line length) are enabled; *Given* a single circle, *Then* Radius and Diameter are enabled; *Given* one element, *Then* the two-element kinds (Coincident, Parallel, Perpendicular, Equal, Tangent, Symmetric, Distance between elements, Angle) are shown disabled with the reason ("Parallel needs two lines"); *Given* nothing selected, *Then* the flyout is hidden or its trigger disabled with "Select an element to constrain". Only constraint kinds with a passing property test (Story 4.1.3) are listed (Spike 0.1.2 fallback).
- Glyph declutter and spacing on dense sketches (UX-106). *Given* a sketch where more than one glyph anchor falls within one hit-area size (the `TouchTargetFloor`: 24 dp mouse-only, 44 dp when a touch or pen pointer is present) of another at the current zoom, *Then* the glyphs at that spot collapse into ONE count badge ("3") with a hit area of that size whose activation (tap, click, Enter on focus) opens a list popup of the underlying constraints, each row selectable; *Given* an element is selected, *Then* all of its glyphs are shown fanned out with hit areas of that size and 4 dp gaps even where they would have collapsed; *Given* zoom below 50%, *Then* glyphs of unselected elements are hidden (a count badge on the element remains) and the view-cluster toggle "Show constraint glyphs" overrides; *Given* 200 constraints on one sheet laid out at random, *Then* no two visible glyph targets overlap (property test), and glyphs keep 3:1 contrast.
- UX criteria owned by this story, cited literally (each has a test row in validation.md): UX-31 on a sheet with constraints or a technical sheet the status line is always one of three states and a live region, and absent on a plain diagram sheet; UX-32 an over-constraining add is rejected, geometry unchanged, naming the conflict, with Remove, Make reference, Show conflicts and Esc as Remove; UX-33 constraint state by glyph or dash plus text, never colour alone; UX-34 list rows and geometry highlight each other; UX-37 invalid Constrain items are disabled with a reason, not hidden.
**Files**: `UI2/ConstraintPanel.kt`, `UI2/ConstraintGlyphs.kt`, `AT/sketch/v2/ConstraintPanelTest.kt`, `AT/sketch/v2/UxConstraintsAcceptanceTest.kt` (new; UX-30, UX-81, UX-83, UX-84, UX-106)
##### Task 4.2.3a: Panel + list highlight (M)
##### Task 4.2.3b: Glyph overlay + status live region + Constrain flyout (and its compact-width dock button and overflow entry, UX-118) shown for 1+ selected elements, with per-item disabled reasons from `ConstraintEligibility`, and an always-present Constraints tab with its empty state (S)
##### Task 4.2.3c: Glyph collapse into count badges, selected-element fan-out, zoom rule, "Show constraint glyphs" toggle, overlap property test (S)

#### Story 4.2.4: Projection rounding and fusion mismatch surfacing
(The projection source and per-sheet fusion dispatch were moved to Story 1.4.5 and must already exist; this story only adds solver-aware rounding reports.)
This story is NOT held back by the A1 gate or by an A6 DEFER (D-46): it has no dependency edge from Task 6.2.4e, it is the no-regression surface that reports when a converted sheet no longer matches its fusion dimensions (metric 6), and it is therefore built in every gate outcome and is not part of the gated list or the "Job 2 first" pivot cost.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Rounding only at projection; mismatch reported, not snapped.
  - *Given* a solved `Rect` width 100.03 units with a named `Sum` dimension needing 100, *When* projected, *Then* the primitive is rounded to sixteenths once, fusion verification reports "off by 1/16 on Wall A", and the UI says "N elements not used for dimensions" for `Dropped`.
**Files**: `V2/bridge/V2ToV1.kt` (extends), `RT/projection/SheetProjectionSource.kt` (extends), `BT/sketch/v2/ProjectionFusionTest.kt`
##### Task 4.2.4a: Rounding-once projection reports + fusion verification surfacing (S)

#### Story 4.2.5: Dimensions and zones surfaces in the v2 editor (lifts the conversion refusal of Story 1.1.4; needs 1.4.2, which converts the sheets it edits)
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- The v2 editor edits named dimensions and zones on the model-level technical data using the existing components.
  - *Given* a converted sheet with zone `Kitchen` and named dimension `Wall A = 10ft`, *When* the user edits `Wall A` in `DimensionField` and a zone's bounds, *Then* the model's `dimensions[]`/zone entries change (one model write, same autosave), the unedited dimensions, zones and scale bytes are unchanged, and fusion/CSV/digest output equals the output a v1 edit of the same values produces (dimensions have no primitive reference, so output equality is the criterion, not id resolution). After each commit the editor re-runs `ZoneConstraints.resolve` exactly as the v1 editor does, and a test asserts the zone check results match v1 for the same inputs.
- With this story green, `ConversionPolicy.refusal` returns null (Story 1.1.4 predicate), and the Task 1.4.2c technical-data golden runs against the real policy instead of the test-only override.
**Files**: `UI2/TechnicalDataPanel.kt`, `RT/convert/ConversionPolicy.kt`, `AT/sketch/v2/TechnicalDataPanelTest.kt`
##### Task 4.2.5a: Panel reusing v1 `DimensionField`/zone components + policy flip (M)

#### Story 4.2.6: Sub-element selection for constraint targets (UX-98; Task a needs 4.2.1 and 1.3.3 only, because the model and `PartHitTester` use no non-drag layer (engineering E1); Task b needs 4.2.6a, 3.6.3a (non-drag layer), 4.2.3 and the A1 gate)
**As a** user sketching technical geometry, **I want** to pick an endpoint, vertex, centre or edge as a constraint target, **so that** Coincident, Distance, Fixed, Symmetric and arc/circle tangency are expressible exactly, with or without dragging.
**Design**: a constraint operand is a `ConstraintTarget(elementId, part)`; parts per kind: Line/polyline Start, End, Vertex(i), Edge(i); Rectangle Vertex(0..3), Edge(0..3), Centre; Circle Centre and Whole (circumference); Arc Centre, ArcStart, ArcEnd, Whole; Point Whole. Each constraint kind declares which part classes it accepts (Coincident: two point parts; Distance: point-point or point-edge; Fixed: a point part or Whole; Horizontal/Vertical: an edge or two point parts; Tangent: Edge/Whole of line, circle, arc; Symmetric: two point parts and an edge). The part is stored in the constraint fields of `SheetCodecV2` (default `Whole`).
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Hit-test priority is defined and pure.
  - *Given* a point within tolerance of both a line's endpoint and its body, *Then* `PartHitTester.hitPart` returns the endpoint: priority is point-like parts (Point element, endpoints, vertices, centres, arc ends) over Edge over Whole, then nearest distance, then topmost z; tolerance is 6 px / zoom for mouse and stylus and 22 dp / zoom for touch (half of the 44 dp `TouchTargetFloor`); elements on hidden layers are never targets and locked-layer elements are (constraints do not move them); *Given* an endpoint shared by two coincident lines, *Then* the topmost element's endpoint is returned and the picker lists the other as "also: Line 4 start".
- Candidates are highlighted without relying on colour.
  - *Given* the picker is armed (after choosing a constraint that needs sub-targets from the Constrain menu), *Then* every valid target of the selected and nearby elements shows a 24 dp marker (square = endpoint or vertex, circle = centre, bar = edge; not colour alone) and the hovered or focused one gets a label chip "Endpoint of Line 3" / "Centre of Circle 1" / "Edge of Rectangle 2", announced politely; targets invalid for the armed constraint are not shown (their reason is exposed through touch help, Story 3.6.7).
- Picking has a non-drag and a keyboard path.
  - *Given* the armed picker, *Then* it works by tapping or clicking a marker (no drag), by Tab/Shift+Tab and arrow keys cycling the markers in a fixed order (element z order, then Start, End, Centre, Edges), Enter picks, Esc cancels, and by the "Pick target" list (every target of the selected and nearby elements grouped by element name: "Line 3 start", "Line 3 end", "Line 3 edge", "Circle 1 centre"; virtualised), which is also the screen-reader path.
- A constraint is created from two picks.
  - *Given* Coincident armed, Line 1 End picked, then Line 2 Start picked, *Then* one Coincident between those parts is added as one undo step and the toast reads "Constraint added." and its Details "Added Coincident: Line 1 end, Line 2 start"; picking the same part twice, or two parts that are already coincident, shows "Pick a different target" and changes nothing.
- Whole-element selection is unchanged.
  - *Given* the Select tool and no armed picker, *Then* a click or tap selects the whole element as before; sub-element picking happens only while a picker is armed; with a selected line, a Constrain item that needs one point (Fixed, Horizontal between points) arms the picker immediately.
- The armed picker is cancellable by touch (UX-98; D-17). *Given* the picker is armed on a touch device with no keyboard, *Then* a visible "Cancel" chip (hit area per the `TouchTargetFloor`) sits beside the status text "Pick the first point"; activating it disarms the picker, removes the markers and commits nothing; Esc does the same on a keyboard; tapping empty canvas does NOT cancel, so a mis-tap cannot lose a first pick.
**Files**: `V2/constraints/ConstraintTarget.kt`, `V2/hit/PartHitTester.kt`, `UI2/ConstraintTargetOverlay.kt`, `UI2/TargetListPicker.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/ConstraintTargetTest.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/PartHitTesterTest.kt`, `AT/sketch/v2/ConstraintTargetPickerTest.kt`
##### Task 4.2.6a: `ConstraintTarget`/`TargetPart` model, per-constraint accepted parts, codec field, `PartHitTester` with priority and tolerance, property test against brute force (M)
##### Task 4.2.6b: Armed-picker overlay with markers, label chips and the visible Cancel chip, keyboard cycle, "Pick target" list, announcements, two-pick creation (M)

#### Story 4.2.7: Edit an existing dimension value, Replace existing, and smart dimension suggestions (UX-99, UX-115; needs 4.2.1; Tasks b and d need 4.2.3 and the A1 gate)
**As a** user, **I want** to change the value of a dimension I already placed by tapping its label, **so that** I never have to delete and re-add it, and a duplicate dimension offers to replace the old one.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Edit in place.
  - *Given* a Distance 120 on Line 1 shown as the label "120" on the canvas and as a row, *When* the user taps or clicks the label (or focuses the glyph or row and presses Enter or F2), *Then* the `DimensionField` opens in place with the value selected, Enter commits through `SetDimensionValue(constraintId, value)` as ONE undo step that also commits the solver-moved geometry, Esc cancels, an invalid value ("Not a length. Try 12 3/16\"") keeps the field open and the old value; the label of a reference (non-driving) dimension is read-only with "Reference: driven by other constraints" and the action Make driving.
- Failure keeps the last good state.
  - *Given* the new value makes the sketch unsolvable or over-constrained, *Then* the old value and geometry stay and the message reads "Changing 120 to 40 can't be solved: it conflicts with Horizontal on Line 3" with actions Keep editing, Revert and Make reference.
- Replace existing.
  - *Given* a driving Distance 120 on Line 1 and the user adds a second Distance (length) on Line 1 with value 150, *When* the solver reports the two conflict, *Then* the rejection card offers Replace existing (edits the existing dimension to 150 in place, one undo step, no second constraint added) alongside Remove new, Make reference and Show conflicts; for a non-dimension conflict (Parallel vs Horizontal) Replace existing is not offered; *Given* the user chooses Distance on a target that already has a driving Distance, *Then* the existing label opens for editing instead of adding a duplicate (the duplicate is prevented before it can conflict).
- Smart dimension suggestions shorten the common path (UX-115; D-21). *Given* one line selected on a technical sheet, *Then* a chip "Length 6 in? [Dimension]" (current value in the sheet's unit) appears as a canvas chip above the status line; *Given* one circle, "Radius 1/2 in?"; *Given* a circle and a line selected, "Centre to edge 1 in?"; *When* the chip is activated by tap, click, or Enter or D while it has focus, *Then* the `DimensionField` opens on the new label with the current value selected, Enter commits a driving dimension through the same add path as the Constrain menu (duplicate redirect and Replace existing apply), and Esc commits nothing; *Given* a selection with no natural dimension (text, an arrow, two lines), *Then* no chip appears and the Constrain menu remains the path; *Given* the status shows N > 0 degrees of freedom and nothing is selected, *Then* the chip "Suggested: Width of Line 1 [Dimension]" offers the first unconstrained length or radius; the chip never adds a constraint until a typed value is committed. `BracketPlateUxScenarioTest` includes a smart-dimension variant that reaches 0 DOF in at most 34 actions on the mouse path (the Constrain-menu path stays within 42), under the ONE counting convention of ux.md section 9 (a typed value plus its Enter is one action; the Line tool is held with tool lock).
**Files**: `V2/constraints/SetDimensionValue.kt`, `V2/constraints/DimensionSuggestion.kt` (new), `UI2/DimensionLabelEditor.kt`, `UI2/DimensionSuggestionChip.kt` (new), `CT/sketch/v2/SetDimensionValueTest.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/DimensionSuggestionTest.kt` (new), `AT/sketch/v2/DimensionEditTest.kt`
##### Task 4.2.7a: `SetDimensionValue` command, duplicate-dimension redirect and Replace-existing logic in the add path (S)
##### Task 4.2.7b: Label tap/click/Enter/F2 editor, reference-dimension read-only state, failure card with Keep editing / Revert / Make reference (S)
##### Task 4.2.7c: `DimensionSuggestion` pure rules (which selection suggests which dimension and value) with `DimensionSuggestionTest` (S)
##### Task 4.2.7d: Suggestion chip UI, keyboard path (Enter or D), the "Suggested" chip for remaining freedom, and the bracket-plate smart-dimension variant bound (S); needs Task 4.2.7c, Story 4.2.3 and the A1 gate

# Phase 5: Interop and export

### Epic 5.1: `.excalidraw`
#### Story 5.1.1: Bounded tolerant parser and sanitizer
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Bounds and rejects.
  - *Given* a 21 MB file (over `SceneLimits.maxImportBytes`, 20 MB), *Then* it is refused before parsing with the stated cap and the parser call counter stays 0; *Given* depth 200 nesting, 10,000 elements, a NaN coordinate string, duplicate ids, a cyclic `frameId`, and an `svg+xml` data URL, *Then* the result is `Either.Left(reason)` or a sanitised scene with an `ImportReport`, within the time/memory budget, never an exception (fuzz).
- No network.
  - *Given* the import package, *Then* `SketchImportImportDirectionTest` proves it imports no HTTP client; `link`/`embeddable` URLs remain inert text.
- A time cap that works on single-threaded wasmJs (engineering gap 11). `wasmJs` has one thread, so a "background dispatcher" gives neither isolation nor cancellation there (CLAUDE.md dispatcher matrix: Default on WASM). The parser therefore takes an injected `ParseBudget` (clock + deadline, default 5 s on every target, `SceneLimits.maxParseMillis`) and COOPERATES: it checks the budget and calls `yield()` at least every 256 elements and every 64 KB of input, and returns `Either.Left(ImportTimeout)` as soon as the deadline passes or the coroutine is cancelled.
  - *Given* a fake clock that advances 1 s per yield and a 10,000-element file, *When* the parser runs, *Then* it returns `Left(ImportTimeout)` after at most 5 yields past the deadline, never blocks for more than one 256-element slice (asserted by the fake clock's maximum gap), and a cancel issued between slices stops it within one slice.
- No single uninterruptible decode call (triad repair iteration 4, E1; Task 5.1.1e). `Json.parseToJsonElement` on a whole 20 MB text is ONE call that no yield can interrupt, so the budget and the timer-callback test above could not pass for a large file. CHOSEN design (one code path on every target): the importer never decodes the file as one JSON text. A `JsonSpanScanner` walks the file BYTES (never one 20 MB `String`) with a small state machine (string and escape state, brace and bracket depth, no allocation per byte), yielding every 64 KB, and cuts the top-level object into spans: each key of the top-level object and each element of the `elements` array is one span (a `files` entry is split by the `files` rule below), and only a span is handed to `Json.parseToJsonElement` (after `decodeToString` of that span alone). A non-binary span larger than `SceneLimits.maxImportSpanBytes` (2 MB) is refused with the reason `ImportSpanTooLarge` (one oversized element is dropped into the `ImportReport` and the rest import; an oversized top-level span other than `elements`/`files` refuses the file) so the largest single decode call is bounded by 2 MB, not 20 MB. A `files` entry's `dataURL` base64 payload is NOT JSON-decoded (the `files` rule below): it is base64-decoded in 64 KB slices with a `yield()` between slices, so a 15 MB embedded image costs many short slices, never one long call. Alternative rejected: a wasm-only smaller `maxImportBytes` (for example 2 MB) is simpler but refuses on web every legitimate file that carries an image (such files are routinely over 2 MB), creating a platform asymmetry in what "supported" means, and a single 2 MB decode would still be one uninterruptible call; the scanner removes the cost instead of lowering the cap.
  - *Given* a synthetic 20 MB file (one `elements` array of 4,000 elements of at most 1 KB each plus one `files` entry carrying a 15 MB base64 `dataURL` and small other members, about 19 MB in total), *When* the importer runs in `commonTest` with a counting decoder and a fake clock, *Then* the largest string handed to `parseToJsonElement` is at most `maxImportSpanBytes` (asserted by a call-size counter, the structural proof), the byte count processed between two `yield()` calls is at most 64 KB plus one span, the base64 payload is decoded in slices of at most 64 KB, and the result equals the whole-file oracle (below); *Given* an element of 2.5 MB, *Then* it is dropped with `ImportSpanTooLarge` and the other elements import; *Given* a 3 MB top-level `appState` span, *Then* the file is refused with the cap named.
  - The `files` rule (ONE statement; replaces the earlier "each entry of the files map is one span"): the scanner descends into every `files` entry and treats ONLY a member named `dataURL` whose value is a JSON string as a raw byte range (checked to contain no escapes other than `\/`; an entry whose `dataURL` contains any other escape is NOT raw-ranged and not decoded: the image becomes a placeholder with the reason `DataUrlEscaped`, class 9 below; any member order, so `dataURL` before or after `mimeType`, `id` and `created` works); the REMAINING members of the entry are re-assembled from their byte ranges into one small object, decoded with `parseToJsonElement`, and the 2 MB `maxImportSpanBytes` cap applies to those remaining members (a 3 MB unknown member makes that one entry `ImportSpanTooLarge`, the image becomes a placeholder and the rest import). A non-string `dataURL` is treated as missing. The raw range counts against `maxImageBytes` (compressed) before any base64 decode, and the header probe of the next criterion reads only the first bytes.
  - Depth is enforced in the scanner BEFORE decode (E4; `SceneLimits.maxJsonDepth` = 64, counted from the document root: the root object is depth 1, the `elements` array 2, an element object 3). The scanner tracks bracket and brace depth per byte; a span whose depth exceeds the limit is refused with `ImportTooDeep` without being handed to the decoder (an over-depth element is dropped into the `ImportReport`, an over-depth top-level span other than `elements` or `files` refuses the file). *Given* an element that is a 2 MB span of nested `[` (within the span cap), *Then* it is dropped with `ImportTooDeep`, the decoder call counter shows 0 calls for it, no stack overflow occurs, and the other elements import.
  - Input API (E5; one source type for file, drop and clipboard): `ExcalidrawParser.parse(source: ImportSource, budget: ParseBudget)`, where `ImportSource` is a byte source with `size` and `read(offset, length)` and the factories `ImportSource.ofBytes(ByteArray)` (file picker and drag-and-drop: the platform seam returns bytes, on wasm via `File.arrayBuffer()`) and `ImportSource.ofText(String)` (clipboard paste, Story 5.1.4: a cheap first-character check comes first, so ordinary text paste costs nothing: the first non-whitespace character after an optional BOM must be `{`, else the source is `NotExcalidraw` and nothing is encoded; then the String is refused before any copy when `length > maxImportBytes` (a string of N chars encodes to at least N bytes), otherwise UTF-8 encoded ONCE into a byte array, and the ENCODED size is checked against `maxImportBytes` again before scanning, because a char can encode to up to 3 bytes (so 20 million chars can be 60 MB); no second 20 MB `String` is ever built). A leading UTF-8 byte-order mark (or a leading U+FEFF in a String) is skipped; a BOM anywhere else is malformed JSON.
  - Whole-file oracle and equivalence classes (E3). Equality with a whole-file `Json.parseToJsonElement` holds only for well-formed, in-limit input, so the differential test (`JsonSpanScannerTest`, over the six Story 0.1.5 fixtures plus generated cases) states each class explicitly. The oracle is: decode the whole file with kotlinx `parseToJsonElement` (last duplicate key wins), then run the same field extraction. (1) Well-formed in-limit input (fixtures, generated): the scanner path and the oracle produce EQUAL scenes and reports. (2) Malformed input: BOTH paths reject with `MalformedJson` (accept/reject equivalence; the scanner may detect it at a different byte, the reason code is the same). The generated families, each applied at every position of a small fixture by a generator (not a hand list): truncation at every byte offset; one extra or one missing `{`, `}`, `[` or `]`; trailing garbage after the root; a bad escape (`\q`, a short `\u12`); an unquoted key; a single-quoted string; a trailing comma; a bare `NaN` or `Infinity`; a raw control character inside a string; a BOM that is not first; empty input; a root that is not an object. (3) Duplicate top-level keys (two `elements` arrays, two `files` objects): LAST wins in both, because the scanner records span offsets per key and decodes only the winning one. (4) Duplicate ids inside `files`: last wins, as kotlinx does. (5) Escaped keys (`"\u0065lements"`): keys are unescaped before comparison, so they are recognised as `elements` in both paths. (6) `elements` that is not an array (object, null, number) and `files` that is not an object: both paths ignore it with the report reasons `ElementsNotAnArray` and `FilesNotAnObject`. (7) A leading BOM: accepted and skipped by both. (8) Over-limit input (an over-2 MB span, over-depth, over-count): the oracle has no span cap, so the class is NOT compared for equality; the expectation is the oracle's result minus the dropped spans, each with its stated reason (`ImportSpanTooLarge`, `ImportTooDeep`). (9) A `files` entry whose `dataURL` carries an escape other than `\/` (for example `\u003d`): the oracle decodes the escapes and would import the image, the scanner does not raw-range it, so this class is NOT compared for equality either; the expectation is the oracle's result with that image replaced by a placeholder carrying `DataUrlEscaped` (D-47), and every other entry equal.
  - What `wasmJsBrowserTest` can and cannot prove (stated so the AC is not over-claimed): *Given* the same 20 MB file on the real single wasm thread (the test body runs on the real dispatcher with real timers; `kotlinx-coroutines-test` virtual time is NOT used, because it would skip the event loop), *When* a `setTimeout(0)` callback is scheduled before the parse starts, *Then* the callback fires before the parse finishes (the event loop is not starved: this is a real, binary check), and the maximum wall-clock gap between consecutive yields is RECORDED into `sketch-engine-budgets.json` as `sketch.import_max_yield_gap_ms` and `sketch.import_span_decode_ms_per_mb`; the wall-clock figure is advisory and not a pass/fail assertion (it depends on the host), but if the recorded worst single-span decode on wasm exceeds 100 ms, `maxImportSpanBytes` is lowered in `SceneLimits` (and ADR-002's addendum) before release, never the test loosened.
- Embedded images have explicit decoded-size limits (engineering gap 13; `SceneLimits.maxImageSidePx = 8192`, `maxImagePixels = 16,000,000`, `maxDecodedImageBytesPerSheet = 64 MB`, owner-confirmable defaults D-5). The decoded size is derived from the image HEADER (PNG IHDR, JPEG SOF, WebP, GIF) BEFORE any pixel decode, so a small compressed file that declares huge dimensions (a decompression bomb) is refused without allocating.
  - *Given* a 4 KB PNG whose IHDR declares 60,000 x 60,000, a JPEG whose SOF declares 20,000 x 20,000, a data URL with a mismatched declared mime and bytes, and 40 images that each fit individually but together exceed 64 MB decoded, *Then* the first two and the mismatch are replaced by labelled placeholders with an `ImportReport` reason code (`ImageTooLarge`, `ImageMimeMismatch`), the 40 are admitted only until the per-sheet decoded budget is reached (the rest become placeholders), no pixel decode of the oversize images is attempted (decoder call counter 0), and no exception escapes.
- Every untrusted entry shares one bounded parser (pre-mortem P3 #9).
  - *Given* the importer, clipboard paste (Story 5.1.4), library import (Story 3.5.6b) and the `extras` passthrough, *Then* all go through the same `SceneLimits`-checked parse (extras bytes count against the byte budget), run off the main thread with a time and memory cap, and the fuzz suite of this story covers the importer and clipboard entry points as well as `SheetCodecV2`.
- Dependencies (engineering E3): Task 1.2.4a (`SceneLimits`) and the Story 0.1.5 fixtures only. The parser and sanitiser import no scene types and no renderer, so Story 5.1.1 starts in W3; the mapper is the separate Story 5.1.2.
- UX criteria owned by this story, cited literally (each has a test row in validation.md): UX-52 a file over the size cap is refused before parsing with the cap stated.
**Files**: `XCAL/ExcalidrawParser.kt` (limits come from `V2/scene/SceneLimits.kt`; there is no `ImportLimits.kt`), `XCAL/JsonSpanScanner.kt` (new; E1), `XCAL/ImportSource.kt` (new; byte-or-text input), `CT/sketch/excalidraw/JsonSpanScannerTest.kt` (new; E1), `CT/sketch/excalidraw/LibraryImportSpanTest.kt` (new; Task 5.1.1g), `kmp/src/wasmJsTest/kotlin/dev/stapler/stelekit/sketch/excalidraw/JsonSpanScannerWasmTest.kt` (new; Task 5.1.1f), `CT/sketch/excalidraw/ParserFuzzTest.kt`, `BT/sketch/excalidraw/SketchImportImportDirectionTest.kt`
##### Task 5.1.1a: `ExcalidrawScene` types (all-optional, `ImportedDataState`-like) + size/depth/count caps (M)
##### Task 5.1.1b: Sanitiser (finite/range, ids remap, mime allow-list, image decode limits) (M)
##### Task 5.1.1c: Fuzz + import-direction tests; `Throwable` guard returns Left (S)
##### Task 5.1.1d: `ParseBudget` cooperative-yield time cap (wasm-safe) and header-based decoded-image limits in `SceneLimits` with `ParserBudgetTest` and `ImageBombTest` (S)
##### Task 5.1.1e: `JsonSpanScanner` and `ImportSource` (byte-level span splitter with the in-scanner depth limit, the `files` member rule, 64 KB yields and the per-span decode cap) plus the whole-file oracle differential over the nine equivalence classes and the reason codes `ImportSpanTooLarge`, `ImportTooDeep`, `MalformedJson`, `ElementsNotAnArray`, `FilesNotAnObject` (M)
##### Task 5.1.1g: Library mode of `JsonSpanScanner` (one span per library item, the `maxLibraryBytes` and `maxImportBytes` caps, image items as placeholders) with `LibraryImportSpanTest` over a generated 9 MB library and an over-cap item (S)
##### Task 5.1.1f: wasm timer-callback test (run on the REAL wasm dispatcher and event loop with real `setTimeout`, never `runTest` virtual time, which skips the event loop the test is about) and import budget recording for the span scanner (`sketch.import_max_yield_gap_ms`, `sketch.import_span_decode_ms_per_mb`), plus one library-mode run (a generated 9 MB library of 120 items through the same real-timer test, recording `sketch.import_library_max_yield_gap_ms`, because library mode yields per item and is the other long-running path) (S); split from Task 5.1.1e in iteration 5

#### Story 5.1.2: Element mapping (import)
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Fixtures import with a report.
  - *Given* each Task 0.1.5a fixture, *When* imported, *Then* element kinds, geometry, text, bindings (new `fixedPoint` and legacy `focus/gap`) and z-order match expectations, `isDeleted` elements are dropped, and unknown types/arrowheads/fills are skipped or mapped per the report ("Imported 41 of 44. Skipped: 2 embeddables, 1 unsupported arrowhead"); OD-6 decides placeholders.
- Dependencies (engineering E3): Story 5.1.1 (parser output types), Story 1.2.1 (scene model), Story 1.2.3 (`reconcile`), Story 1.3.1 (`ImportScene` is a `Command`), Task 3.5.7a (`NameCounters`: import assigns ordinals), Story 2.1.1 (arrowhead kinds) and Story 0.1.5; it therefore sits in W7, not W3.
- UX criteria owned by this story, cited literally (each has a test row in validation.md): UX-55 missing image binaries become labelled placeholders, linked images state they were not downloaded.
**Files**: `XCAL/ExcalidrawImporter.kt`, `CT/sketch/excalidraw/ImporterFixtureTest.kt`
##### Task 5.1.2a: Shapes/lines/arrows/text/freedraw/frame/image mapping; Excalidraw has no point or arc, so `PointElement`/`ArcElement` round trip through their export fallback (a tiny `ellipse` / a sampled `line`) tagged `customData.stelekit.kind` and are restored from that tag on import (OD-8) (M)
##### Task 5.1.2b: Bindings (both generations), `boundElements` reciprocity via `reconcile`, `index` to `ZKey` with array-order fallback (M)
##### Task 5.1.2c: Layer hint (`customData.stelekit.layerId`), `extras` retention, one undoable `ImportScene` command (S)

#### Story 5.1.3: Export and round trip
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Property round trip.
  - *Given* any generated scene of the supported subset, *When* exported then imported, *Then* element kinds, geometry (within 1e-9), text, bindings and z-order are preserved, and `version`/`versionNonce`/`updated` are regenerated deterministically so repeated saves do not churn diffs.
- Layer flattening per ADR-006; imported `customData.stelekit.layerId` hints are capped (`SceneLimits.maxLayerIdChars` 64, at most `SceneLimits.maxLayers` 64 distinct layers) and ignored beyond that.
- Point and arc survive a round trip (OD-8).
  - *Given* a scene with a `PointElement` and an `ArcElement`, *When* exported then imported, *Then* both come back as the same kinds with geometry within 1e-9 (the exported file shows a tiny ellipse and a sampled line to other Excalidraw readers, and `customData.stelekit.kind` restores them).
- export(import(fixture)) preserves the supported subset (success metric 7).
  - *Given* each hand-authored fixture from Task 0.1.5a, *When* imported then exported then imported again, *Then* the second scene equals the first (kinds, geometry within 1e-9, text, bindings, z-order), and unsupported elements recorded in `extras` are emitted back unchanged.
- Images round trip.
  - *Given* the image fixture with `files` data, *When* imported, *Then* bytes are stored once via `ImageAssetStore` by sha256 and exported again, the exported `.excalidraw` contains the `files` entry with the same dataURL payload (mime allow-list png/jpeg/gif/webp, never `svg+xml`), and an image whose asset is missing exports a placeholder entry without failing.
- Fidelity pivot (requirements; Task 6.2.9d). *Given* any of the six fixtures fails exact-geometry round trip, *Then* its element kinds leave the stated supported subset before release; with four or fewer of six passing, import is labelled "beta" in the UI and export is off by default until fixed; the owner amends metric 7.
**Files**: `XCAL/ExcalidrawExporter.kt`, `CT/sketch/excalidraw/RoundTripPropertyTest.kt`, `CT/sketch/excalidraw/FixtureRoundTripTest.kt`
##### Task 5.1.3a: Exporter incl. `files` for images (M)
##### Task 5.1.3b: Property test + layer flatten test + `export(import(fixture))` and image round-trip tests (M)

#### Story 5.1.4: Paste Excalidraw clipboard JSON
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Text/plain Excalidraw clipboard payload pasted at the cursor creates elements via the importer limits (the clipboard text enters through `ImportSource.ofText`, Story 5.1.1: refused above `maxImportBytes` before any copy, else encoded to UTF-8 once and scanned like a file), ids remapped, one undo step; non-Excalidraw text does nothing.
  - *Given* a clipboard string `{"type":"excalidraw/clipboard","elements":[...3...]}`, *When* Ctrl+V at (200,100), *Then* 3 elements appear offset to the cursor.
- UX criteria owned by this story, cited literally (each has a test row in validation.md): UX-59 pasting Excalidraw clipboard JSON places elements at the cursor in one undo step, other text does nothing.
**Files**: `V2/clipboard/ExcalidrawClipboard.kt`, `CT/sketch/excalidraw/ClipboardTest.kt`
##### Task 5.1.4a: Detection + routing (S)

#### Story 5.1.5: File pick, save, and import UX
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Import runs off the main thread with cancel and a summary dialog with "Copy details"; the file picker uses the existing platform file seam; Android process death mid-import leaves the sheet unchanged.
- An import over the element cap offers three working choices (UX-53).
  - *Given* a file with 7,200 elements into a sheet with 100, *When* the import is attempted, *Then* the dialog "This drawing has 7,200 elements; the limit is 5,000." offers Import first 5,000 (adds exactly the first `maxElements - 100` elements by z-order, reports the rest as skipped), Import into a new sketch (creates a new sheet through `SheetSplitter`'s new-sheet path, Story 2.2.4, holding the first 5,000, never touching the current sheet) and Cancel (nothing changes); each is one undo step or creates nothing.
- Undo import is a button on the summary dialog (UX-56).
  - *Given* a finished import of 41 elements, *When* the summary dialog shows, *Then* it offers "Copy details", "Undo import" and "Done"; *When* "Undo import" is activated, *Then* the single `ImportScene` command is undone (the 41 elements and their library/asset additions are removed, the selection is cleared, the sheet equals its pre-import scene) and the dialog closes with a "Import undone" notice; *When* the user has already made another edit after the import and opens the dialog again from the notice history, *Then* "Undo import" is hidden (no out-of-order undo) and Done remains.
- A newer-Excalidraw-schema file proceeds with a notice and a Cancel before commit; dropping a file onto the desktop or web canvas starts the same flow (web uses a canvas-local drop handler, `CanvasFileDrop` in `wasmJsMain`, not the page-level drop target: BUG-004 is still open in `docs/bugs/open/`, and with Option W web import is enabled in every browser whose verdict is Available, D-104), and so does pasting Excalidraw clipboard JSON; on a platform where `V2StorageAvailable` is false (iOS, an OPFS-less browser, no store) the picker action, the drop and the paste show the `V2StorageUnavailable` reason and create nothing (UX-135, D-118).
- UX criteria owned by this story, cited literally (each has a test row in validation.md): UX-135 on a platform without file storage import, drop and paste show the reason and create nothing, a v2 embed is a labelled placeholder and a v2 copy source is refused with the reason (D-118); UX-50 import takes at most 3 steps and is one undo step; UX-51 a non-Excalidraw file shows the reason code, creates nothing, offers Choose another file and Close; UX-54 import is cancellable and leaves the sheet unchanged on cancel or process death.
**Files**: `UI2/ImportExportActions.kt`, `AT/sketch/v2/ImportFlowTest.kt`
##### Task 5.1.5a: Actions + dialog (M)
##### Task 5.1.5b: Over-cap options (first 5,000 / new sketch / cancel), newer-schema notice, canvas file drop and paste through the `CanvasFileDropHost` seam with its wasm handler, the storage-unavailable refusal (S)
##### Task 5.1.5c: "Undo import" button on the summary dialog and its out-of-order guard (XS)

### Epic 5.2: Export
#### Story 5.2.1: SVG from the draw list
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- All kinds render; text as `<text>/<tspan>` from persisted line breaks; frame clip as `clipPath`.
  - *Given* the architecture-15 fixture, *When* exported, *Then* output equals `golden/architecture-15.svg`; v1 sheets still use `SketchSvgWriter` unchanged.
- XML-safe output.
  - *Given* text elements containing `<`, `&`, `"`, `'`, `]]>`, a `\u0000` control character and an emoji, *When* exported (property test over generated strings), *Then* the SVG parses as well-formed XML, the decoded text equals the original (control characters stripped by documented rule), and an image whose bytes or mime are `svg+xml` is refused (placeholder `<rect>`, never embedded), with `fileId`/attribute values escaped.
**Files**: `V2/export/SvgExporter.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/SvgExporterGoldenTest.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/SvgEscapingPropertyTest.kt`
##### Task 5.2.1a: SVG writer over DrawOps incl. Sketchy paths and images (M)

#### Story 5.2.2: PNG export
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Scale 1x/2x/3x, background toggle, selection-only.
  - *Given* a 200x100 scene at 2x, *When* exported, *Then* PNG bytes decode to 400x200 on JVM and Android; wasm follows the Spike 0.1.3c result (offscreen or Canvas element).
- Every element kind actually renders (success metric 7).
  - *Given* a scene with one of each kind (rect, diamond, ellipse, line, arrow, freedraw, text, image, frame) in Clean and Sketchy, *When* exported to PNG, *Then* for each kind the decoded pixels inside that element's bounds are non-blank (at least 1% non-background pixels) and match the in-process draw-list render within the Task 2.1.3c tolerance; on targets where pixel decode is not available (per Spike 0.1.3c/e) the check is draw-list equality plus the owner check.
- A PNG over `SceneLimits.maxPngSidePx` (16,000 px per side) is refused with the reason, never attempted (Story 5.2.3 offers Use 1x).
**Files**: `V2/export/PngExporter.kt`, `JT/sketch/v2/PngExportTest.kt` (new, `// jvmTest-reason: pixels`; per-kind non-blank, 400x200 at 2x, side-cap refusal), `CM/platform/PngEncoder.kt` (expect), `kmp/src/{jvmMain,androidMain,iosMain,wasmJsMain}/.../PngEncoder.*.kt` (actual), tests

##### Task 5.2.2b: wasmJs + iOS actuals (S)

#### Story 5.2.3: Export frame/selection and dialog
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Frame export crops to the frame bounds and clips children; "only selected" exports just selection.
- An impossible export shows the reason and a one-tap fix (UX-57).
  - *Given* a 12,000x8,000 unit scene at 3x (36,000 px wide, over `SceneLimits.maxPngSidePx`), *When* Export is pressed, *Then* the dialog stays open with "This image would be 36,000 px wide. Choose 1x or export a frame." and actions Use 1x (sets 1x and re-enables Export when the result fits) and Cancel; an encode/OOM failure shows "Couldn't create the image. Try a smaller scale." with Retry and Cancel and the scene is unaffected; a blocked web download shows "Your browser blocked the download." with Try again; Selection scope is disabled with a reason when nothing is selected; the format list offers PNG (1x/2x/3x, background toggle), SVG, .excalidraw, .excalidrawlib and CSV (CSV only when the sheet has dimensions); Style offers As drawn or Clean.
- Layers flattened notice (UX-58).
  - *Given* a sheet with 2 layers, *When* the .excalidraw format is selected, *Then* the line "Layers are flattened in the exported file (ADR-006)." is shown; with one layer it is not.
- Export success feedback (UX-114). *Given* an export completes, *Then* a notice names the file and where it went (the `ExportSaved` toast "File exported." with [Open] and, under More, Copy path where the platform can open it; its Details name "architecture.png in Downloads"; on web the downloaded file name and "Check your downloads"), announced politely, and shown only on success (cancel and failure use the error states).
**Files**: `UI2/ExportDialog.kt`, `AT/sketch/v2/ExportDialogTest.kt` (new; UX-57, UX-58), `AT/sketch/v2/SuccessFeedbackTest.kt` (new; UX-114: export, library add and insert, restore notices)
##### Task 5.2.3a: Dialog + frame/selection scopes (S)
##### Task 5.2.3b: Impossible-export fixes (Use 1x, OOM retry, blocked download), Style option, flatten notice (S)

# Phase 6: Integration and rollout

### Epic 6.1: Embeds and cross-graph
#### Story 6.1.1: Embed/preview dispatch on schema
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- One dispatcher.
  - *Given* a journal embed of `s1` (schema 1) and `s2` (schema 2), *When* rendered, *Then* `SketchPreview` renders `s1` and the v2 draw list renders `s2`; a `NewerSchema` project shows "Update SteleKit to view" instead of crashing; thumbnails of 500-element sheets build within the budget.
- A missing sketch is not a dead end (UX-61).
  - *Given* an embed whose slug was deleted, *When* rendered, *Then* it shows "Sketch not found" with Remove embed (removes the embed block text only) and Choose another (opens the sketch picker); nothing crashes and no sidecar file is created.
- No file storage is not a crash (UX-135; D-118). *Given* a v2 embed while `V2StorageAvailable` is false (iOS, an OPFS-less browser, no store), *Then* the embed renders the labelled placeholder "New-editor sketch. Open it in the desktop or Android app." with [Open page], builds no thumbnail and writes nothing; *Given* a browser whose OPFS verdict is Available, *Then* the v2 draw-list preview renders from the preloaded sidecar as on desktop (`EmbedDispatchTest`).
- UX criteria owned by this story, cited literally (each has a test row in validation.md): UX-135 the storage-unavailable embed placeholder (above); UX-60 a journal embed of a v1 and a v2 sketch both render; a newer-schema project shows "Update SteleKit to view" and never crashes.
**Files**: `CM/ui/sketch/SketchRenderDispatcher.kt`, `CM/ui/sketch/SketchEmbedWiring.kt`, `CM/ui/sketch/SketchPreviewLoader.kt`, `JT/sketch/v2/EmbedDispatchTest.kt` (`// jvmTest-reason: pixels`; includes the missing-sketch case)
##### Task 6.1.1a: Dispatcher + wiring (S)
##### Task 6.1.1b: Preview fit/thumbnail for v2 (S); dispatch keys on the DB `schema_version` mirror, file wins
##### Task 6.1.1c: "Sketch not found" state with Remove embed / Choose another (XS)

#### Story 6.1.2: Cross-graph copy via `TargetWriterRouter`
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Whole-bundle asset copy, never edit.
  - *Given* sketch `kitchen` in graph A and inactive graph B without that slug, *When* "Copy sketch to graph" runs, *Then* the files are created by `MarkdownTargetWriter`-path asset copy under `GraphWriteLock(B)`; *Given* B already has `kitchen`, *Then* the copy is refused with `SlugExists`; no existing file in B is modified; no `GraphWriteLock` is held while acquiring another.
- Image assets travel with the sketch.
  - *Given* sketch `kitchen` whose v2 sheet references `fileId` F1 and F2 (F2's asset file missing in A), *When* copied to graph B, *Then* the asset files for F1 are copied to B's assets under `GraphWriteLock(B)` (content-addressed, so an existing identical file is left untouched and a differing existing file is never modified: the copy refuses with `AssetConflict`), F2 is reported as a missing-asset placeholder in the result, and no dangling reference is left unreported.
- Success names the destination and offers Open (UX-63).
  - *Given* a successful copy of `kitchen` to graph B, *Then* the result model carries the destination graph name and an `Open` action target ("Copied to B." with Open); a name collision returns `SlugExists` with Rename and copy and Cancel and changes nothing; an unwritable target is listed disabled with its `TargetWriterCapabilities` reason; a failure shows "Couldn't copy" with Retry and Cancel and leaves no partial bundle.
- A source on a platform without file storage is refused (UX-135; D-118). *Given* a v2 sketch whose source graph is on a platform where `V2StorageAvailable` is false, *When* copy is requested, *Then* the copy is refused with the `V2StorageUnavailable` reason and nothing is written; *Given* an Available browser, *Then* the copy is as on desktop to every target `TargetWriterCapabilities` reports writable (a graph the browser has not preloaded is shown disabled with its reason, INFERRED, the capability check decides; `SketchBundleCopyTest`).
- UX criteria owned by this story, cited literally (each has a test row in validation.md): UX-62 copy to graph refuses a name collision with "already exists" and changes nothing; unwritable targets are shown disabled with a reason.
**Files**: `CM/merge/SketchBundleCopy.kt`, `CM/merge/TargetWriterRouter.kt` (new method only), `BT/merge/SketchBundleCopyTest.kt` (added to `TargetWriterContractSuite` runs for both writers)
##### Task 6.1.2a: Router method + writers (M)
##### Task 6.1.2b: Contract suite + refusal tests (S)
##### Task 6.1.2c: Result model (destination, Open action, Rename and copy) and its dialog (XS)

### Epic 6.2: Quality gates and rollout
#### Story 6.2.1: Wiring, registration and audits
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- All new seams are classified and covered (D-146). Five of the 14 names are interfaces on `WiringAudit.SEAMS` (`TextMetricsProvider`, `SketchWriterLease`, `BrowserPersistence`, `SketchDurability`, `PageLifecycle`): each implementation is constructed in a composition root by the task that adds its interface to the list or by the task that declares it, each commonMain implementation has `wiring-catalog.txt` lines and an entry-point test in `androidUnitTest` and `jvmTest` (the only source sets with a fixture; businessTest cannot call `WiredAppFixture` or `DesktopWiredAppFixture`) added by a task after the list task, and the wasm-only implementations have no catalog line (D-141); the nine others (`StrokeStyleProvider`, `SheetSaver`, `SolveRunner`, `PngEncoder`, `SketchEditorRouter`, `SheetLeaseRegistry`, `SheetConverter`, `SheetProjectionSource`, `ImageAssetStore`) are classes or pure strategies, never on the list and never catalogued, and the two reachability tests of Task 6.2.1a drive the real fixtures and assert each is reachable; `first-open-screens.txt` and `FIRST_OPEN_SCREENS` gain "SketchScreenV2"; no new baseline entries; all test classes registered.

#### Story 6.2.2: Benchmarks and budgets
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Budgets enforced.
  - *Given* the 500-element scene and the 100-constraint sketch, *When* the JVM benchmark runs in CI, *Then* frame p95 (at most 1.5x the empty-canvas p95 in the same run), solve ms, hit-test candidate counts, import ms, native decode ms, encode ms and save bytes for a 5,000-element sheet (`sketch.encode_ms_5000`, `sketch.save_bytes_5000`; pre-mortem P2 #6) are within `sketch-engine-budgets.json`; breach fails the job. Android/Pixel_Fold frame time is NOT a CI check (see Story 6.2.4c).

##### Task 6.2.2a: Harness + CI hook (M)

#### Story 6.2.3: Observability
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Log lines exist and carry ids only.
  - *Given* a failing solve on constraints c1,c2 of a sheet containing text "Secret", *Then* the log has `sketch.solve.failed kind=Conflict ids=c1,c2` and does not contain "Secret".
- No per-pointer-move logging (UX-73/UX-70 robustness).
  - *Given* 1,000 pointer-move events dispatched through the tool and adapter, *Then* zero `sketch.*` log lines are emitted.
- Each Observability line is emitted at its trigger (consistency C13).
  - *Given* a scripted run (tool switch, failing solve, failed and successful import, one conversion, a failed save, a rejected native sheet, a save conflict), *Then* the log contains exactly `sketch.tool.activated`, `sketch.solve.failed`, `sketch.import.failed`, `sketch.import.summary`, `sketch.migrate.outcome`, `sketch.persist.failed`, `sketch.sheet.rejected` and `sketch.save.conflict` once each with ids and counts only.
- Quality signals are counted locally (requirements.md "Flag and legacy-editor exit criteria"; D-26). *Given* a scripted session with 200 committed solves of which 3 return `NoConvergence`, one save conflict and one crash record read back from `last-crash.txt`, *Then* `sketch.quality v2Sessions=1 crashes=1 solves=200 noConvergence=3 conflicts=1` is emitted once per session end with counts only, the Settings counter shows the same figures, nothing is transmitted (no network seam is referenced), and no element text appears.
- Metric 8 counts authored sheets only (requirements.md metric 8; D-48). *Given* a scripted run that creates two new v2 sheets and draws 3 or more elements in one and 2 in the other, converts one v1 sheet and imports one `.excalidraw` file, *Then* `sketch.adoption authored=1 imported=1` is emitted: the sheet with fewer than 3 drawn elements, the conversion and the import are not authored, and the Settings readout shows the two counts on separate lines.
**Files**: `RT/telemetry/SketchLog.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/SketchLogTest.kt` (the pointer-move, per-line and authored-versus-imported cases are new tests in this class)
##### Task 6.2.3a: Log helper + no-user-text test (S)
##### Task 6.2.3b: `sketch.adoption` line (counts of AUTHORED v2 sheets and, separately, `.excalidraw` files imported, local only, no names; only authored sheets are the metric-8 numerator), the `sketch.quality` line of `QualitySignals` (v2 sessions, crashes, solves, `NoConvergence` solves, save conflicts) and the Settings counters for requirements metric 8 and the flag-exit criteria (S); reclassified from XS

#### Story 6.2.4: Scenario tests, parity gate and owner verification
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- 15-element architecture diagram scripted on JVM and Robolectric.
  - *Given* the event script (9 labelled boxes, 4 bound arrows, 1 frame, 1 group), *When* run through tools and commands, *Then* the final scene equals `golden/architecture-15.scene.json`, moving a box moves its arrows' endpoints, and draw list equals the golden in Clean and Sketchy.
- Owner checklist written to `project_plans/excalidraw-sketch-engine/implementation/owner-verification.md`: desktop pass, Pixel_Fold AVD pass (`~/Android/Sdk/emulator/emulator -avd Pixel_Fold -no-window -no-audio -no-snapshot`, `ANDROID_HOME=$HOME/Android/Sdk`), stylus pressure/hover/palm rejection, TalkBack, wasm browser pass; iOS deferred (compile-only).
- Parity gate: legacy editor retained until the v1 regression suite (every v1 capability in requirements Baseline) passes in v2.
- Early vertical slice, split in two because its first form could not run where it was placed (engineering blocker E1; pre-mortem P2 #8).
  - Headless slice at W7 (`HeadlessSliceScenarioTest`, businessTest, needs 1.1.3, 1.1.5, 1.3.2, 1.4.4 and nothing from the flag, router or painter): *Given* an empty `SketchDocumentV2` and the commands of Story 1.3.2 (add rect, add text, add arrow with bindings), *When* the scenario script applies them, `SheetSaver` writes through `SheetCodecV2` to a real-file store, the store is re-read and the sheet decoded in a fresh object graph, *Then* the reloaded scene equals the in-memory scene, the bound arrow endpoints are intact, `reconcile` is clean, the DB row carries `schemaVersion = 2`, and a following `SidecarReconciler.reconcile` is `Unchanged`. No UI, no flag, no render.
  - Rendered and flagged slice after 2.1.3 and 1.4.3 (`RenderedSliceScenarioTest`, Task 6.2.4g, W9): the same scenario created as a NEW v2 sheet through the router with the flag on, reopened, and Clean-rendered (draw-list equality, plus a non-blank pixel check on JVM). The owner's real diagram and the go/no-go decision on it are Task 6.2.4e (the A1 gate); the gated UI stories do not start before it.
- A1 go/no-go gate (Task 6.2.4e; owner Tyler; wave W10; requirements.md "A1 go/no-go gate").
  - *Given* the rendered slice (Task 6.2.4g) and the Task 0.1.7c list, *When* the owner draws one diagram from the list in-app (a reachable item under the row's ceiling, its platform (`desktop`, `android`, or `web` in a browser whose Spike 0.1.8 verdict is Available; never `ios`) and its project kind (`new`, `existing-classic`, `existing-classic-technical` or `existing-v2`) recorded in `a1-gate.md`, the technical kind being unreachable under every ceiling; with no reachable item the gate is recorded "unmeasured under the cap" and never PROCEEDs, requirements.md "Reachable demand") and answers finished / time / blocker / would-continue, *Then* the owner also draws the same diagram in excalidraw.com on the same device (in-app run first; both times recorded; n = 1 by design) and `implementation/a1-gate.md` records, with the precedence UNMEASURED, then PIVOT, then ADJUST, then PROCEED: UNMEASURED (no listed item is reachable under the row's ceiling and platforms: no run is made, nothing of the gated set is released and the owner chooses PIVOT or records a changed A4 result), PROCEED (finished, in-app time at most 2.0x the excalidraw.com time of the same diagram, would continue, no named blocker classed a must-fix defect), ADJUST (finished and would continue but the ratio exceeds 2.0, or any named must-fix defect; the blockers become defects against their owning stories and the gate repeats once; a SECOND ADJUST is an owner decision recorded in `a1-gate.md`: PROCEED with the named defects kept, or PIVOT; how the result combines with A4 and A6 is the table "Combined outcomes: A1 x A4 x A6" of requirements.md, D-48) or PIVOT (not finished, or would not continue; gated stories stop, pure engines stay, the owner chooses Job 2 first, import/export first, or v1 extended and an ADR records it; Job 2 first is not offered while the A6 result in `a6-job2-demand.md` is DEFER, and Story 4.2.4 is never gated, D-46); until the file records PROCEED, Stories 4.2.2, 4.2.3, 4.2.5, 4.2.6b, 4.2.7b, 4.2.7d, 5.1.5, 5.2.3 and Task 6.2.5c do not start.
- Bracket-plate UX scenario (requirements Job 2; ux.md section 9).
  - *Given* a scaled/technical sheet (auto-constrain ON), *When* the scenario script draws four lines as a closed outline with endpoint snaps, picks Fixed on the lower-left corner vertex, Distance 6 in on the bottom edge and 4 in on the left edge, draws a circle and sets Radius 1/2 in and the centre Distance 1 in from the left edge and from the bottom edge (every target chosen through the Story 4.2.6 picker, once by tap and once by keyboard only), *Then* the status reads "Fully constrained (nothing can move)", dragging any corner moves nothing, an extra Parallel between a Horizontal and a Vertical edge is rejected naming the conflict ("Parallel conflicts with Vertical on Line 2"), editing the width label from 6 in to 7 in in place (Story 4.2.7) widens the plate by 1 in with the hole still 1 in from the left edge in one undo step, and the whole script completes within the action bound recorded in the test (at most 42 actions through the Constrain menu and at most 34 with the smart-dimension chips of Story 4.2.7, one counting convention, ux.md section 9 walkthrough; `BracketPlateUxScenarioTest`).

**Files**: `JT/sketch/v2/ArchitectureScenarioTest.kt`, `AT/sketch/v2/ArchitectureScenarioAndroidTest.kt`, `JT/sketch/v2/ToolsEndToEndTest.kt` (new, `// jvmTest-reason: desktop-only`), `JT/sketch/v2/ParityV1SuiteTest.kt` (Task 6.2.4b), `AT/sketch/v2/UxAuthoringAcceptanceTest.kt` (new; UX-01, UX-02, UX-03, UX-04), `BT/sketch/v2/HeadlessSliceScenarioTest.kt` (new, W7, Task 6.2.4d), `JT/sketch/v2/RenderedSliceScenarioTest.kt` (new, W9, Task 6.2.4g), `kmp/src/commonTest/resources/sketch/golden/*`, owner-verification.md

#### Story 6.2.5: Migration golden and flag rollout
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Metric 6 compares against the baseline recorded BEFORE the change (Task 0.1.7d), not one taken at Phase 5.
  - *Given* `implementation/regression-baseline.txt` (committed in W1), *When* Task 6.2.5a re-runs `scripts/sketch-baseline/record-sketch-baseline.sh --sha <the baseline-sha on line 1>` (so the baseline is re-derived at the recorded commit, not at HEAD) and diffs the result against the committed file, and checks `git diff --quiet <sha> HEAD -- <each listed test file>`, *Then* every test class and golden fixture listed in the baseline still exists with an identical SHA-256 for fixtures, every listed test passes unmodified, the current count is at least the baseline count, and any removed or edited baseline entry fails the check unless a change record in the file names it.
- Golden migration over every shipped v1 fixture; `MigrationRunnerSchemaSyncTest` green; flag-off v2 sheet read-only.
  - *Given* a converted sheet and flag off, *When* opened, *Then* it is read-only with the reason text.
- The release path is independent of the gate for the reader-only release (P2; requirements.md "Parked outcomes"). *Given* the edge block, *Then* Task 6.2.5e has no path to 6.2.4e, 6.2.4f, 6.2.5a or any parked node, Task 6.2.5c depends on 6.2.5e (A4 GO: the reader-only release ships before conversion is reachable) and on the gate, and `check-plan.py` fails if either property breaks; *Given* a parked outcome, *Then* `implementation/parked.md` lists each skipped node with its outcome and Task 6.2.5b names them.

#### Story 6.2.6: Backup restore operation
**As a** user who wants v1 content back, **I want** a tested restore of a sheet's v1 backup, **so that** rollback is not a hand-edited file copy (UX 14.6, U4).
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Restore returns the original v1 sheet.
  - *Given* a converted sheet `s1` with `backup/s1.v1.json`, *When* `SketchBackupRestore.restore(slug, s1)` runs (also exposed as a settings-screen action "Restore original (v1) sheet", with a confirmation naming that v2 edits since conversion are lost), *Then* a confirmation dialog (UX-78) first states "Restore the original sketch? Everything you changed in the new editor since the upgrade will be discarded. A copy of the new-editor version is kept in backup/. [Restore original] [Cancel]", Cancel changes nothing, and only on confirmation, in this order: the current v2 file is first copied to `backup/s1.v2-before-restore.json`, `sheets/s1.json` is written from the backup, ONE model write flips `sheetRef.file/schema/fileSha256` back, `sheets/s1.v2.json` is deleted, DB `schema_version = 1`, and the project reads `Present` with the original v1 bytes; a crash after any step leaves a `Present` project (crash-injection seam as in Story 1.4.2); a missing or sha-mismatched backup refuses with `BackupMissing` and changes nothing.
- Restore success feedback (UX-114). *Given* the restore completes, *Then* the `Restored` toast "Restored. Copy kept." with [Copy path] shows, announced politely (its Details: "Restored the original sketch. The new-editor version is kept in backup/.").
**Files**: `RT/convert/SketchBackupRestore.kt`, `UI2/RestoreBackupAction.kt`, `BT/sketch/v2/RestoreFromBackupTest.kt`
##### Task 6.2.6a: Restore operation + crash-injection tests (S)
##### Task 6.2.6b: Settings action + the v2-edits-discarded confirmation (XS)
