#### Story 6.2.7: Shared `SketchErrorState` enum, log mapping and dead-end walk (UX-71, UX-75; consistency C13; needs 3.6.4, 6.2.3)
**As a** maintainer, **I want** one enum of user-visible error states that drives both the UI actions and the log line, **so that** no error state is a dead end and none is silent.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Every error state has exactly one log line (UX-71).
  - *Given* `SketchErrorState` (at least: `LoadCorrupt`, `SheetBusy`, `NewerSchema`, `SaveFailed`, `SaveConflict`, `LowMemory`, `ImportInvalid`, `ImportTooLarge`, `ImportOverCap`, `ImportCancelled`, `ImportCrashed`, `ExportTooLarge`, `ExportEncodeFailed`, `ExportWriteFailed`, `ConversionFailed`, `HasTechnicalData`, `ConstraintConflict`, `ConstraintNoConvergence`, `ConstraintCapReached`, `ElementCapReached`, `LayerLocked`, `CopyRefused`), *When* `ErrorLogCoverageTest` iterates the enum, *Then* each value maps to exactly one `sketch.*` line name (a `when` with no `else`), and triggering each in a fake host emits that line once with ids and counts only.
- No error state is a dead end (UX-75).
  - *Given* the same enum, *When* `DeadEndWalkTest` renders each state in the fake host, *Then* each exposes at least one action or a dismissal, and activating it restores a usable editor (no modal left, tools enabled or the read-only banner present); the manual walk of ux.md sections 1-16 is recorded as a checklist row in `owner-verification.md`.
**Files**: `RT/telemetry/SketchErrorState.kt`, `CT/sketch/v2/ErrorLogCoverageTest.kt` (new; UX-71), `AT/sketch/v2/DeadEndWalkTest.kt` (new; UX-75)
##### Task 6.2.7a: Enum, exhaustive log mapping, UI action table, `ErrorLogCoverageTest` over the closed story list in the dependency table (M); reclassified from S: 22+ states, each with a log mapping and an action row
##### Task 6.2.7b: `DeadEndWalkTest` and the owner-verification walk row (S)

#### Story 6.2.8: Usability think-aloud validation (UX-96; owner wall-clock blocker)
**As a** maintainer, **I want** 3-5 real users to try the riskiest flows aloud, **so that** discoverability and touch problems that tests cannot see are found before release.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- A written protocol and a recorded result.
  - *Given* 3-5 participants (the owner plus 2-4 others the owner already knows who make diagrams, invited unpaid by the owner from a list of names recorded before W1; at least one on a phone with no stylus; with fewer than 2 others the result is "owner-only, UNMEASURED against others", never PASS; D-48), *When* each is asked, without help, to (1) find the hidden tool (for example "draw a diamond" with the tool strip at compact width), (2) enable the new editor and read what it tells them, then answer the written comprehension question of UX-123 ("If you upgrade this sketch and then open this project on another device that has not been updated, what happens when you open this project there? Which sketches does it affect?"; correct = the whole project, all its sketches, won't open; "just this sketch" or "nothing" is wrong), (3) add a Parallel constraint between two lines, (4) draw a box and an arrow with touch only on a phone, (5) with Finger drawing ON pan the view to reach a shape that is off screen, then say what they think happened when a pen was first held to the screen (only if a pen is available), and (6) on a technical sheet with one line drawn, give the line a length of 6 in without being told where dimensions live (first unaided; then, if they missed it, after being shown the smart-dimension chip of Story 4.2.7, recording which path they found and how many actions it took), *Then* at least one session (task 4 or 6) runs in a 320 x 480 dp window at font scale 2.0 (the smallest guaranteed window) and notes whether the chrome left a usable drawing area, and the protocol records per task: completed unaided yes/no, time, wrong turns and quotes; the bar is that at least ceil(0.8 n) of n participants (3 of 3, 4 of 4, 4 of 5) complete tasks 1, 3, 4 and 5 unaided and answer the task-2 comprehension question correctly and at least ceil(0.6 n) (2 of 3, 3 of 4, 3 of 5) complete task 6 unaided, and every unmet task becomes a defect against the owning story or a documented owner decision; the result is filed under `project_plans/excalidraw-sketch-engine/implementation/usability-results.md`.
- When the constraint UI is parked (A6 DEFER, import/export first, v1 editor extended only; requirements.md "Parked outcomes") the protocol drops tasks 3 (Parallel constraint) and 6 (a length of 6 in), which need Stories 4.2.3 and 4.2.7b-d; the bar is computed over tasks 1, 2, 4 and 5 and the result is labelled "constraint tasks not run".
- Nag observation for the auto-opening consent sheet (iteration 14, UX): the "Upgrade this sketch?" sheet opens by itself at most once per sketch project per day (Task 1.4.6i), so the protocol records for every participant who meets it (font scale 1.15 or more at 320 dp, or 1.52 or more at 360 dp, with mode `On`) whether it read as a nag or interrupted work, with a quote; two or more such reports make the daily cadence a defect against Task 1.4.6i and the owner chooses a weekly cadence or a manual-only sheet, recorded in the ADR index.
- Decision rule for assumption A5 and the findings (recorded in `usability-results.md`). *If* fewer than the bar can pan in task 5, the one-finger-pan alternatives are promoted first (the edge strip is labelled "Pan" and always visible while Finger drawing is ON, the Hand tool is pinned in the compact dock) with no default change, and the session is re-run once with new participants (with no new participant available, the owner-only case, there is no re-run: the result is recorded "Task 5 below the bar, not re-run, UNMEASURED against others", the promoted alternatives still ship and the default does not flip on an unmeasured miss unless the owner records it); *if* the re-run still misses, the Finger drawing default flips to OFF everywhere and the finger-only-phone hint offers [Turn on Finger drawing]. *If* two or more participants report that a single-letter shortcut did something unexpected, `SingleKeyShortcuts` defaults to OFF on desktop as well and the hint chip of Story 3.6.1 appears there. *If* task 2 misses the bar (UX-123), the defect goes to the `SketchConsentCopy` wording or placement (Story 1.4.6) and the session is re-run once; a second miss is an owner decision recorded in the ADR index (for example a typed confirmation or an always-shown modal). *If* tasks 1, 3 or 4 miss the bar, the defect goes to Story 3.6.8, 4.2.3 or 3.1.2 and the session is re-run once; a second miss is an owner decision recorded in the ADR index, not an automatic scope change.
**Files**: `project_plans/excalidraw-sketch-engine/implementation/usability-protocol.md` (new), `project_plans/excalidraw-sketch-engine/implementation/usability-results.md` (written by the owner run)
##### Task 6.2.8a: Write the protocol (six tasks, incl. the dimensioning task), the participant script, the decision rule and the result template; schedule the owner session (XS); the sessions themselves are an owner wall-clock blocker, OD-2 class

#### Story 6.2.9: Abort and pivot checkpoints (requirements.md "Abort and pivot criteria"; each task records one decision after its owning story)
**As a** maintainer, **I want** each abort/pivot criterion evaluated at a named point and its outcome recorded, **so that** a failing risk narrows a claim on purpose instead of being discovered late.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- Each checkpoint has a trigger, a measured input and a recorded outcome.
  - *Given* the owning story is green, *When* its checkpoint task runs, *Then* it evaluates the criterion of requirements.md against the measured figure (Task 0.1.7b references, Spike 0.1.3 results, `BindingInvariantPropertyTest`, the six fixtures), writes proceed / narrow / defer into the named ADR and `implementation/pivots.md`, and applies the per-target switch or label the plan already provides (`SketchyAvailability`, lower `softElementThreshold`, binding scope, "beta" label); no checkpoint removes a feature from scope.
**Files**: `project_plans/excalidraw-sketch-engine/implementation/pivots.md`, `ADR-004` (Sketchy availability), `CT/sketch/v2/SketchyAvailabilityTest.kt`
##### Task 6.2.9a: Sketchy on wasmJs/iOS checkpoint after Story 2.2.3 (XS)
##### Task 6.2.9b: Binding and elbow routing checkpoint after Story 3.3.2 (XS)
##### Task 6.2.9c: 500-element Canvas performance checkpoint on Pixel_Fold and wasmJs after Story 2.1.3, using the Task 0.1.7b references (XS)
##### Task 6.2.9d: `.excalidraw` fidelity checkpoint after Story 5.1.3 (XS)

## Effort estimate

Basis: bands and weights INFERRED; recalibrate after the first implementer run. Raw tokens are per-story sums of task cost classes (XS 20k, S 55k, M 130k; no L). Assumed output share 12% for implementation, so CU = tokens x (1 + 4 x 0.12) = 1.48 x tokens (cache reads not discounted, so conservative). Verification multiplier x1.75 (spec + quality review per story, 6-verify layers, CI reruns).

| Epic | Stories | Tasks | Cost class mix | Est. raw tokens | Out share | Est. CU | Wall-clock blocker |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1 Spikes and baselines | 8 | 28 | 3M 15S 10XS | 1415k | 12% | 2.09M | wasm Karma/CI queue; iOS compile; Node+network for goldens; OD-3 before 0.1.2a; owner baselines (0.1.7a, 0.1.7c), real excalidraw.com exports (0.1.5d), A6 check (0.1.2d), edge-gesture probe on the AVD and a touch laptop (0.1.4d), Firefox and Safari OPFS runs (0.1.8b) |
| 1.1 Sidecar codec + bundle dispatch | 5 | 14 | 7M 5S 2XS | 1225k | 12% | 1.81M | - |
| 1.2 Scene + bridge | 4 | 9 | 3M 5S 1XS | 685k | 12% | 1.01M | - |
| 1.3 Commands/index | 3 | 7 | 5M 2S | 760k | 12% | 1.12M | - |
| 1.4 Persistence + fusion wiring + upgrade flow | 7 | 61 | 14M 45S 2XS | 4335k | 12% | 6.42M | OD-1 |

- Counts are mechanically recomputed by `check-plan.py` (validation.md) from the plan's `### Epic` / `#### Story` / `##### Task` headings and each task's `(XS|S|M)` tag, and from the glossary and `ux.md` criteria lists: **19 epics, 94 stories, 313 tasks (91 M / 176 S / 46 XS)**, 70 glossary terms, 146 UX criteria. History: 215 tasks before repair iteration 2; 239 tasks (77 M / 127 S / 35 XS) after it; iteration 3 added 13 tasks (0.1.4d, 0.1.5d, the split of the former Task 1.1.3b into 1.1.3b/c/d/e which adds two net tasks and one M, 3.1.2g, 3.5.4c, 3.6.2c, 3.6.6d, 3.6.7c, 4.2.7c, 4.2.7d, 6.2.4g, 6.2.5d) and reclassified Task 6.2.3b from XS to S (quality signals); epic rows moved accordingly. Iteration 4 added 5 tasks (0.1.7d, 3.1.4a, 3.1.4b, 3.6.3d, 5.1.1e) and one story (3.1.4), reclassified Task 3.1.2g from S to M, and added 7 UX criteria (UX-119, UX-120, UX-121, UX-122, UX-123, UX-124, UX-125). Iteration 5 added 2 tasks (3.1.2h split from 3.1.2g, 5.1.1f split from 5.1.1e), no story and no UX criterion; every other fix edited text in place. Iteration 6 added 3 tasks (3.1.4c action chip row and toast lane, 3.2.2d handle tiers, 5.1.1g library mode of the scanner) and 3 UX criteria (UX-126, UX-127, UX-128), no story; every other fix edited or deleted text. Iteration 7 added 1 task (3.1.4d top bar tiers and the compact-height Selection menu), no story and no UX criterion, moved the pure `BannerCandidate` into Task 3.6.4a (no new task, no new edge except 3.1.4 after 3.6.3d) and added 6 owner defaults (D-48 to D-53); every other fix edited or deleted text. Iteration 8 added 1 task (3.6.4c, the chrome-table fixture generated from ux.md), reclassified Task 6.2.5d from XS to S, added no story and no UX criterion and added 5 owner defaults (D-54 to D-58); every other fix edited or deleted text. Iteration 9 added 8 tasks (1.4.3d, 1.4.3e, 1.4.6h, 1.4.6i, 3.6.4d, 3.6.4e, 3.6.8e, 6.2.5e; Task 3.6.4c was split into 3.6.4c, 3.6.4d and 3.6.4e, so two of the eight come from that split), added no story and no UX criterion, 2 glossary terms (`V2Mode`, `ConversionOffered`) and 7 owner defaults (D-59 to D-65), and added the edges 0.1.7c to the roots of the held set, 3.6.4d, 3.6.4e and 6.2.5e; every other fix edited or deleted text. Iteration 10 added 2 tasks (3.6.4f and 3.1.4e, the Bazel `srcs` and `AllBusinessTests` registration of the two commonTest files that consume the chrome fixture, both XS), no story, no UX criterion and no glossary term, 8 owner defaults (D-66 to D-73), the edges 3.6.4f after 3.6.4a and 3.1.4e after 3.1.4 and 3.6.4f, and `check-plan.py` extensions (pre-check stop set, prose edge table, ADR wording, nodes per wave); every other fix edited or deleted text. Iteration 11 added 1 task (1.1.5b, the registration of the two commonTest classes of the `ConversionReadyConsistencyTest` list, XS), no story, no UX criterion and no glossary term, 9 owner defaults (D-74 to D-82), and `check-plan.py` extensions (A6 scope, reachable demand, ConversionReady registration, the `NewSketchProject` sentence); the one-line classic notice is retired; every other fix edited or deleted text. Iteration 13 added 2 tasks (1.4.3g, the bounded prefix read `FileSystem.readFilePrefix`, M; 6.2.4h, `EscOrderIntegrationTest`, S) and reclassified Task 1.4.6e from XS to S (the `ClassicNoticeLedger`), one toast row (`ClassicEditorFixed`), no story, no UX criterion and no glossary term, 12 owner defaults (D-92 to D-103) and `check-plan.py` extensions that parse values (KEPT derived from the plan-hold closure, probe bound arithmetic and file key against the code, Esc order rows, project kinds, ClassicEditor scope, chars-per-line property); the previous figures were 275 tasks (82 M / 150 S / 43 XS), 19.77M raw, 29.26M CU. Iteration 14 added 1 task (1.4.3h, `readModel` and `isAvailable` on `ProjectSidecarStore`, S), no story and no glossary term, 5 UX criteria (UX-129, UX-130, UX-131, UX-132 and UX-133), 8 owner defaults (D-104 to D-111), moved the A1 pre-check (Task 0.1.7c) into the OWNER0 session (wave 0, edge `@OWNER0`) with an edge from it into every W1 node, removed the edge 1.4.3g to 0.1.1a (Task 1.4.3g now depends on the OWNER0 session only, so its wave is W1), added the plan-hold `MERGES` line, and extended `check-plan.py` (every node has a path from the gate, the quoted stop costs, the header-first offsets, the platform capability, the fourth gate outcome, the Esc rows quoted by D-85 and D-100); the figures before it were 277 tasks (83 M / 152 S / 42 XS), 19.99M raw, 29.59M CU. Iteration 15 added 10 tasks (0.1.8a, 0.1.8b, 1.1.5c, 1.4.3i, 1.4.3j, 1.4.6j, 1.4.7a, 1.4.7b, 1.4.7c, 1.4.7d: 2 M, 7 S, 1 XS) and 2 stories (0.1.8 the OPFS spike, 1.4.7 Web storage), 3 glossary terms (`V2StorageAvailable`, `OpfsStorageVerdict`, `SolverTier`), 5 UX criteria (UX-134, UX-135, UX-136, UX-137 and UX-138) and 7 owner defaults (D-112 to D-118), recorded D-104 as the owner's decision (Option W, not a default), split Task 1.4.3d into 1.4.3d, 1.4.3i and 1.4.3j, moved `classifyHeader` into Task 1.1.5c, and extended `check-plan.py` (multipliers, ADR offsets, code citations in every document, waves stated in task headings, D-id lists, the platform axis, the per-target solver tier, Option W, the no-editor screen); the figures before it were 278 tasks (83 M / 153 S / 42 XS), 20.05M raw, 29.67M CU. Iteration 12 added no task and no story and 9 owner defaults (D-83 to D-91). Iteration 17 added 7 tasks (1.4.3k to 1.4.3p and 1.4.7m: 5 S, 1 XS in Story 1.4.3 and 1 M in Story 1.4.7), reclassified Task 1.4.7j from S to M, added no story and no glossary term, 5 UX criteria (UX-144, UX-145, UX-146, UX-147 and UX-148) and 9 owner defaults (D-129 to D-137), moved the shared storage types and ports out of Story 1.4.7 into Story 1.4.3 (set `OPFSSHARED`), added the plan-wiring table, the `SceneLimits` field `maxPreloadBytes` (32 fields) and the Option W keep or drop rule; the figures before it were 296 tasks (87 M / 166 S / 43 XS), 21.30M raw, 31.52M CU. - **Critical path (agent waves)**, recomputed by script from the machine-readable edge list in "Dependency Visualization" (rule: wave = 1 + the maximum wave of a node's dependencies; `OWNER0` is the batched owner session). **The count is 14 waves, W1 to W14**, unchanged by iteration 3 although several nodes moved (3.4.2 to W6, 3.5.1 to W7 because it extends the select tool, 5.1.2 to W7, 3.6.4a to W3, 3.1.2d/e/g to W8, 3.6.1c stays W2). Wave widths are computed in the "Nodes per wave" line of the critical-path notes below, not stated here; shared-file serialisation points still apply (`V2/commands/*` and `SketchSceneV2` for Phase 3 stories; `SteleDatabase.sq`/`MigrationRunner.kt` only in 1.4.1; `ProjectBundle`/`ProjectModelJson`/`SidecarReconciler`/`ProjectSidecarStore` only in 0.1.1b, 0.1.1c, 1.1.3, 1.1.4 and 1.4.2).   - **Chains that set the wave count (each arrow is an edge of the block above)**: 0.1.3 > 1.2.4a > 1.2.1 > 1.2.3 > 1.1.5 > 1.4.5 > 1.4.2 > 1.4.3 > 6.2.4g (rendered slice) > 6.2.4e (A1 gate) > 4.2.3 > 4.2.6b > 6.2.4f > 6.2.5b (14); and 0.1.3 > 1.2.4a > 1.2.1 > 1.3.1 > 1.3.3 > 3.5.5 > 3.2.1 > 3.3.1 > 3.3.2 > 3.3.3 > 6.2.4a > 6.2.4b > 6.2.5a > 6.2.5b (14); 1.4.6 ends in W9 (a sibling of the rendered slice); 6.2.8 is W13 because its dimensioning task needs 4.2.7d.
  - **Early slices**: the headless slice (`HeadlessSliceScenarioTest`, Task 6.2.4d, W7: commands + `SheetSaver` + `SheetCodecV2` + reload; no flag, router or painter) and the rendered slice (`RenderedSliceScenarioTest`, Task 6.2.4g, W9) feed the A1 gate, Task 6.2.4e (owner, W10).
  - **Serial depth inside W3 (Story 1.1.3)**: the wave count counts nodes, not tasks. Story 1.1.3 is one node (W3) but its Tasks 1.1.3a to 1.1.3e touch the shared files `ProjectBundle`, `ProjectModelJson`, `SidecarReconciler`, `ProjectSidecarStore` and both `replaceProjectContents` implementations, so they run SERIALLY: a, then b, c, d and e in that order (serial depth 5 inside one wave). W3's wall-clock is therefore set by this five-step chain, not by the 3-8 agent width; no other W3 node touches those files. Dependent nodes (1.1.4, 1.4.2, 1.4.5) cannot start before the whole story is green.
  - **Intra-wave ordering** (only where one node's output is another's input within the same wave): none; the previous intra-wave exceptions (Task 3.6.1c before 3.6.1a/b, 4.2.2 and 4.2.3 after 4.2.1d) are now strict edges across waves.
  - **Sunk cost before the A1 gate**: W1-W9 hold 82.7% of the raw estimate (27.46M CU, 48.06M CU with the x1.75 verification multiplier) and W1-W10 hold 85.7% (28.45M CU); the gate decides the remaining stories, the flag release and the direction, not the build (see requirements.md "Sunk cost before the A1 gate").
  - **Nodes per wave.** About 14 serial waves; the widths are computed by `check-plan.py` from the edge block (a node is one story or task, which one agent can take; Story 1.1.3 is one node but serial inside, see above): W1 to W14 = 13, 14, 9, 11, 9, 21, 16, 17, 11, 8, 10, 12, 7, 3 (script-checked: the widest wave is W6 with 21 nodes, the narrowest W14 with 3).
- **Overrun checkpoint**: if cumulative spend on completed stories exceeds the table value by more than 25% (about 1.25x), pause and report the delta and its cause; informational, not an invitation to cut.
- **Wall-clock blockers** (with owners): OD-1..OD-8 decisions plus the owner-confirmable defaults D-1..D-157 (among them the Option W keep or drop answer of D-133, read from the platform field of the pre-check list) and the pre-W1 items (Tyler; answered in ONE batched session before W1); the usability think-aloud sessions of Story 6.2.8 (3-5 participants, owner schedules); the owner's baselines (Tasks 0.1.7a, 0.1.7c), the real excalidraw.com exports (Task 0.1.5d), the Firefox and Safari OPFS runs (Task 0.1.8b), the web browser pass (Task 1.4.7d), the A1 go/no-go gate diagram on the rendered slice (Task 6.2.4e, wave W10), the A6 Job 2 demand check (Task 0.1.2d) and the 8-week adoption read (metric 8); Pixel_Fold AVD (frame-time advisory, edge-gesture probe and device passes) and device passes for stylus pressure/hover/palm rejection, IME and TalkBack (Tyler; CI cannot cover device input); wasmJs/Karma CI queue; iOS device verification deferred (compile-only in v1); review latency on standing sketch branches (PRs #614, #620, #650, #663, #671 must be merged or rebased onto, owner: Tyler) and the open BUG-001/002/003/004 (see External prerequisites); font license sign-off (OD-4).

## Requirement traceability (success metrics)
1 tool parity (14 tools frozen in requirements metric 1): E3.1-E3.5, each tool has a scene-engine test (`ToolInventoryCompletenessTest`); the pinned Excalidraw feature baseline (41 counted items) is `FeatureBaselineCompletenessTest` (Task 3.5.4c). 2 diagram authoring: Story 6.2.4 scenario. 3 constraints: E4.1/E4.2, property tests; geometry elements per OD-8 (1.2.1, 3.2.4, 4.2.1b); single-element constraints via the 1+ selection flyout (4.2.3b). 4 cross-platform: commonTest goldens (2.1.1, 2.2.1) + pixel tolerance (2.1.3c, 2.2.3a). 5 layers UX: 3.5.1, 3.5.2 (numeric bar and named scenario "floor plan layers" in requirements.md metric 5, asserted by `UxOrganisationAcceptanceTest`). 6 no regression/no data loss: 1.1.1, 1.1.3 (v2 passthrough, writer-race and metadata round-trip goldens), 1.1.4, 1.1.5, 1.4.1d (schema_version preserved by every sheet-row write), 1.4.2 (crash-injection + goldens), 1.4.4c (external-overwrite conflict copy), 1.4.5, 1.4.6, 6.2.5, 6.2.6, `MigrationRunnerSchemaSyncTest`. 2 baselines: Task 0.1.7a (owner excalidraw.com time) and 0.1.7b (frame reference). 8 adoption (advisory): Task 0.1.7c pre-check, Task 6.2.3b counter, the A1 go/no-go gate Task 6.2.4e at the rendered slice, Story 6.2.8 usability session; Job 2 demand A6 Task 0.1.2d; abort/pivot checkpoints Story 6.2.9. 7 interop: E5.1 round-trip property + `export(import(fixture))` (5.1.3b) over the six hand-authored fixtures plus at least 5 owner-supplied real exports or the labelled fallback (Task 0.1.5d), E5.2 incl. per-kind PNG checks and SVG escaping.
